### [1] [Symmetric spaces and skew multiplicity free representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38507)
*Kieran Calvert,Kyo Nishiyama,Pavle Pandžić*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文给出了判别实约化李群的抛物子群具有交换幂零根的一系列等价条件，并由此推导紧致对称空间的de Rham上同调公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛物子群的幂零根是否交换是一个结构性关键问题，它与skew multiplicity free、multiplicity free作用等表示论性质密切相关；同时希望借此为紧致对称空间G/P的上同调提供显式公式。

Method: 对非紧连通实约化李群G及其抛物子群P，建立P具有交换幂零根的等价条件，并借助Clifford代数与三次Dirac算子理论进行推导。

Result: 得到一组关于P具有交换幂零根的等价条件，并给出紧致对称空间G/P的de Rham上同调的公式。

Conclusion: 交换幂零根条件与多重自由作用等表示论性质等价，且通过Clifford代数与三次Dirac算子可有效计算G/P的de Rham上同调。

Abstract: Let $ G $ be a non-compact connected reductive real Lie group and $ P $ its parabolic subgroup. We give a list of equivalent conditions for $ P $ to have an abelian unipotent radical. These conditions are related to skew multiplicity free and multiplicity free actions, and using the theory of Clifford algebra and the cubic Dirac operator we deduce formulas for the de Rham cohomology of compact symmetric spaces $ G/P $.

</details>


### [2] [Normalization graphs and Dold-Kan equivalences for generalized Reedy categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38992)
*Liping Li*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入标准模投射性的组合判定准则，用有限二分图的归一化赋权刻画归一化幂等元与标准模的投射分裂，并由此导出多类Dold–Kan型等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Reedy范畴上标准模的投射性及Dold–Kan型等价通常需要较繁重的范畴论构造，缺乏统一、可计算且组合化的判定工具。作者希望用图论与线性代数提供一套机械化框架。

Method: 构造有限二分图，其归一化赋权（normalized weightings）确定归一化幂等元，从而给出标准模的投射分裂；结合此前关于标准模之间Hom空间的工作，形成组合机器。

Result: 恢复了若干经典归一化构造与Dold–Kan型等价；对有限域上的有限维仿射、半线性、半仿射空间范畴得到新的Dold–Kan型等价；并给出相应无限变换幺半群的一致连续表示的显式描述。

Conclusion: 结果为广义Reedy范畴的Dold–Kan型等价提供了基于图论与线性代数的组合化统一框架。

Abstract: We introduce a combinatorial criterion for the projectivity of standard modules over generalized Reedy categories, formulated in terms of a finite bipartite graph whose normalized weightings determine normalization idempotents and hence projective splittings of standard modules. We apply this framework to recover several classical normalization constructions and Dold--Kan--type equivalences, and to obtain new Dold--Kan--type equivalences for categories of finite-dimensional affine, semilinear, and semiaffine spaces over a finite field. These equivalences also yield explicit descriptions of uniformly continuous representations of the corresponding infinite transformation monoids. Together with our previous work on Hom-spaces between standard modules, the results provide a combinatorial machinery, based mainly on graph theory and linear algebra, for establishing Dold--Kan--type equivalences for generalized Reedy categories.

</details>


### [3] [Higher Cotorsion Pairs on Image-Nilpotent \(η\)-Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39003)
*Dajun Liu,Hanpeng Gao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为图像幂零的 η-扩张建立了右 n-余挠对的提升定理与完备性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η-扩张（如分裂幂零环扩张、逗号范畴、形式三角矩阵环、Morita context 环及经典平凡扩张）在代数中广泛出现，但缺少统一处理其上余挠对（cotorsion pair）提升与完备性的框架。

Method: 在具有足够投射与内射对象的阿贝尔范畴 B 上，考虑由右正合自函子 F 诱导的 η-扩张 A=B⋉_η F；引入 η-扩张的一致图像幂零性，并用结合自然变换 η:F^2→F 的幂零性加以刻画；借助 η-Loewy 滤过证明提升定理与完备性定理。

Result: 刻画了图像幂零性等价于 η 的幂零性；证明了图像幂零 η-扩张上右 n-余挠对的提升定理及相应的完备性定理。

Conclusion: 结果统一并推广了分裂幂零环扩张、逗号范畴、形式三角矩阵环、Morita context 环和经典平凡扩张中的相关构造。

Abstract: Let \(\cB\) be an abelian category with enough projective and injective objects, and let $
\cA=\cB\ltimes_η\sF $
be an \(η\)-extension induced by a right exact endofunctor \(\sF\). We introduce uniform image-nilpotence for \(η\)-extensions and characterize it in terms of the nilpotence of the associative natural transformation \(η:\sF^2\to\sF\). Using the associated \(η\)-Loewy filtration, we prove a lifting theorem for right \(n\)-cotorsion pairs over image-nilpotent \(η\)-extensions and establish a corresponding completeness theorem. As applications, our results recover and unify related constructions for split nilpotent ring extensions, comma categories, formal triangular matrix rings, Morita context rings and classical trivial extensions.

</details>


### [4] [Singularities of orbit closures in loop spaces of symmetric varieties II: twisted cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39079)
*Tsao-Hsien Chen,Lingfei Yi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究环路对称簇中轨道闭包的奇点，将此前工作推广到扭曲情形，证明IC复形逐点纯且满足奇偶消没性质，并应用于扭曲仿射Lusztig–Vogan模及实群Langlands对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将环路对称簇轨道闭包奇点的研究从非扭曲情形推广至扭曲情形，并为扭曲仿射Lusztig–Vogan模理论及实群Langlands对偶提供几何基础。

Method: 研究环路对称簇（loop symmetric varieties）中轨道闭包的奇点；证明其IC复形的逐点纯性与奇偶消没性质；构造环路对称空间中的锥形辛横截切片。

Result: 证明了轨道闭包IC复形的逐点纯性与奇偶消没性质；建立了扭曲仿射Lusztig–Vogan模的基础性结果，包括扭曲仿射Kazhdan–Lusztig–Vogan多项式的正性性质；构造了环路对称空间中的锥形辛横截切片。

Conclusion: 该工作将环路对称簇奇点理论推广到扭曲情形，并为扭曲仿射Lusztig–Vogan模与实群Langlands对偶提供了几何与表示论应用。

Abstract: We study the singularities of orbit closures in loop symmetric varieties, extending our previous work https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.20006 to the twisted setting. We prove that the IC complexes of orbit closures are pointwise pure and satisfy a parity-vanishing property. We apply these geometric results to the study of twisted affine Lusztig--Vogan modules, establishing foundational results, including positivity properties of the twisted affine Kazhdan--Lusztig--Vogan polynomials. Along the way, we construct conical symplectic transversal slices in loop symmetric spaces. We provide applications to Langlands duality for real groups.

</details>


### [5] [Unequal-parameter Kostka--Foulkes polynomials of type $C_n$ at fundamental weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39095)
*Murilo Corato-Zanarella*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了不等参数 $C_n$ 型 Kostka--Foulkes 多项式在基本权处的显式公式，并将其应用于拟分裂非分裂特殊正交群某个特殊极大紧子群的 Hecke 代数，得到显式 Lusztig--Kato 公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $C_n$ 型不等参数 Kostka--Foulkes 多项式在基本权处缺乏显式表达，而此类公式是推导相应 Hecke 代数显式基与结构常数的关键；同时，$p$ 进域上拟分裂非分裂特殊正交群的特殊极大紧子群对应的 Hecke 代数需要显式的 Lusztig--Kato 公式以刻画其结构。

Method: 在不等参数情形下，对 $C_n$ 型 Kostka--Foulkes 多项式在基本权处进行组合/表示论计算，建立其显式公式；随后通过该显式公式把结果转换到 Hecke 代数框架，推导对应的 Lusztig--Kato 公式。

Result: 得到了不等参数 $C_n$ 型 Kostka--Foulkes 多项式在基本权处的显式公式；并由此得到了拟分裂非分裂特殊正交群的特殊（非超特殊）极大紧子群 Hecke 代数的显式 Lusztig--Kato 公式。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了基本权处不等参数 $C_n$ 型 Kostka--Foulkes 多项式的显式描述，并展示了其在 $p$ 进群 Hecke 代数理论中的直接应用，为相关 Hecke 代数提供了显式的 Lusztig--Kato 公式。

Abstract: We establish an explicit formula for the Kostka--Foulkes polynomial of type $C_n$ with unequal parameters at fundamental weights. As an application, we deduce an explicit Lusztig--Kato formula for the Hecke algebra of the special (but not hyperspecial) maximal compact subgroup of a quasi-split but non-split special orthogonal group over a $p$-adic field.

</details>


### [6] [Duality functors for Coulomb branches I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39122)
*Sabin Cautis,Eric Vasserot*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为有限型箭图构造了从仿射箭图Hecke代数有限维模范畴到Koszul-反常相干层Coulomb范畴的精确函子，该函子将单模映为单模或零，且在单模上(几乎)本质满，由此说明单Koszul-反常层给出了对偶典范基的范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限型箭图可关联两类阿贝尔范畴：仿射箭图Hecke代数的有限维模范畴，以及Koszul-反常相干层的Coulomb范畴。二者分别具有表示论与几何来源，但此前缺乏明确的联系；本文旨在建立两者之间的精确对应，从而为对偶典范基提供范畴化解释。

Method: 定义从仿射箭图Hecke代数有限维模范畴到Koszul-反常相干层Coulomb范畴的一个精确函子，并分析其在单模上的行为（映为单模或零），以及在整个单模集合上的本质满性。

Result: 所构造的精确函子将单模映为单模或零，并在单模上(几乎)本质满；因此单Koszul-反常层构成对偶典范基的范畴化。

Conclusion: 该函子建立了Hecke代数模范畴与Coulomb范畴之间的精确联系，表明单Koszul-反常层可范畴化对偶典范基。

Abstract: To a quiver of finite type one can associate two abelian categories: the category of finite dimensional modules over the corresponding affine quiver Hecke algebra and the Coulomb category of Koszul-perverse coherent sheaves. In this paper we define an exact functor from the former to the latter. This functor sends a simple to a simple or zero and is (almost) essentially surjective on simples. This implies that simple Koszul-perverse sheaves categorify the dual canonical basis.

</details>


### [7] [Tilting mutation of gentle algebras and its combinatorial description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39536)
*Difan Deng,Shengfei Geng,Pin Liu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用广义BB-倾斜模研究gentle代数的倾斜突变，给出突变存在的充要条件，并在存在时显式构造突变后倾斜模的自同态代数的Gabriel箭图与关系，从而得到gentle对上的组合突变，保证新代数仍是gentle且与原代数导出等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜突变是构造导出等价代数的自然方法，通过替换倾斜对象的不可分解直和项实现。作者希望针对gentle代数这一重要代数类，给出倾斜突变存在性的可计算判据，并精确描述突变后倾斜模块的结构及其自同态代数，从而在组合层面刻画gentle代数的倾斜突变。

Method: 对gentle代数 A=kQ/<I> 及顶点 k∈Q_0，作者用与k关联的箭头和关系给出 P(k) 的最小左逼近产生倾斜突变的充要条件，该判据对含环与不含环顶点统一适用。突变存在时，确定突变倾斜模各不可分解直和项之间的不可约态射，并据此显式构造其自同态代数的Gabriel箭图与定义关系，进而得到gentle对 (Q,I) 到 (Q',I') 的组合突变，使 μ_k^+(A)≅kQ'/<I'>。最后对偶地表述余倾斜突变并通过相反gentle对给出组合描述。

Result: 给出了倾斜突变存在的充要条件（用入射箭头的箭和关系表达）；在突变存在时确定了不可分解直和项间的不可约态射，并显式构造了自同态代数的Gabriel箭图和定义关系；得到组合突变 (Q,I)↦(Q',I')，满足 μ_k^+(A)≅kQ'/<I'>，从而突变后代数仍为gentle且与A导出等价；并给出对偶余倾斜突变的组合描述。

Conclusion: gentle代数的倾斜突变可由gentle对的组合突变完全刻画，突变后代数保持gentle性并导出等价于原代数，对偶情形对应相反gentle对上的余倾斜突变。

Abstract: Tilting mutation provides a natural way to construct derived-equivalent algebras by replacing an indecomposable summand of a tilting object. In this paper, we study tilting mutation of gentle algebras via generalized $\mathrm{BB}$-tilting modules. For a gentle algebra $A=\mathbb{k} Q/\langle I\rangle$ and a vertex $k\in Q_0$, we first give a necessary and sufficient condition, expressed in terms of the arrows and relations incident with $k$, for the corresponding minimal left approximation of $P(k)$ to yield a tilting mutation. This criterion applies uniformly to vertices with or without loops. When the mutation exists, we determine the irreducible morphisms between the indecomposable summands of the mutated tilting module and use them to construct explicitly the Gabriel quiver and defining relations of its endomorphism algebra. In particular, we obtain a combinatorial mutation $(Q,I)\mapsto(Q',I')$ of gentle pairs such that $μ_k^+(A)\cong \mathbb{k} Q'/\langle I'\rangle,$ so the resulting algebra is again gentle and derived equivalent to $A$. Finally, we formulate the dual cotilting mutation and its combinatorial description via opposite gentle pairs.

</details>


### [8] [On Irreducibility and Multiplicity One Property for Non-Archimedean Type-II Theta Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39683)
*Kaidi Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了非阿基米德II型Howe对应的重数一精细化，并据此结合Godement-Jacquet zeta积分计算了Arthur型表示的大theta提升，进而提出关于大theta提升不可约性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Howe对应中big theta lift与small theta lift的关系（特别是Jordan-Hölder重数）尚需精细刻画，且Arthur型表示的大theta提升结构缺乏明确计算，作者希望在这些方面给出精确结果并引出进一步猜想。

Method: 建立非阿基米德II型Howe对应的重数一精细化：证明当small theta lift非零时，其在big theta lift中以Jordan-Hölder重数一出现；并将该结果与Godement-Jacquet zeta积分结合，用于计算Arthur型表示的大theta提升。

Result: 得到small theta lift在big theta lift中Jordan-Hölder重数为一的精细化结论，并完成对Arthur型表示大theta提升的计算。

Conclusion: 基于上述计算，提出了关于big theta lift不可约性的猜想。

Abstract: We establish a multiplicity-one refinement of the non-archimedean type-II Howe correspondence: whenever the small theta lift is non-zero, it occurs in the big theta lift with Jordan-Hölder multiplicity one. Furthermore, we apply this result alongside the Godement-Jacquet zeta integral to compute big theta lift for Arthur-type representations. Based on these computations, we also propose a conjecture regarding the irreducibility of the big theta lift.

</details>


### [9] [Unipotent representations and the categorical trace of Frobenius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39721)
*Wyatt Reeves*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出分裂有限李型群幂幺表示分类的一个新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分裂有限李型群的幂幺表示分类提供新的证明思路，避免原有方法的复杂性。

Method: 构造从Hecke代数到渐近代数的Lusztig映射的(∞,2)-范畴化，并利用权理论证明其在取Frobenius迹后诱导出同构。

Result: Lusztig映射在取Frobenius迹后诱导出同构。

Conclusion: 借助(∞,2)-范畴化与权理论，得到分裂有限李型群幂幺表示分类的新证明。

Abstract: We give a new proof of the classification of unipotent representations for split finite groups of Lie type. To do this, we first construct an (infinity, 2)-categorification of Lusztig's map from the Hecke algebra to the asymptotic algebra. Then we use the theory of weights to show that it induces an isomorphism after applying trace of Frobenius.

</details>


### [10] [Tensor Product Does Not Behave Additively on Delooping Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39745)
*Ryan Lam*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文指出关于"delooping level"的一个常见误解:对有限维代数 $A$、$B$,一般并不成立 $\operatorname{dell}(A\otimes_{\mathbf{k}}B)=\operatorname{dell}(A)+\operatorname{dell}(B)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维代数表示论中,Finitistic Dimension Conjecture 是最重要的同调猜想之一。Gélinas 近期引入"delooping level"作为研究该猜想的工具,随之出现了一些想当然的等式假设,本文旨在澄清其中的错误认知。

Method: 通过构造反例(具体论证细节在正文中给出),对张量积代数的 delooping level 是否满足加性进行检验,给出否定的判定。

Result: 证明对有限维 $\mathbf{k}$-代数 $A$ 与 $B$,一般地 $\operatorname{dell}(A\otimes_{\mathbf{k}}B)\neq \operatorname{dell}(A)+\operatorname{dell}(B)$,即该等式不普遍成立。

Conclusion: 在借助 delooping level 研究 Finitistic Dimension Conjecture 时,不能默认其关于张量积具有加性;需要逐例验证,避免此类隐性假设导致错误结论。

Abstract: The Finitistic Dimension Conjecture is one of the most important homological conjectures in the representation theory of finite-dimensional algebras. Recently, Gélinas arXiv:2004.04828 proposed the notion of ``delooping level'' to study the Finitistic Dimension Conjecture. This article reports a misconception between the delooping level and the tensor product of algebras. Namely, for finte-dimensional algebras $A$ and $B$, it is not always true that $\operatorname{dell}(A\otimes_{\mathbf{k}}B) = \operatorname{dell}(A)+ \operatorname{dell}(B)$.

</details>


### [11] [Lower affine MV polytopes of rank 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39771)
*Kathlyn Dykes*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对秩2仿射MV多面体的某些子类，证明了其与约化双Bruhat胞腔非负热带点之间的双射，推广了有限型MV多面体的已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当G为复约化代数群时，MV多面体与G的单幂群的非负热带点存在双射。本文希望将这一结论推广到秩2仿射MV多面体的特定子类（下多面体与上多面体）。

Method: 对Kac-Moody群$\widehat{SL_2}$，将仿射MV多面体分解为下、中、上三个子多面体；描述秩2仿射MV多面体的BZ数据，并证明某些广义minor函数满足下多面体的条件。

Result: 证明了对仿射Weyl群元素$w$，最高顶点为$w$的下仿射MV多面体类与标号为$w^{-1}$的约化双Bruhat胞腔的非负热带点构成双射；由于上多面体是下多面体的反射，秩2上仿射MV多面体也有类似结论。

Conclusion: 将有限型MV多面体的已知双射结果推广至秩2仿射MV多面体的下多面体（及通过反射得到的上多面体）子类。

Abstract: When $G$ is a complex reductive algebraic group, MV polytopes are in bijection with the non-negative tropical points of the unipotent group of $G$. In this paper, we prove a similar theorem for certain subclasses of rank 2 affine MV polytopes.
  For the Kac-Moody group $\widehat{SL_2}$, an affine MV polytopes splits into three subpolytopes: a lower, a middle and an upper polytope. The lower polytopes are natural generalizations of finite-type polytopes with highest vertex labelled by an arbitrary Weyl element. We extend the known results from this subclass of finite-type MV polytopes to the case of lower affine MV polytopes of rank 2. We prove that for an element $w$ of the affine Weyl group, the class of lower affine MV polytopes with highest vertex $w$ are in bijection with the non-negative tropical points of the reduced double Bruhat cell labelled by $w^{-1}$. To do this, we describe the BZ data of a rank 2 affine MV polytope and show that certain generalized minor functions satisfy the conditions of a lower polytope. As any upper polytope is a reflection of some lower polytope, a analogous result will hold for the class of upper affine MV polytopes of rank 2.

</details>

### [12] [Spectral numbers in Tate Rabinowitz Floer homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39951)

Abstract: We show that circle valued spectral values in Tate Rabinowitz Floer ho- mology give rise to quantized energy levels, which recover the quantum mechanical spectrum of a system of uncoupled harmonic oscillators.

### [13] [Exact Operator Complexity Measures from Random Matchgate Unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38300)
*Gregory A. L. White,Jens Eisert,Neil Dowling*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究随机Matchgate线路中的Heisenberg绘景复杂度度量，提出Majorana基下的自由费米子幺正Weingarten calculus，给出算符纠缠、算符稳定子熵及高阶OTOC的闭式表达，揭示经典可模拟动力学仍可饱和体积律复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matchgate线路在物理相关场景下经典可模拟，却展现出高magic等复杂量子系统特征，由此引出一个更广泛的问题：典型复杂度度量在平凡动力学下究竟能被饱和到什么程度？本文旨在系统研究随机Matchgate系综中不同Heisenberg绘景复杂度度量的行为，并厘清量子资源与模拟复杂度之间的区别。

Method: 核心技术贡献是提出一种完全在Majorana基下表述、可处理且可推广的自由费米子幺正Weingarten calculus。该方法针对算符矩的计算而设计，可简化为一个依赖于给定算符泛函所规定边界条件的组合学问题。基于此框架，推导典型局域算符纠缠、算符稳定子熵以及高阶out-of-time-ordered correlator（OTOC）的闭式表达。

Result: 对于广延初始Majorana弦，算符纠缠与稳定子熵均遵循体积律，尽管底层线路仍经典可模拟。OTOC同样可指数衰减，表明其晚时饱和值本身并不能见证计算硬度，并构成涌现自由独立性（free independence）的一个经典可处理实例。此外，将上述分离与算符资源理论联系，证明超出高斯基线的算符纠缠为掺杂Matchgate线路中非高斯门数量提供下界。

Conclusion: 本文为计算随机Matchgate上的精确平均提供了一个实用工具箱，同时澄清了量子资源与模拟复杂度之间的区别，以及二者对所使用表示方式的依赖性。

Abstract: Matchgate circuits describe classically tractable dynamics in a physically relevant setting while nonetheless exhibiting features characteristic of complex quantum systems, such as high amounts of magic. This invites the broader question: to what extent are typical complexity measures saturated under trivial dynamics? Here, we address this by studying the behaviour of different Heisenberg-picture complexity measures in random matchgate ensembles. Our main technical contribution is a tractable and generalisible free-fermionic unitary Weingarten calculus formulated entirely in the Majorana basis. This method is tailored to computing moments of operators, and simplifies to a combinatorics problem which depends on the boundary conditions prescribed by a given operator functional. Using this framework, we derive closed-form expressions for the typical local-operator entanglement, operator stabiliser entropy, and higher-order out-of-time-ordered correlators. For extensive initial Majorana strings, both operator entanglement and stabiliser entropy obey volume laws, even though the underlying circuits remain classically simulable. Out-of-time-ordered correlators can likewise become exponentially small, showing that their late-time saturation value alone does not witness computational hardness and constituting a classically tractable example of emergent free independence. Finally, we relate these separations to operator resource theories, showing how operator entanglement beyond the Gaussian baseline lower-bounds the number of non-Gaussian gates in doped matchgate circuits. Our results present a practical toolbox for computing exact averages over random matchgates, while clarifying the distinction between quantum resources versus simulation complexity, and their dependence on the representation being exploited.

</details>

*Matthew Simon Tan,Bartosz Regula,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文改进并扩展了基于Regula与Tomamichel剩余哈希引理的隐私放大界，并将其纳入基于熵不确定关系的QKD安全分析，从而在有限码块条件下提升密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD可提取的密钥量同时取决于实际观测误码率与用于证明安全性的数学界。在有限数据集下，保守的界迫使用户丢弃大量潜在可用密钥，因此需要更紧致的数学估计。

Method: 进一步精炼与扩展由Regula和Tomamichel近期剩余哈希引理（arXiv:2603.04493）给出的隐私放大界，并将其整合进基于熵不确定关系的QKD安全性分析框架。

Result: 在有限码块区间改进了最先进密钥率，在协议不变、实验数据相同的情况下认证出更多密钥，并优于基于熵累积的技术。

Conclusion: 更锐利的数学估计可直接提高量子通信系统的可用输出，说明安全性证明中的数学紧致性对实际性能具有关键意义。

Abstract: The amount of secret key that can be obtained from a quantum key distribution run depends on both the physically observed error rates and the mathematical bounds used to certify security. For finite datasets, conservative bounds force users to discard a substantial fraction of the potentially available key. Here we further refine and extend the privacy amplification bounds achievable through the recent leftover hash lemma of Regula and Tomamichel [arXiv:2603.04493] and incorporate them into the security analysis of quantum key distribution based on entropic uncertainty relations. This improves on state-of-the-art key rates in finite-block regimes, certifying more secret key from the same experimental data without changes to the protocol, and outperforming techniques based on entropy accumulation. The results illustrate how sharper mathematical estimates can directly increase the usable output of a quantum communication system.

</details>


### [15] [When Symmetry Suppresses Magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38319)
*A. de Oliveira Junior,Jake Xuereb,Rafael A. Macêdo,Jonatan Bohr Brask,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明N量子比特X态的魔术鲁棒性（RoM）不超过√3，揭示了特定对称性对非稳定子性的限制，并提出高效下界方法用于大尺度多体态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非稳定子性（nonstabilizerness）是量子优势的关键资源，但混合态下的评估需要随系统规模超指数增长的样本量，属于NP难问题。已知对称性可降低复杂度，但对称性是否同时限制魔术量尚不清楚。

Method: 证明X态的RoM上界为√3，利用该对称性约束构造计算高效的下界方法，应用于自旋-1/2哈密顿量基态；并对平衡态为X态的哈密顿量解析推导魔术出现的临界温度。

Result: X态RoM ≤ √3；所提方法可在远超精确评估能力的系统尺寸下给出多体态RoM下界；对特定哈密顿量解析得到魔术涌现的临界温度。

Conclusion: 阐明了为何某些对称性约束魔术而其他不约束，提供可扩展的下界方法，并识别出多体非稳定子性可解析求解的非平凡区域。

Abstract: Nonstabilizerness is a critical resource for quantum advantage, but evaluating it, especially for mixed states, requires superexponentially many samples in system size, making the problem NP-hard. While symmetries are known to reduce this complexity, it is unclear whether they also restrict the amount of magic. In this work, we provide an explicit example of such a symmetry by proving that the Robustness of Magic (RoM) for N-qubit X-states is at most $\sqrt{3}$. Leveraging this symmetry constraint, we introduce a computationally efficient method to lower-bound the RoM of arbitrary many-body states, demonstrating its utility on the ground states of a spin-1/2 Hamiltonian with system sizes well beyond the reach of exact evaluation. Furthermore, for Hamiltonians whose equilibrium states are X-states, we analytically derive the critical temperature at which magic emerges. Our work shows why certain symmetries constrain magic while others do not, provides a scalable lower-bounding method, and identifies a nontrivial regime where many-body nonstabilizerness is analytically solvable.

</details>


### [16] [Collective Dynamics in Spin Chains with Constrained Dissipation: Classically Fragile but Quantum Robust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38333)
*Pietro Brighi,Gianluca Frazzei,Igor Lesanovsky,Juan P. Garrahan,Alberto Biella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究耗散远东模型（动力学约束自旋链）中经典涨落与量子涨落对相共存现象的不同影响，并绘制稳态相图与动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体系统中的集体非平衡现象受涨落强烈影响，尤其在相变附近，经典效应与量子效应的相互作用会改变协同行为，但二者在动力学约束体系中的竞争机制尚不清晰。

Method: 以耗散远东模型（动力学约束自旋链）为对象，采用集团平均场方法刻画稳态相图，并使用随机张量网络方法分析动力学行为。

Result: 无涨落时系统呈现涌现集体动力学与相共存；该现象对量子涨落稳健且被其进一步稳定，而经典涨落则在长时极限下抑制相共存。在双稳相内，局域磁化与纠缠动力学均表现出动力学非均匀性特征。

Conclusion: 量子涨落与经典动力学约束的竞争共同塑造多体系统的弛豫动力学与相结构。

Abstract: Collective non-equilibrium phenomena in many-body systems are strongly influenced by fluctuations, particularly in the vicinity of phase transitions, where the interplay between classical and quantum effects can alter cooperative behavior. Here, we investigate this classical-quantum competition in a kinetically constrained spin chain, the dissipative far-East model. In absence of fluctuations, the system exhibits emergent collective dynamics, resulting in phase coexistence. We find that this phenomenon is robust to quantum fluctuations, which further stabilize it, whereas classical fluctuations suppress phase coexistence at long times. We map out the steady-state phase diagram using a cluster mean-field approach and characterize the dynamics with stochastic tensor-network methods. Within the bistable phase we identify signatures of dynamical heterogeneity in both the local magnetization and the entanglement dynamics. This highlights how competing quantum fluctuations and classical kinetic constraints shape the relaxation dynamics and the phase structure of many-body systems.

</details>


### [17] [Exponential strong converse for blind quantum data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38328)
*Kohdai Kuroiwa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有限维混合态源的无辅助与纠缠辅助盲量子数据压缩建立了指数强逆定理，解决了最优速率处强逆是否成立的一般性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态源的压缩强逆已有结论，但混合态源的最优压缩率由量子态的结构分解决定，且对微小扰动极为敏感，导致压缩率与误差之间的权衡难以定量刻画；最优速率处是否存在强逆此前一般情形下仍未解决。

Method: 引入一种基于量子态结构的新重叠量（overlap quantity），将源信息的保持与传输系统的维数相联系，并据此给出无辅助压缩与无限纠缠辅助压缩的指数强逆界。

Result: 无辅助压缩下，对一般混合态源且不限制协议，任何低于最优的速率处精度随块长指数衰减；无限纠缠辅助盲压缩在其最优速率处亦满足指数强逆；两者结合得到完全可达速率区域之外任意压缩-纠缠速率对的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作肯定性地解决了混合态源盲压缩在最优速率处强逆是否成立的问题，并提供了可用于其他涉及混合态的量子信息处理任务逆定理分析的工具。

Abstract: We establish exponential strong converse theorems for blind quantum data compression of finite-dimensional mixed-state sources, with and without entanglement assistance. Blind compression is a fundamental quantum information processing task in which the sender aims to transmit quantum states without direct access to the classical label. Strong converse is a notable aspect of data compression: the accuracy of the compression abruptly drops down to zero once the compression rate goes below a threshold. Strong converses for data compression have been established for pure-state sources; however, for mixed-state sources, the optimal compression rate is determined by a structural decomposition of quantum states and can change substantially under small perturbations. This sensitivity makes it difficult to quantitatively analyze the trade-off between compression rate and error. Despite recent progress, whether a strong converse holds at the optimal rate has remained unresolved in general. In this work, we resolve this question affirmatively. For unassisted compression, we show that at every rate below the optimum, the accuracy decays exponentially with the block length for general mixed-state sources without restrictions on protocols. We also prove exponential strong converse for blind compression with unlimited entanglement assistance at its optimal rate. Combining the two strong converse bounds yields exponential decay at every compression-entanglement rate pair outside the complete achievable rate region. Our proof introduces a new overlap quantity based on the structure of quantum states; this quantity connects preservation of the source information to the dimension of the transmitted system. We expect that this approach provides useful tools for converse analyses of other quantum information-processing tasks involving mixed states.

</details>

*Andrea Cataldo,Emil J. Bergholtz,Daniel Leykam,Jun Gao,Ali W. Elshaari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用可编程光子集成电路，通过幺正伸缩在合成动量空间实现非厄米动力学，并用相移干涉恢复演化相位，从而在强耗散下测量动量空间拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米动力学中的拓扑信息编码在体演化的相位中，但强耗散会抑制承载该相位的振幅，导致相位信息难以直接读取。因此需要一种能够在强耗散条件下恢复演化相位、进而探测动量空间拓扑的方案。

Method: 构建可编程光子集成电路，借助幺正伸缩（unitary dilation）在合成动量空间中实现非厄米动力学；采用相移干涉术（phase-shifted interferometry）恢复演化相位。以非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型为对象区分平庸与非平庸Zak相位，并通过合成环面（synthetic torus）结合Rice-Mele泵浦进行扩展。

Result: 在非厄米SSH模型中成功区分平庸与非平庸Zak相位，并测得由例外点（exceptional point）诱导的相干缠绕数q=±1；在Rice-Mele泵浦构建的合成环面上，得到平庸与非平庸循环对应的第一陈数Ch₁=0与1。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条在强耗散非厄米动力学下探测动量空间拓扑的可编程路径，验证了通过幺正伸缩与相移干涉相结合来提取相位编码拓扑信息的可行性。

Abstract: Topology under non-Hermitian dynamics is encoded in the phase of the bulk evolution, yet strong dissipation suppresses the amplitudes carrying it. We resolve this using a programmable photonic integrated circuit that implements such dynamics in synthetic momentum space through unitary dilation, with the phase recovered by phase-shifted interferometry. For the non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model, the method distinguishes trivial and non-trivial Zak phases and yields a coherence winding $q=\pm 1$ induced by an exceptional point. Extending to a synthetic torus via a Rice-Mele pump gives the first Chern number $\mathrm{Ch}_1=0,1$ for trivial and non-trivial cycles, showing a programmable route to momentum-space topology under strongly dissipative non-Hermitian dynamics.

</details>

*O. S. Yáñez-Soria,G. Guarnieri,B. Çakmak,R. Román-Ancheyta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了由热辅助系统中粒子簇（量子比特或谐振子）通过产生稳态相干性的复合系统-环境耦合反复充能的量子比特电池的遍历性和热力学势。单参数编码环境量子统计，可实现闭式解。对于大簇，遍历性与环境统计无关，并在最优耦合下达到有限分数。遍历性在低温下呈现与第三定律相关的平台，且与平衡焓成正比。多个量子比特的电池中，相干性指数增长而遍历性线性增长。带电态为零温下具有有限熵的非平衡稳态，由开关碰撞相互作用的功维持。

Motivation: 研究量子热力学中量子电池的充能机制及相干性对可提取功的贡献，探索环境量子统计、簇大小及耦合方式对遍历性和热力学势的影响。

Method: 通过复合系统-环境耦合，使量子比特与热辅助系统簇（量子比特或谐振子）进行反复相互作用，产生能量基上的稳态相干性；解析推导单参数编码环境量子统计下的相干性和遍历性闭式表达式。

Result: 对于大簇，遍历性独立于环境统计，在最优耦合下达到平衡内能的普适分数；低温下遍历性出现平台，与第三定律相关；遍历性与平衡焓成正比，焓的斜率随热容消失。多量子比特电池中，相干性随量子比特数指数增长，遍历性线性增长，两者均保持近似恒定的最大值分数。带电态为零温下具有限熵的非平衡稳态，由开关碰撞相互作用的功维持，成本在平行耦合非零时非零且依赖环境统计。

Abstract: We study the ergotropy and the thermodynamic potentials of a qubit battery charged by repeated interactions with clusters of thermal ancillas, qubits or oscillators, through a composite system-bath coupling that generates steady-state coherence in the energy basis. A single parameter encoding the bath quantum statistics yields the coherence and ergotropy in closed form for any cluster size. For large clusters, the ergotropy becomes independent of the bath statistics and, at optimal couplings, reaches a universal fraction of the equilibrium internal energy of the qubit at the bath temperature. Since the charged state has no population inversion, all extractable work comes from coherence. The ergotropy is flat at low temperatures. We trace this plateau to the third law, and show that the ergotropy is proportional to the equilibrium enthalpy of the qubit, whose slope vanishes with the heat capacity. For a battery composed of multiple qubits, the coherence grows exponentially with the number of qubits while the ergotropy grows linearly, both remaining a nearly constant fraction of their respective maxima. The charged state is a non-equilibrium steady state with finite entropy at zero temperature, sustained by the work of switching the collisional interaction on and off. The cost is nonzero whenever the parallel coupling is nonzero, and, unlike the ergotropy, it depends on the bath statistics.

</details>

Abstract: Neodymium-doped gadolinium vanadate (Nd:GdVO$_4$) is a popular laser material at room temperature. It has received limited attention for quantum technologies because of the linebroadening effects of disordered gadolinium spins in the host. Here we investigate the optical properties at temperatures below the 2.5 K Néel temperature, revealing narrow optical transitions. We demonstrate that rare earth ions in magnetic hosts give us a new way of observing the signs of magnetic g-factors. The interplay between the exchange and Zeeman interactions means that we see clear evidence of negative g-factors. These results provide a detailed spectroscopic foundation for Nd:GdVO$_4$ and expand the understanding of its candidacy as a material for hybrid quantum systems.

Abstract: Whether quantum computation can reduce the amount of data sampled from an unknown probability distribution required to learn a prediction rule is a fundamental question in quantum machine learning. However, it has remained unclear whether forward-only access allows such an improvement.

To prove them, we establish a reduction using $q$ copies of the prepared state to approximate the Haar-averaged output of any $q$-query forward-only algorithm.

Our reduction also provides a new framework for analyzing limitations of forward state-preparation access via state-copy lower bounds.

Abstract: Understanding randomness formation under conservation laws is important both for characterizing chaotic phenomena in isolated quantum systems and for symmetry-constrained quantum information processing. $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits on an $N$-qubit system provide a minimal tractable setting for this problem, in which the formation rate of unitary designs was conjectured to be governed by charge transport in the underlying circuit geometry. However, whether charge transport indeed sets the slowest relaxation mode has remained an open problem, due to the difficulty in deriving a tight lower bound on the symmetric design formation rate. In this work, we resolve this problem by determining the optimal scaling of the unitary $k$-design formation rate for $k\leq O(\sqrt{\log N})$ across a wide range of circuit geometries, including lattices, expander graphs, and all-to-all interactions. In particular, for $k\geq 2$, we find that the formation rate is governed by a two-particle encounter rate---the rate at which two random walkers encounter each other in a given circuit geometry. This encounter rate is parametrically smaller than the single-particle transport rate in many geometries, thereby disproving the previous conjecture. We further establish that this encounter mechanism indeed sets the design formation rate by deriving its matching lower bound with a novel proof technique based on an auxiliary subgroup ensemble. Although this encounter mode imposes a robust bottleneck on design formation in $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits, we also show that this bottleneck can be circumvented by using symmetry-breaking local gates and provide an explicit protocol that achieves faster design formation. These results establish a new picture of randomness formation under conservation laws and provide guiding principles for characterizing and efficiently generating symmetry-constrained randomness.

Abstract: Efficient models for secure computation often rely on offline preprocessed correlations, which subsequently enable private online computation from minimal assumptions. Specifically, when the target resource is classical, our framework enables us to analyze the problem of disseminating private correlations from entanglement, in addition to classical resources. Our primary technical result is that QSNIR privacy in this context can be computed exactly via a semidefinite program (SDP) pair that quantifies an error operationally rooted in a simulation-based cheating advantage. Abstract: Near-field wavefront curvature enables joint angle-range localization, but exploiting it entails repeated covariance-eigenspace tests over a two-dimensional spherical manifold. Q-SPARSE prepares each spherical steering hypothesis as a quantum state, applies quantum phase estimation (QPE) to covariance-generated evolution, and converts the resolved spectral mass into a localization spectrum. Abstract: We introduce a classification of quantum phases of mixed states based on the ability to preserve and fault tolerantly transfer information. We show that the existence of a recovery threshold for local noise is a property of an entire phase. We prove that any two sets of states with nonzero thresholds that are related by finite-depth local channel circuits belong to the same fault tolerant phase. Our approach leads to a classification of subsystem codes without favoring any state of the gauge subsystem, and we demonstrate phase equivalence between 2D and 3D color codes using dimensional jumps within the 3D gauge color code. Lastly, we study how syndrome structure affects fault tolerance in circuits with measurements. We show that qudit stabilizer codes with extended syndromes permit single-shot state preparation, whereas a broad class of shallow circuits measuring point-like syndromes, including state preparation for 2D topological codes and non-Abelian topological orders with solvable anyon theories, is not fault tolerant.

Abstract: High-dimensional spatial entanglement generated via spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) provides a powerful resource for quantum information processing, yet its mode structure becomes increasingly complex when the pump occupies a higher-order Laguerre--Gaussian (LG) mode. Here, we develop an algebraic framework for characterizing biphoton spatial-mode entanglement by factorizing LG-pumped SPDC into a spatial-mode beam-splitter transformation followed by spatial two-mode squeezing. By choosing the biphoton LG basis radius as the geometric mean of the pump-beam radius and the crystal-induced correlation width, we obtain a natural modal basis in which the two operations can be treated separately. We further derive an analytical expression for the Schmidt number for arbitrary LG pump modes and squeezing strengths, revealing how the radial and azimuthal pump indices govern the dimensionality of the spatial entanglement. This algebraic characterization provides a unified description of the mode structure, conservation laws, and entanglement dimensionality of higher-order-LG-pumped SPDC.

Abstract: The Scrooge ensemble associated with a density matrix $ρ$ is the maximally random ensemble whose average density matrix is $ρ$. It has attracted growing interest for its applications to quantum many-body dynamics and quantum information. The intrinsic statistical properties of the Scrooge ensembles are encoded in their higher-order moments, yet they are nontrivial to analyze and prepare for they feature a non-polynomial Haar integrand, blocking direct use of Haar integration machinery. Existing physical mechanisms to sample multiple copies of states from the Scrooge ensembles, meanwhile, typically require costly postselection, rendering them inefficient for large system size. In this work, we develop efficient and accurate methods for analyzing and implementing Scrooge moments. We first introduce a polynomial approximation to the Scrooge $k$-th moments whose error is tunable down to a threshold that improves from inverse polynomial to exponential in $1/||ρ||_\infty$ over previous results. Building on our approximation, we then give the first efficient quantum algorithm that estimates observable expectation values using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(k^3/ε^5)$ copies of $ρ$ for any target error $ε$ above this threshold. We further provide a construction of a block encoding of the Scrooge $k$-th moment when the preparation circuit of $ρ$ is known. Of independent interest could be a key subroutine in our algorithms, which efficiently implements projection onto the $k$-fold symmetric subspace for $ρ^{\otimes k}$ using $\mathcal{O}(k^3/ε^2)$ samples of $ρ$, avoiding the naive $k!$ postselection cost of such a projection. These results provide new tools for both the theoretical analysis and practical prediction of properties of Scrooge ensembles, with potential applications to quantum many-body dynamics and quantum information processing.

Abstract: We introduce the terminology \emph{passive realism} to encapsulate the scale-independent assumption that a physical system's pre-existing properties can be passively observed without altering its subsequent dynamics. A necessary condition for passive realism is provided by the no-signalling in time (NSIT) condition, which states that a measurement outcome is independent of whether or not a prior measurement was performed. While closed quantum systems generically violate NSIT due to measurement back action, we investigate whether open-system dynamics can dissipate this disturbance to the environment and restore NSIT. By modeling the time evolution between sequential measurements as an arbitrary quantum channel, we show that such environmental masking is possible only in two special cases. Specifically, we show that if the initial state of a two-time sequential measurement scenario is not maximally mixed, and if the channel governing the dynamics between measurements is not a discard-and-prepare channel, then there necessarily exist binary projective measurements violating NSIT, hence violating passive realism. By providing a constructive procedure for determining these violating measurements---applied here to depolarizing and dephasing channels---we demonstrate that, outside these two cases, system-environment interactions cannot erase the statistical footprint of a quantum measurement for all measurement choices.

Concretely, we show that expectation values of local observables at time $t$ can be estimated to accuracy $\varepsilon$ using circuits of maximum depth $O\!\left(\left[1+\left(t/γ\right)^{3/2}\right]\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\right)$, independent of the system size $n$. The gap between our quantum and classical upper bounds leaves room for a substantial polynomial quantum advantage as dissipation weakens, an observation further supported by our numerical results.

Result: 在大系统尺寸极限下，算法总量子资源为 $O(N_t \log N_t)$，显著优于传统方法的 $O(N_t^2)$ 门开销；证明该标度在理论上是渐近最优的；并以一维无自旋 Hubbard 模型的格林函数计算作为演示验证。

Conclusion: 通过时间量子变量编码实现平衡关联函数的并行量子计算，将资源需求降至 $O(N_t \log N_t)$ 且达到理论最优下界，为高效计算动态关联函数（如格林函数）提供了新范式。

We show that this circuit needs to be run only $O(\log N_t)$ times, which in total requires $O(N_t \log N_t)$ quantum resources in the large system size limits. We prove that this scaling is optimal, and demonstrate our algorithm calculating the Green's function of a one-dimensional spinless Hubbard model.

</details>

Inspired by these works, we introduce the notion of meta-complexity of quantum relativization. We ask: for any two quantum complexity classes, under which "types" of quantum oracles are they equal or separated? Different pantheons of oracles (or quantum oracle types, e.g. unitary vs state, poly- vs superpoly-dimensional, closed under inverse or not) form a partially ordered set based on their power in separating complexity classes. Moreover, two oracle pantheons A and B are separated if there exists a pair of complexity classes that are separated under an oracle from pantheon A but yet the complexity classes are equivalent under all oracles from pantheon B.

Within our taxonomy, they remain equal relative to real or polynomial-dimensional unitary oracles and whenever inverse or conjugate access is supplied. In contrast, we give a separation relative to forward-only complex diagonal unitaries of superpolynomial dimension, as well as a separation relative to single-qubit state-preparation oracles. We view this as a test case for the meta-complexity of oracles which underscores the subtlety inherent to the relativization of quantum complexity classes.

</details>

</details>

Abstract: We develop a theory of exact transmission of finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras through quantum channels. These algebras describe hybrid classical-quantum information, allowing the dimension of the quantum system to depend on the classical message. Allowing arbitrary encodings and decodings yields a transmission set of algebra types, ordered by embedding, that unifies zero-error information theory with operator-algebraic error correction. In its absence, several incomparable maximal algebras can describe different optimal uses of the same channel, forcing the user to select one depending on the operational task and the type of information to be preserved. Finally, we construct a channel whose $n$-fold tensor powers have doubly exponentially many maximal algebra types, attaining the largest possible growth scaling for finite-dimensional channels. Consequently, new transmittable algebra types appear at arbitrarily large block-lengths for this channel, so its full transmission structure cannot be generated from any finite collection of bounded-block-length codes.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum measurements and channels determine how information is extracted, encoded, and transmitted in quantum protocols. Abstract: Open quantum systems can rapidly lose most microscopic information, leaving only a few degrees of freedom to govern their long-time dynamics. Classical Markov state models (MSMs) describe such metastable dynamics as transitions between a few representative phases and are a well-established tool in condensed matter and chemical physics. Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases (MUBs) describe quantum measurements for which certainty in one basis gives uniform outcomes in the others. Each basis in the ansatz consists of a diagonal phase matrix applied to a fixed unitary, which is a tensor product of Fourier matrices and, optionally, any real Hadamard matrix. This construction yields five MUBs in dimension 12, six in dimensions 48, 96, and 192, seven in dimensions 36 and 108, and ten in dimension 648. These bases can then be extended to exceed the tensor product bound, asymptotically, in every eighteenth dimension. This count grows as $\sqrt d$ and cannot be exceeded by tensor products of smaller sets.

Abstract: We introduce a framework for combinatorial optimization using sum-constrained continuous quadratic programs solvable by QCi's Dirac-3S photonic entropy computer. On the other hand, well-tuned classical continuous optimizers retain an edge only on the hardest planted-clique instances. Abstract: Given multiple uses of an unknown quantum channel, we determine whether it is a specified non-mixed-unitary channel or belongs to the set of mixed-unitary channels. Formulating this as a composite channel hypothesis testing problem, we characterize the Stein exponents achievable by parallel strategies under progressively weaker restrictions on probe states and auxiliary memory. In particular, we consider block-i.i.d. probes, which allow arbitrary correlations within blocks of fixed size while remaining i.i.d. across blocks, and derive finite-letter expressions of the corresponding Stein exponents. Varying the block size, which interpolates between fully i.i.d. and arbitrary probes, together with further restrictions on intra-block correlations and access to auxiliary memory, results in a multifaceted hierarchy. Without auxiliary memory, we prove that fully i.i.d. probes yield a vanishing Stein exponent for every unital non-mixed-unitary channel, while blocks of three product probes suffice to obtain a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel. For block size two, although product probes seem to be insufficient for O(3)-covariant channels, an entangled probe achieves a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel, even though every maximally entangled probe fails. In contrast, auxiliary memory makes fully i.i.d. probes sufficient by yielding a strictly positive exponent for every non-mixed-unitary channel, even without requiring input-reference entanglement, and this exponent admits a lower bound determined by the diamond-norm distance from the mixed-unitary set. Moreover, for odd-dimensional Werner-Holevo channels, we find that this exponent is infinite, with every full-Schmidt-rank pure state being optimal.

Method: 利用方差缩减技术构造CRVG，提出两种变体：3线路（单边）非递归CRVG与递归CRVG；通过中心点缓存机制复用量子测量，将内循环线路复杂度降低25%；并建立两条路径达到ε²-稳定点的理论分离。

Abstract: Variational Quantum Algorithms (VQAs) rely heavily on classical optimization routines to navigate high-dimensional, noisy parameter landscapes. However, the evaluation of analytical gradients via the parameter-shift rule requires $\mathcal{O}(p)$ circuit executions, rendering this approach computationally prohibitive for deep circuits. While simultaneous perturbation methods provide an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ alternative, their gradient estimates suffer from severe $\mathcal{O}(p)$ spatial variance, which impedes convergence. To resolve this tension, we introduce the Cached Recycled Variance-Reduced Gradient (CRVG) by leveraging variance reduction techniques. We formulate two variants: a 3-Circuit (1-Sided) Non-Recursive CRVG and a Recursive CRVG. By introducing a center-point caching mechanism, the 3-Circuit CRVG mathematically recycles quantum measurements to reduce the inner-loop circuit complexity by 25\% across both variants. We establish a theoretical separation between the two routing paths to achieve an $ε^2$-stationary point: the non-recursive variant reaches a sample complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\max\{p/ε^2, p^{2/3}/ε^{10/3}\})$, while the recursive variant achieves an improved complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\max\{p/ε^2, \sqrt{p}/ε^3\})$. Empirically, extensive evaluations on MaxCut and Maximum Independent Set (MIS) benchmarks validate these findings. While standard simultaneous perturbation remains a robust baseline across varying depths, CRVG demonstrates targeted advantages in specific amortizable regimes, such as the non-recursive variant on EfficientSU2 circuits and both variants on depth-three QAOA, yielding superior energy minimums and tighter output variances. Ultimately, CRVG establishes a principled bias-throughput trade-off for scaling near-term quantum optimization.

Abstract: High-order coherent-state modulation with reflective cavity-coupled atomic memories can distribute multiple heralded ebits per transmitted optical mode, but requires a collective midpoint POVM optimized for maximum distillable entanglement rather than minimum-error state discrimination. For 4-QAM, a variational POVM with more outcomes than constellation points improves the SRM rate by $4.0\%$ yet remains below capacity, revealing measurement-design headroom.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OMMP 在量子零和博弈中可达到 O(1/ε) 的平均迭代精度保证，但该精度依赖是否紧、能否实现几何（线性）末迭代收敛尚不明确，本文旨在回答这两个问题。

Conclusion: OMMP 的 O(1/ε) 精度依赖在均匀平均输出意义下是紧的；量子零和博弈中乐观类算法一般无法保证几何末迭代收敛，末迭代误差与对偶间隙仅呈多项式速率衰减。

Abstract: Optimistic matrix mirror-prox (OMMP) computes $ε$-approximate Nash equilibria in quantum zero-sum games with an $O(1/\varepsilon)$ average-iterate guarantee [arXiv:2311.10859]. We investigate whether this dependence on accuracy is tight and whether geometric last-iterate convergence can be guaranteed. We study these questions through explicit games with one qubit per player. Second, we construct a fixed game on which optimistic gradient descent-ascent (OGDA), initialized at the maximally mixed state, has last-iterate Frobenius distance to equilibrium $Θ(1/t)$ and duality gap $Θ(1/t^3)$ for every sufficiently small fixed step size. A separate fixed game exhibits arbitrarily long delays in reducing the initial error by a constant factor across a family of initial states. Finally, we give a fixed game with a unique, strictly complementary equilibrium on which optimistic matrix multiplicative weights updates (OMMWU) converge only polynomially from the maximally mixed state for every fixed positive step size. The last-iterate Frobenius distance and quantum relative entropy from the equilibrium to the iterates decay as $Θ(1/t)$, while the duality gap decays as $Θ(1/t^2)$.

</details>

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) seeks quantum advantage by sampling photon-number patterns generated with squeezed inputs and passive linear optics. Many proposed GBS applications instead target function computation by applying functions to mode-resolved photon-number outcomes---a natural form of experimental postprocessing that produces classical outputs. Sampling hardness alone, however, does not determine the complexity of these tasks. By analyzing the irreducible decomposition of fixed-photon-number operator spaces, we prove that the expectation value of every such function in the average case over passive linear-optical networks can be classically evaluated for inputs with finite squeezing strength. We also provide a classical algorithm that estimates this value to inverse-polynomial additive error. The result provides new theoretical tools for analyzing linear-optical quantum systems, helps clarify the origin of current GBS hardness evidence, and inspires new applications of GBS with genuine quantum advantages.

</details>

Abstract: Number-conserving cooling can fail to prepare an interacting flat-band target when Pauli exclusion blocks its transfer destinations. We study this failure on vertex-edge decorated graphs at one particle per flat orbital. For a chain with fixed finite cooling range, we construct a physical Fock initial state whose overlap with a wrong dark state is independent of system size. This gives fidelity and bright-density bounds valid at every time. In higher dimensions, the bare overlap decays with boundary area, while local boundary rotations prepare states with a finite failure weight in constant circuit depth. Dephasing the occupations of all compact bright modes restores global attraction to the ferromagnetic target when the graph and cooling destinations satisfy the stated conditions. The result allows Hamiltonians that preserve the target. The compressed dynamics in the strong-dephasing limit also remains attractive and has a positive gap at each fixed size. Gram calculations, finite-system dynamics, and Liouvillian spectra test the analytic results. Abstract: In this work, we propose a defect-aware parallel atom reloading protocol that reloads atoms based on defect information to increase the atom filling rate. The proposed protocol is useful for neutral-atom quantum computers, where reloading atoms into the defects, or atom-loss positions, is mandatory for continuous operation against atom loss. Our protocol takes advantage of both parallelism and defect awareness to improve atom-reloading efficiency. The proposed protocol consists of the coherent atom reloading operation and the defect-aware planner. The former replenishes the defects with atoms preserving the quantum information coherently. This coherence allows us to adaptively choose the reloading strategy based on the current defect configuration. The latter determines the atom reloading pattern to efficiently replenish the defects. In numerical experiments assuming a low-noise environment, we confirmed that the average atom filling rate improved from 98.61% to 99.94% for a 36 x 90 atom array, matching the filling rate obtained with a mixed integer programming solver to within 0.001 percentage points while planning each reload in under 0.1 ms. These results demonstrate that defect-aware parallel reloading can achieve and sustain high atom filling rates while the performance of the defect-aware planner meets the real-time requirements of neutral-atom quantum computers.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文在同态加密假设下实现了交互论证并行重复的紧致指数级可靠性误差降低，并将适用范围从经典扩展至后量子作弊证明者及门限验证者，进而首次给出仅依赖量子同态加密的常数轮 QMA 简洁论证。

Abstract: We show that assuming the existence of homomorphic encryption, parallel repetition of all interactive arguments (after being run under homomorphic encryption) reduces the soundness error at a tight exponential rate even in the post-quantum setting. Moreover, we generalize this result to hold for threshold verifiers, where the parallel repeated verifier accepts if and only if at least $t$ of the executions are accepted (for some threshold $t$). Prior to this work, these results were known only when the cheating prover was assumed to be classical, and it was not known how to achieve tight bounds.
  As a corollary, we construct the first constant-round succinct argument for $\mathsf{QMA}$ with negligible completeness and soundness errors assuming only the existence of quantum homomorphic encryption.

</details>

Abstract: The generation of certified random bits is an emerging near-term application of quantum computers. Potential applications, such as randomness beacons and CRS generation, require nearly uniform randomness, whose rate (the ratio of min-entropy to bitlength) is ~ 1. However, existing protocols for certified randomness either produce weakly random strings with rate o(1), or they require a trusted random seed.
  We show how to certify randomness with the optimal rate ~ 1 without requiring any trusted randomness from the verifier. Our protocol is secure unconditionally in the quantum random oracle model (QROM). This improves the result of Coladangelo et al., who certified weakly random strings unconditionally in the QROM, and answers a question posed by Aaronson and Hung.
  We also define and construct a proof of conditional min-entropy, which certifies optimal min-entropy even conditioned on an adversarially chosen transcript. We show an application of this primitive to randomness beacons, where each pulse should have high min-entropy conditioned on all previous messages.

</details>


### [82] [The Commuting Local Hamiltonian Problem: Relativized Evidence Against BQP-Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38957)
*Itay Shalit,Mark Zhandry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造经典 oracle,证明了 $\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}\neq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$,从而给出反对一般 CLH 问题为 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全(乃至 $\mathsf{BQP}$-困难)的相对化证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Commuting local-Hamiltonian (CLH) 问题是 local-Hamiltonian 问题的限制,要求哈密顿量各项两两对易。已有大量工作表明某些 CLH 家族属于 $\mathsf{NP}$,但一直缺乏反对一般 CLH 问题为 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全的正式证据。由于 CLH 恰好完全于 $\mathsf{QIMA}$(由局部门对易的量子验证者定义的复杂性类),因此 CLH 是否 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全等价于 $\mathsf{QIMA}=\mathsf{QMA}$ 是否成立。作者希望从相对化角度(即 oracle 分离)给出反对证据,以说明一般 CLH 不太可能是 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全的。

Method: 作者引入了 $\mathsf{QIMA}$ 的经典 oracle 类比 $\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$,即允许验证者在拥有经典 oracle 访问的情况下、其局部门仍需两两对易。然后构造出一个特定的经典 oracle $\mathcal{O}$,使得在此 oracle 下 $\mathsf{BQP}^{\mathcal{O}}\not\subseteq\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$。论证的关键在于:对任意经典 oracle $\mathcal{O}$ 都有 $\mathsf{BQP}^{\mathcal{O}}\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$,于是所构造的 oracle 同时给出 $\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}\neq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$。

Result: 存在一个经典 oracle $\mathcal{O}$,使得 $\mathsf{BQP}^{\mathcal{O}}\not\subseteq\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$;结合 $\mathsf{BQP}^{\mathcal{O}}\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$,得到 $\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}\neq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了相对化证据,反对一般 commuting local-Hamiltonian 问题是 $\mathsf{BQP}$-困难的,因而也反对其为 $\mathsf{QMA}$-完全的。这补上了此前缺失的、针对一般 CLH 问题难度上界的反证方向。

Abstract: The commuting local-Hamiltonian (CLH) problem is a restriction of the local-Hamiltonian problem, in which the terms of the Hamiltonian are required to pairwise commute. A long line of work has shown that the problem lies in $\mathsf{NP}$ for certain families of commuting local Hamiltonians. Nevertheless, there has been no formal evidence against the possibility that the general CLH problem is $\mathsf{QMA}$-complete.
  CLH is complete for the complexity class $\mathsf{QIMA}$, defined through quantum verifiers whose local gates commute. Therefore, CLH is $\mathsf{QMA}$-complete if and only if $\mathsf{QIMA}=\mathsf{QMA}$. In this work, we introduce a classical-oracle analogue $\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$ and construct a classical oracle $\mathcal{O}$ such that $\mathsf{BQP}^{\mathcal{O}}\not\subseteq\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$.
  Since $\mathsf{BQP}^{\mathcal{O}}\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$ for any classical oracle $\mathcal{O}$, this implies $\mathsf{QIMA}^{\mathcal{O}}\neq\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathcal{O}}$ for our constructed oracle. Thus, our result provides relativized evidence against the possibility that the general commuting local-Hamiltonian problem is $\mathsf{BQP}$-hard, and hence also against the possibility that it is $\mathsf{QMA}$-complete.

Abstract: Pseudorandom error-correcting codes (PRCs), introduced by Christ and Gunn [CRYPTO 2024], are classical error-correcting codes whose codewords are computationally indistinguishable from uniformly random strings. We initiate the study of quantum analogues of PRCs. We define quantum pseudorandom error-correcting codes (QPRCs). Assuming that Learning Parity with Noise (LPN) is hard for $2^{O(\sqrt{n})}$-time quantum algorithms, we construct QPRCs whose encodings are indistinguishable from Haar-random isometries. We call these pseudorandom isometric error-correcting codes (PRICs), and our construction is robust to all $o(n \frac{\log \log n}{\log n})$-local quantum noise, where $n$ is the number of physical qubits. Under the same assumption, we also construct QPRCs whose encodings are indistinguishable from the completely depolarizing channel and that are robust to all $αn$-local quantum noise for some constant $α>0$. The latter QPRCs give a direct quantum analogue of classical PRCs.

First, we introduce a new classical cryptographic primitive which we call pseudorandom functional error-correcting codes (PRFCs) and construct them under the same LPN assumption. Second, we develop a new efficient decoding procedure within the codeword-stabilized (CWS) framework introduced in [Cross, Smith, Smolin, and Zeng, IEEE ISIT 2008], which is a generic framework for constructing quantum error-correcting codes by combining (possibly nonlinear) classical error-correcting codes and graphs. This resolves the open problem of finding a general method for efficiently decoding such CWS codes based on nonlinear classical codes, raised in [Li, Dumer, Grassl, and Pryadko, Physical Review A 2010].

Extending this line of work, we construct asymptotically good CSS codes with \textbf{fully addressable} transversal $T$, permitting selective control over which logical qubits receive a $T$ gate. We also give Pauli-LDPC codes with transversal $T$ and growing distance at vanishing rate; and a protected-subsystem construction with fully addressable transversal $T$, constant protected rate, distance $Ω(\sqrt n)$, and sparse (non-Pauli) checks. More generally, we obtain asymptotically good binary codes with full transversal addressability for every fixed finite-order one-qubit gate, retaining CSS realizations for dyadic phase gates such as $\sqrt T$ and using generally nonadditive codes for rotations such as $R_z(π/7)$. The remaining challenge is to construct asymptotically good Pauli-LDPC codes with fully addressable transversal non-Clifford one-qubit gates.

Abstract: The rapidly growing landscape of quantum error-correction (QEC) protocols has produced a wealth of numerical data, but comparatively few heuristics for understanding and predicting their performance. Here, we develop a simple entropy-based proxy that predicts the thresholds of a variety of QEC protocols, ranging from the code-capacity setting of Clifford-deformed surface codes with biased Pauli or erasure noise to the circuit-level noise model of the surface or color codes with flag qubits. Our proxy estimates the threshold by locally comparing the noise entropy with the error information gained via stabilizer measurements (or spacetime detectors and flag outcomes in the circuit-level settings). Despite neglecting correlations between stabilizer outcomes and the contribution from code degeneracy, the proxy captures the main trends across diverse settings and yields threshold estimates in good agreement with numerical results. Our work develops much-needed phenomenology that enables simple back-of-the-envelope estimates of QEC performance, recovering and providing an explanation for the results of computationally intensive detailed simulations.

</details>

Abstract: Broadening the study of quantum optimization algorithms from binary to $k$-element alphabets has been shown to open new avenues for potential quantum advantage. A quantum advantage claim for approximate optimization requires showing that, under the same assumptions, an efficient quantum algorithm provably achieves a better performance than can be proven for the best known efficient classical algorithms. We study local classical and quantum algorithms for Max-$k$-Cut on $d$-regular graphs of girth $g$. We advance classical algorithms for this problem by developing a local vector algorithm based on the explicit vector construction of Thompson, Parekh, and Marwaha (TPM). Our algorithm gives the best provable cut fraction guarantee among known efficient classical algorithms on regular graphs for $k\geq3$. To evaluate the performance of QAOA under identical assumptions of girth and regularity, we develop tensor network techniques for general $d$ and $k \geq 2$. In addition, using an equivalence to a coupled qudit--boson system, we compute the QAOA performance in the infinite-degree limit. Together, these techniques give provable guarantees on QAOA performance on large graphs. Despite the improvements we introduce to the classical algorithm, QAOA achieves a better cut fraction guarantee for depths $p\geq 9$, corresponding to girth $g\geq 20$, for both finite- and infinite-degree regimes. Thus, we obtain an apparent quantum advantage from applying QAOA to the Max-$k$-Cut problem.

</details>


### [87] [Fixed-Point Bifurcations and Path-Information Bounds in Nonlinear Exceptional-Point Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39046)
*Xue-Hao Yu,Cong-Feng Qiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明非线性奇异点(NEP)传感中的响应度奇异性必然伴随动力学不动点分岔，在局部涨落区响应度与长期频率不确定度的奇异性均由临界弛豫速率决定，导致信噪比精确抵消，不存在发散增强，并给出普适的路径信息上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性奇异点传感器的噪声-响应度抵消限制了其性能，非线性奇异点(NEP)被提出用于规避该限制，理论上可在哈密顿量Petermann因子保持有限时获得频率响应奇异，从而暗示信噪比(SNR)的真正提升。然而，近期针对非线性涨落的模型特定分析得出相互矛盾的结论，亟需一个与模型无关的一般性判据来澄清NEP传感的SNR增强极限。

Method: 采用理论证明的方法：(1) 证明任意奇异的稳态频率响应都要求非线性动力学发生不动点分岔；(2) 在局部涨落区分析响应度与长期频率不确定度，指出二者奇异性均由临界弛豫速率标定，量化SNR中的抵消；(3) 推导同时适用于局部与非线性涨落区的路径信息(path-information)上界。

Result: 发现响应度与长期频率不确定度的奇异性由同一临界弛豫速率决定，SNR中精确抵消，排除了任何发散增强；推导出的路径信息上界对局部和非线性涨落区均成立，对长期SNR增强给出有限天花板；该上界在局部涨落区可达到。

Conclusion: 非线性奇异点无法突破信噪比的有限增强上限，噪声-响应度抵消在非线性情形下依然存在；但局部涨落参数设置下的频率读出是信息最优的，达到了路径信息上界，为NEP传感器设计提供了理论边界。

Abstract: Nonlinear exceptional points (NEPs) were proposed to circumvent the noise--responsivity cancellation that limits linear exceptional-point sensors. At an NEP, the stationary frequency response can be singular while the Hamiltonian Petermann factor remains finite, suggesting a genuine signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) enhancement. Yet recent model-specific analyses of nonlinear fluctuations have reached conflicting conclusions. We prove that any singular stationary frequency response requires a fixed-point bifurcation of the nonlinear dynamics. In local fluctuation regimes, the singularities in responsivity and long-time frequency uncertainty are both set by the critical relaxation rate, leading to exact cancellation in the SNR and precluding any divergent enhancement. Furthermore, we derive a path-information bound that applies across both local and nonlinear fluctuation regimes and places a finite ceiling on the long-time SNR enhancement. Notably, this upper bound is attainable in the local fluctuation regime, implying that frequency readout under such parameter settings is information-optimal.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum circuit compilation with constant overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39092)
*Chenyi Zhang,Xinyu Tan,Robin Kothari,David Gosset,Craig Gidney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 连续门集编译到离散通用门集通常有开销：含 G 个任意单/双比特门的电路用 {H,T,CNOT} 做 ε-近似需每门替换为 O(log(G/ε)) 个基本门。本文证明，对于多项式规模电路与逆多项式目标误差，利用自适应性可以避免这一开销，且该结果是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用量子计算需将连续门集编译到离散通用门集（如 {H,T,CNOT}），而标准编译会引入每门 O(log(G/ε)) 的额外开销。作者希望探究这一开销是否必要，特别是在电路规模与目标精度有特定约束时，能否借助自适应性消除该开销。

Method: 利用自适应（adaptivity）编译方案，针对由 G 个任意单比特和双比特门组成的多项式规模电路，在逆多项式目标误差 ε 下构造编译过程，避免逐门离散化带来的对数开销。

Result: 证明了在多项式规模电路和逆多项式目标误差的情形下，自适应编译可以避免 O(log(G/ε)) 的每门开销；同时指出该结果在相应设定下是最优的。

Conclusion: 自适应编译能够消除连续门集到离散通用门集的标准编译开销，且所达到的性能在多项式规模电路、逆多项式误差条件下是紧的/最优的。

Abstract: Compiling a continuous gate set to a discrete universal gate set generally incurs an overhead. A circuit composed of $G$ arbitrary one- and two-qubit gates can be $ε$-approximated by a sequence of gates from $\{H,T,\mathrm{CNOT}\}$ by replacing each original gate by a sequence of $O(\log(G/ε))$ elementary gates. We show that this overhead can be avoided using adaptivity for polynomial-sized circuits with inverse-polynomial target error, which is optimal.

</details>


### [89] [Optimal testing of fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39113)
*Vishnu Iyer,Bryan O'Gorman,Ojas Parekh,Kevin Thompson,Andrew Zhao*

TL;DR: 该论文研究高斯量子态的量子态性质测试问题，证明五类高斯量子态（费米子高斯态、Slater行列式、玻色子高斯态、零均值玻色子高斯态、玻色子相干态）的性质测试样本复杂度紧确为Θ(1/ε²)，与系统尺寸无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知存在某些态类，其性质测试的样本复杂度必然随系统规模增长；而已有对部分高斯态类的测试器分析仅给出随模式数多项式增长的上界。本文旨在识别样本复杂度与尺寸无关的态类，并给出紧确的样本复杂度刻画。

Method: 证明分为上界与下界两部分。上界方面，测试器为对两或三个副本的"最高"不可约表示（irrep）上的投影；通过下界刻画拒绝概率随离类距离的增速，核心技术是对称扩展测试投影算子的谱隙界定，从而得到常数样本复杂度。下界方面，基于二元态区分（binary state discrimination）为每一类构造匹配的下界以确立最优性。方法亦推广至容忍（tolerant）情形，即情形(A)中ψ可与C相差O(ε)。

Result: 对五类高斯量子态得到紧确样本复杂度Θ(1/ε²)，实现尺寸无关的常数样本复杂度；测试器与最优性结论均扩展到容忍测试情形。

Conclusion: 高斯量子态的性质测试可用常数个样本高效完成，样本复杂度紧确为Θ(1/ε²)，显著优于此前多项式模式数的上界，并为容忍情形提供最优测试方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of property testing quantum states: given a pure state $|ψ\rangle$ that either (A) belongs to some class $C$ of pure states, or (B) is $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all states in $C$, determine which is the case with high probability. It is known that there exists classes $C$ for which the sample complexity of property testing necessarily scales with the size of the system. In this work, we identify classes for which testing only requires a size-independent number of samples. We prove a tight sample complexity of $Θ(1/\varepsilon^2)$ for the property testing of five important classes of Gaussian quantum states: 1) Fermionic Gaussian states, 2) Slater determinants, 3) bosonic Gaussian states, 4) zero-mean bosonic Gaussian states, and 5) bosonic coherent states.
  While property testers for some of these classes have been studied previously, those analyses only gave upper bounds scaling polynomially in the number of modes. In each case, our tester is the projection onto the ``top'' irrep of two or three copies. We get to constant sample complexity by lower bounding how quickly the rejection probability increases with distance from the class; the main technical component involves bounding the spectral gap of symmetric extension of the test projector. We then establish optimality by proving matching lower bounds based on binary state discrimination for each class. Our testers and optimality claims also extend to the tolerant setting, wherein $ψ$ may be $O(\varepsilon)$-close to $C$ in case (A), rather than lying exactly inside.

</details>


### [90] [Complete parameterization and parameter-space topology of discrete Wigner representations on $d \times d$ phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39119)
*Lucky K. Antonopoulos,Nicolas C. Menicucci*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对有限维量子系统中非唯一的环面离散Wigner函数（DWF）进行了完整分类，通过显式求解投影模板（projected-stencil）可容许性条件，刻画了相位点算子满足Hermitian、单位迹、Hilbert-Schmidt正交及Weyl-Heisenberg协变的标记单量子比特 $d\times d$ 表示。分类给出了奇偶维度下不同的参数空间，并区分了由DWF有效性内在决定的自由度与由边际及位移代数要求所选择的自由度。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环面离散Wigner函数在有限维系统中并非唯一，存在多种满足物理约束的有效表示。以往研究（模板定理）将这一族表示为某个 $2d\times2d$ 父表示的"下降（descents）"，但其投影模板的可容许性条件未被显式求解。作者希望完整刻画这一自由度空间，厘清哪些自由度是DWF有效性本身固有的，哪些是由边际（marginal）约束或位移代数（displacement-algebra）要求额外选定的，并明确奇偶维度之间的结构性差异。

Method: 1. 在辛-傅里叶（symplectic-Fourier）表示下，显式求解投影模板可容许性条件；2. 证明可容许性固定了模（modulus），仅留下 $d\times d$ 基元胞上的相位数据，满足依赖于宇称的"扭曲奇性（twisted-oddness）"关系；3. 给出参数空间：奇数 $d$ 为 $(S^1)^{(d^2-1)/2}$，偶数 $d$ 为 $(S^1)^{(d^2-4)/2}\times\mathbb{Z}_2^3$；4. 施加规范水平与垂直边际约束，将参数空间分别约化为 $(S^1)^{(d-1)^2/2}$ 和 $(S^1)^{d(d-2)/2}\times\mathbb{Z}_2$；5. 用同一相位数据参数化加倍Weyl-Heisenberg位移算子的保有效性重相位（rephasings），并要求算子阶整除Hilbert空间维度 $d$，将每个 $S^1$ 因子替换为离散的 $\mathbb{Z}_d$ 因子；6. 用辛-傅里叶特征函数编码相同的算子信息。

Result: - 可容许性条件固定模，相位数据满足宇称依赖的扭曲奇性关系，参数空间为奇数 $(S^1)^{(d^2-1)/2}$、偶数 $(S^1)^{(d^2-4)/2}\times\mathbb{Z}_2^3$；- 水平与垂直边际约化后分别为 $(S^1)^{(d-1)^2/2}$ 和 $(S^1)^{d(d-2)/2}\times\mathbb{Z}_2$；- 要求位移算子阶整除 $d$ 后，每个 $S^1$ 因子被离散 $\mathbb{Z}_d$ 因子替代；- 辛-傅里叶特征函数的逐点模独立于所选的合法模板约定，即这些特征函数编码了相同的算子信息。

Conclusion: 该分类清晰分离了DWF有效性内在的自由度与由边际及位移代数要求所选择的自由度，并显式揭示了奇数维与偶数维之间的结构性区别。相同相位数据同时参数化了加倍Weyl-Heisenberg位移算子的保有效性重相位，特征函数逐点模与有效模板约定的无关性表明算子信息不依赖于具体模板选择。

Abstract: Toroidal discrete Wigner functions (DWFs) for finite-dimensional systems are non-unique. We classify the labeled single-qudit ${d\times d}$ representations whose phase-point operators are Hermitian, unit-trace, Hilbert-Schmidt orthogonal, and Weyl-Heisenberg covariant. Our previously established stencil theorem expresses this family as descents of a doubled ${2d\times2d}$ parent. Here, we solve its projected-stencil admissibility conditions explicitly. In the symplectic-Fourier representation, admissibility fixes the modulus, leaving phase data on a ${d\times d}$ base cell satisfying a parity-dependent twisted-oddness relation. This gives the parameter space ${(S^1)^{(d^2-1)/2}}$ for odd ${d}$ and ${(S^1)^{(d^2-4)/2}\times\mathbb{Z}_2^3}$ for even ${d}$. Canonical horizontal and vertical marginals reduce these spaces to ${(S^1)^{(d-1)^2/2}}$ and ${(S^1)^{d(d-2)/2}\times\mathbb{Z}_2}$, respectively. The same phase data parameterize validity-preserving rephasings of the doubled Weyl-Heisenberg displacement operators. Requiring these operators to have order dividing the Hilbert-space dimension ${d}$ replaces each ${S^1}$ factor by a discrete ${\mathbb{Z}_d}$ factor. The associated symplectic-Fourier characteristic functions encode the same operator information, with pointwise magnitudes independent of the valid stencil convention. This classification separates the freedom intrinsic to DWF validity from that selected by marginal and displacement-algebra requirements and makes explicit the structural distinction between odd and even dimensions.

</details>


### [91] [Bivariate Bicycle Codes and Metachecks: Syndrome Repair, Measurement-Fault Ambiguity, and Logical Obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39121)
*Mohammad Rowshan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二元自行车（BB）码中稳定子校验的依赖性所蕴含的元校验（metacheck）冗余能否用于综合征修复，并揭示其与码逻辑结构的相互作用。通过零化子商与有效综合征理想的约化分析，给出距离二刻划、可区分单故障的单位群界，以及有界k族在固定测量错误率下仅靠元综合征精确修复失效的族级阻断；同时用标准例子（[[72,12,6]]与Gross [[144,12,12]]）做精确区分，并给出联合译码优于分离修复的唯象实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障的综合征测量会破坏原本正确的量子纠错步骤。BB码含有相互依赖的稳定子校验，其测得综合征满足可用作metacheck的宇称约束。作者希望弄清这种内建冗余在何种条件下真正支持综合征修复，以及它与码逻辑结构的相互作用，从而区分测量歧义与由此诱发的数据错误。

Method: 采用代数方法：用零化子商（annihilator quotients）刻画单块逻辑类，并判定混合块搜索何时不可避免；将metasyndrome等价于模有效综合征理想的约化；由此把平移群映入维数为k/2的商代数的单位群，其核为K_M；据此给出距离二刻划、可区分单故障的单位群界与族级阻断；并进行精确计算与持续唯象实验比较。

Result: 对相互依赖校验的BB码，metasyndrome可视为模有效综合征理想的约化；平移群映入维数为k/2的商代数单位群，核为K_M，由此得到距离二刻划与可区分单故障的单位群界。族级阻断表明：对有界k且块长增长的BB族，任意固定测量错误率下仅用metasyndrome的精确修复失效概率趋于1。无中间数据故障时，同一轨道计数给出消除所有单故障歧义所需的最小部分二次测量。精确计算区分了标准例子：[[72,12,6]]码综合征距离为三，最小权修复可纠正每个单测量故障；Gross [[144,12,12]]码有36对不可区分的单故障对。持续唯象实验显示相同定性对比，并在歧义更强的码上支持联合数据—测量译码优于分离修复阶段。

Conclusion: 该分析将作者此前的互素周期处理推广到一般两块BB码，并分离了测量歧义与其可能诱发的数据错误；说明内建元校验冗余并非总能支持综合征修复，其可用性受码逻辑结构（如逻辑类与K_M）限制，实践上在歧义较强时应优先采用联合数据—测量译码而非先修复综合征。

Abstract: Faulty syndrome measurements can corrupt an otherwise correct quantum-error correction step. Bivariate bicycle (BB) codes contain dependent stabilizer checks, so their measured syndromes obey parity constraints that can be used as metachecks. We study when this built-in redundancy actually supports syndrome repair and how it interacts with the logical structure of the code. Annihilator quotients identify single-block logical classes and show when a mixed-block search is unavoidable. For syndrome repair, the metasyndrome is equivalent to reduction modulo the valid-syndrome ideal. The translation group therefore maps into the unit group of a quotient algebra of dimension $k/2$, with kernel $K_M$. This gives a distance-two characterization, a unit-group bound on distinguishable single faults, and a family-level obstruction: for bounded-$k$ BB families with growing block length, metasyndrome-only exact repair fails with probability tending to one at any fixed measurement-error rate. With no intervening data fault, the same orbit count also gives the minimum partial second measurement needed to remove every single-fault ambiguity. Exact calculations separate the standard examples. The $[[72,12,6]]$ code has syndrome distance three and minimum-weight repair corrects every single measurement fault, whereas Gross $[[144,12,12]]$ has 36 indistinguishable single-fault pairs. Sustained phenomenological experiments show the same qualitative contrast and favor joint data--measurement decoding over a separated repair stage on the codes with stronger ambiguity. The analysis extends our earlier coprime-period treatment to general two-block BB codes and separates measurement ambiguity from the data error it can induce.

</details>


### [92] [An uncertainty principle for entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39126)
*Mitali Nanda,Daochen Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对双体Hilbert空间中弱纠缠基与强纠缠基之间的不确定性原理给出了定量刻画：任意态在一个基中局域化，则在另一基中必去局域化，其Shannon熵之和下界由两组基态的纠缠Rényi熵之差决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不确定性原理刻画了态在两组基之间的局域-去局域权衡，但尚不清楚当两组基具有不同纠缠程度（一组弱纠缠、一组强纠缠）时是否存在类似的定量约束，以及该约束能否用于限制弱纠缠态表示强纠缠本征态Hamiltonian基态的能力。

Method: 基于新的叠加态纠缠量的上下界，将任一态在两组正交归一基下的系数分布Shannon熵之和，用两基态的纠缠Rényi熵之差作为下界进行界定；并将其应用于具有强纠缠能量本征态的Hamiltonian基态表示问题。

Result: 建立了弱纠缠基与强纠缠基之间的定量不确定性关系，给出了Shannon熵之和的下界；所得叠加态纠缠上下界具有独立价值；该结果限制弱纠缠态表示强纠缠本征态Hamiltonian基态的能力，并在Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 弱纠缠与强纠缠基之间存在由纠缠Rényi熵差控制的不确定性权衡，为理解纠缠基下的表示能力及SYK模型等强纠缠体系提供了新工具与量化约束。

Abstract: Consider two orthonormal bases of a bipartite Hilbert space such that states in one basis are weakly entangled and states in the other are strongly entangled. We establish a quantitative version of the following uncertainty principle: every state that is localized in one basis must be delocalized in the other. More specifically, we lower bound the sum of the Shannon entropies of any state's coefficient distributions in the two bases by the difference between the entanglement Rényi entropies of their basis states. The result follows from new lower and upper bounds on the entanglement of superpositions that may be of independent interest. As an application, our result limits the power of weakly-entangled states in representing ground states of Hamiltonians with strongly-entangled energy eigenstates. We illustrate this application numerically for the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model.

</details>


### [93] [Geometric Characterization and Feasible Region Analysis of Bipartite Qutrit Bound Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39141)
*Mazhar Ali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在双分体qutrit系统中结合PPT判据与结构化非完全正映射，对三参数族ρ_{a,b,c}进行精确解析与几何划分，得到PPT区域的闭式二次边界及束缚纠缠的线性阈值，并证明该结构化映射可认证阈值区域内的全部束缚纠缠，占PPT叶面积的14.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画双分体qutrit系统中的束缚纠缠通常依赖PPT判据，但PPT仅给出必要条件，难以精确界定束缚纠缠子区域。作者希望借助结构化的非完全正映射，对物理态单纯形做精确划分，从而给出束缚纠缠的解析边界与几何刻画。

Method: 结合正部分转置（PPT）判据与一种结构化的非完全正映射，对涵盖三个参数的双分体qutrit族ρ_{a,b,c}进行解析与几何分析，推导其物理态单纯形的分区边界，并对该分解施加结构映射以认证束缚纠缠。

Result: 得到控制叶状PPT区域的闭式二次边界a²+ab+b²-b≤0（定义域0<a≤1/3），以及刻画PPT束缚纠缠子区域的精确线性阈值a>c。该族严格推广了著名的Horodecki束缚纠缠态，后者作为三维叶的单个切片出现；结构映射能认证整个阈值区域内的束缚纠缠，占PPT叶总面积的14.76%。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了双分体qutrit束缚纠缠的精确解析与几何刻画，明确了PPT叶的二次边界和束缚纠缠的线性阈值，表明结构映射可完整认证阈值区域，并揭示了Horodecki束缚纠缠态是本族的一个特例切片。

Abstract: We characterize bound entanglement in bipartite qutrit systems by combining the Positive Partial Transpose (PPT) criterion with a structural, non-completely positive map. Focusing on a comprehensive three-parameter bipartite qutrit family $ρ_{a,b,c}$, we present an exact analytical and geometric partitioning of the physical state space simplex. We derive the closed-form quadratic boundary governing the leaf-like PPT region $(a^2+ab+b^2-b \leq 0)$ over the domain $0 < a \leq \frac{1}{3}$, and identify the exact linear threshold $(a > c)$ that characterizes the PPT bound entangled sub-region. This family strictly generalizes the well-known Horodecki bound entangled states, which appear as a single slice of the three-dimensional leaf. Applying the structural map to this decomposition, we show that it certifies bound entanglement throughout the entire threshold region, accounting for $14.76\%$ of the total PPT leaf area.

</details>


### [94] [A Width-Matched Comparison of Hybrid Quantum-Classical Self-Supervised Learning for Fingerprint Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39172)
*Maria S. Edwards,Kidwell Dlamini,Pin-An Lin,Wen-Hsien Hsu,Wen-Chieh Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将QuFeX量子特征提取模块嵌入SimCLR、MoCo v2（对比式）与BYOL（非对比式）三种自监督学习框架，在SOCOFing指纹数据集上以相同表示宽度（8特征/8量子比特）与经典基线对比，使用kNN识别评估。结果显示混合模型的增益依赖于SSL目标：对比式目标下单次实验增益明显，BYOL多随机种子下无可靠差异；硬件高效电路QNet未呈现同样增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指纹识别等生物特征应用广泛，但监督训练依赖大规模带标注注册集。自监督学习可免除标注需求，混合量子-经典模型被提出以增强表示能力。然而此前的量子SSL研究仅采用单一对比目标，无法判断所报告的性能提升究竟源于SSL目标设计，还是可归因于量子电路本身。因此需要在多种SSL目标下系统检验量子模块的实际贡献。

Method: 将QuFeX量子特征提取模块插入三种SSL框架：对比式的SimCLR、MoCo v2，以及非对比式的BYOL。每个混合模型与其经典对应模型在匹配的表示宽度（8个特征，等价于8个量子比特）下进行比较。实验在SOCOFing指纹数据集上进行，并以CIFAR-10作为对照，采用k近邻识别在编码器特征上评估。对BYOL进行多随机种子分析。此外引入硬件高效电路QNet作对照，并从电路架构、可训练参数量、非线性以及8量子比特电路的经典可模拟性等方面分析增益来源。

Result: 单次运行实验中，两种对比式目标（SimCLR、MoCo v2）下混合模型得分明显更高；而BYOL经多随机种子分析未显示可靠差异，表明增益取决于SSL目标。硬件高效电路QNet未表现出相同的性能提升。研究进一步从电路架构、可训练参数数量、非线性及8量子比特电路的经典可模拟性等角度，探讨增益能否归因于量子电路本身。

Conclusion: 量子特征提取带来的收益并非普遍存在，而依赖于所采用的SSL目标——仅在对比式目标下观察到增益，非对比式BYOL下不可靠；且电路设计（如QNet）与具体架构影响结果。由于8量子比特电路具备经典可模拟性，所观察到的增益能否真正归因于量子特性仍需审慎审视。

Abstract: Fingerprint recognition is a widely deployed biometric, but supervised training requires large labeled enrollment sets.
