Self-supervised learning (SSL) removes this requirement, and hybrid quantum-classical models have been proposed to enrich the learned representations. Prior quantum SSL studies consider a single contrastive objective, so it is unclear whether reported benefits depend on the objective or can be attributed to the quantum circuit. We insert the QuFeX quantum feature-extraction module into three SSL frameworks, the contrastive SimCLR and MoCo v2 and the non-contrastive BYOL, and compare each hybrid with its classical counterpart at matched representation width (8 features, equal to 8 qubits) on the SOCOFing fingerprint dataset, with a CIFAR-10 control, using k-nearest-neighbor identification on encoder features. In single-run experiments the hybrid scores clearly higher for both contrastive objectives, whereas for BYOL a multi-seed analysis shows no reliable difference, suggesting that any benefit depends on the SSL objective. A hardware-efficient circuit (QNet) does not show the same gain. We examine whether the gains can be attributed to the quantum circuit, considering circuit architecture, trainable parameter count, nonlinearity, and the classical simulability of 8-qubit circuits.

</details>


### [95] [Metrological Benchmarking of Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39226)
*Simone Cavazzoni,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于量子Fisher信息(QFI)的计量学基准，通过受控扰动响应刻画随机量子电路，无需计算理想输出概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机电路采样是展示量子计算优势的主要途径，但通过线性交叉熵进行噪声实现基准评测需要代价高昂地计算理想输出概率，因此需要一种更高效的基准方法。

Method: 建立基于受控扰动响应的计量学基准，用QFI表征；建立QFI与乱序关联子(OTOC)的关系，支持局域与全局控制协议；提出butterfly协议利用系统尺寸增强灵敏度；在全局白噪声模型下推导噪声QFI与理想QFI的精确关系。

Result: Haar随机电路的平均QFI在局域协议下趋近最大值，在集体控制下随量子比特数线性增长；Clifford电路尽管存在广泛的算子扩散，QFI仍为零；butterfly协议的逆灵敏度均值(单量子比特读出)与量子比特数成正比，其涨落可区分Haar与Clifford系综；白噪声模型给出QFI退化的定量参考。

Conclusion: 这些协议实现了对噪声随机量子电路的计量学基准评测，无需计算理想输出概率，并揭示了QFI可探测超越算子扩散的动力学性质。

Abstract: Random circuit sampling is a leading approach to demonstrating quantum computational advantage, but benchmarking noisy implementations through linear cross-entropy requires costly calculations of ideal output probabilities. We propose a metrological benchmark based on the response to controlled perturbations, characterized by quantum Fisher information (QFI). A relation between QFI and out-of-time-order correlators enables protocols with local or global control. For Haar-random circuits, the average QFI approaches its maximal value in the local protocol and grows linearly with the number of qubits under collective control. In contrast, Clifford circuits yield zero QFI despite extensive operator spreading, showing that the response probes dynamical properties beyond spreading alone. A butterfly protocol further uses system size to enhance sensitivity, yielding a mean inverse sensitivity proportional to the number of qubits with single-qubit readout. Its fluctuations distinguish the Haar and Clifford ensembles despite their identical mean responses. For noisy implementations, we derive an exact relation between the noisy and ideal QFI within a global white-noise model, providing a quantitative reference for the degradation of the benchmark. These protocols enable metrological benchmarking of noisy random quantum circuits without computing ideal output probabilities.

</details>


### [96] [Breaking the Bounded Entanglement Barrier for Quantum Position Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39234)
*Amit Behera,Vipul Goyal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种BB84量子位置验证（QPV）协议，在连续时间模型下可抵抗任意有限规模的LOCC对手（其拥有任意有限的量子存储与纠缠）；在离散时间模型下也能实现诚实方资源与对手资源之间任意多项式间隔的信息论安全协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典位置验证不可能；即使量子位置验证（QPV），只要对手预共享纠缠足够大（甚至指数级），总存在LOCC攻击。已有协议均要求诚实方资源不低于对手纠缠量，这一资源壁垒亟需突破。

Method: 在连续时间模型（时间为实参数，挑战消息在实区间上均匀采样发送）中构造BB84型协议；并将其实例化到离散时间模型，通过利用时间测量精度来设置挑战发送时刻的不确定性，从而限制LOCC攻击的可行性。

Result: 在连续时间模型下协议对任意有限LOCC对手（任意有限、甚至指数的量子存储与纠缠）保持安全；在离散时间模型下获得信息论QPV协议，诚实方总资源（通信与存储）可显著小于对手资源上限，且可实现任意多项式间隔。安全性间隔取决于时间测量精度。

Conclusion: 突破了QPV中诚实方资源须不低于对手纠缠的壁垒。协议资源间隔随时钟精度提升而增大：对手资源约束不断增大而诚实方资源基本不变，称之为“I sleep, you work”范式。

Abstract: Position verification, introduced by Chandran et al. (SIAM J. Computing 2014), allows verifiers to test a prover's claimed position by an interactive protocol. Classical position verification is impossible. Even for Quantum Position Verification (QPV), there always exists an LOCC (Local Operations and Classical Communication) attack if the adversary can hold an exponentially large amount of preshared entanglement.
  Somewhat surprisingly, we show that we can circumvent this barrier in the idealized continuous-time model, where time is represented by a real-valued parameter and challenge messages can be sent at a time sampled uniformly from a real interval. In this model, we give a BB84-based protocol that remains secure against any finite coalition of LOCC adversaries with arbitrary finite (possibly exponential) quantum storage and entanglement.
  Our construction can also be instantiated in the discrete-time model. Even though the previous impossibility results apply, we are able to obtain an information-theoretic QPV protocol in which the honest parties' total resources (communication and storage) can be significantly smaller than the adversarial resource bound. In fact, we can achieve any desired polynomial gap between honest parties and adversarial resources. To our knowledge, all previous protocols in the literature required resources of the honest parties to be at least as large as the adversarial entanglement.
  Interestingly, the resource gap between the honest and adversarial party resources in our protocol depends on how precisely time can be measured. As the precision of the best-known clock improves with further research, the resource gap in our protocol keeps increasing. In particular, the adversarial resource bound keeps increasing while the honest parties' resources remain largely the same. We call this the "I sleep, you work" paradigm.

</details>


### [97] [Optimal learning of covariant quantum states and channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39280)
*Satoshi Yoshida,Kazuki Okigami,Pietro M. Posta,Dmitry Grinko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究已知对称性下量子态与信道的集体层析，通过随机纯化与膨胀把学习归约为纯态估计；给出紧群表示交换态的最优拷贝复杂度Θ((Σλ m_λ²+logη⁻¹)/ε²)、G-协变信道的并行查询上界Õ(D_G/ε²)，以及置换协变信道在k个qudit上的最优查询标度Θ_d(k^{d⁴−1})与Θ_d(k^{d⁴−d²})，并构造高效量子电路近似Hayashi测量，实现门复杂度O_d(poly(k,ε⁻¹,logη⁻¹))的查询最优学习器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子层析方法一般未充分利用已知对称性，且已有最优估计的测量（如Hayashi测量）缺乏高效可实现的量子电路；本文旨在为具有已知对称群的量子态与信道设计查询/拷贝最优且电路高效的学习协议，并解决最优纯态估计测量电路构造这一开放问题。

Method: 采用随机纯化与信道膨胀，将对称约束下的估计问题归约到纯态估计；对与紧群表示交换的态按重数m_λ分析最优拷贝复杂度；对G-协变信道通过并行查询与Choi算子的实参数计数D_G刻画查询复杂度；置换协变信道结合对称子空间结构得查询标度；构造高效电路时，将对称子空间中态编码为玻色占据模式，用外差探测与归一化实现Hayashi测量，并借助量子Hermite变换在qubit上近似，再与对称兼容的纯化与膨胀电路组合。

Result: 对与紧群表示交换且Σ_λ m_λ²>1的态，最优拷贝复杂度为Θ((Σ_λ m_λ²+logη⁻¹)/ε²)；对G-协变信道，并行Õ(D_G/ε²)次查询可在固定成功概率2/3下达到diamond距离误差ε；对k个qudit上置换协变信道，在足够小固定误差与成功概率2/3下，diamond距离最优查询标度为Θ_d(k^{d⁴−1})，Choi迹距离为Θ_d(k^{d⁴−d²})；同时给出门复杂度O_d(poly(k,ε⁻¹,logη⁻¹))的查询最优、门高效学习器。

Conclusion: 该工作给出统一框架：利用随机纯化与膨胀把对称量子态/信道层析归约为纯态估计，获得紧群交换态与协变信道的最优查询/拷贝复杂度，并通过对Hayashi测量的高效电路近似解决其可实现性开放问题，从而构造出置换协变态与信道的查询最优且门高效学习算法。

Abstract: We give collective tomography protocols for quantum states and channels with known symmetries, using random purification and dilation to reduce learning to pure-state estimation. For states commuting with a compact-group representation with multiplicities $m_λ$, the optimal copy complexity is $Θ((\sum_λm_λ^2+\logη^{-1})/\varepsilon^2)$ for sufficiently small trace-distance error $\varepsilon$ and failure probability $η$ for the nontrivial case $\sum_λm_λ^2>1$. For $G$-covariant channels with finite-dimensional unitary representations of a compact group $G$, parallel $\widetilde{O}(D_G/\varepsilon^2)$ queries achieve a diamond-distance error $\varepsilon $ at fixed success probability $2/3$, where $D_G$ counts the real parameters of covariant Choi operators before imposing trace preservation. For permutation-covariant channels on $k$ qudits of fixed local dimension $d$, at sufficiently small fixed error and fixed success probability $2/3$, we obtain the optimal query scalings $Θ_d(k^{d^4-1})$ for diamond distance and $Θ_d(k^{d^4-d^2})$ for Choi trace distance. Furthermore, we resolve an open problem in quantum tomography of constructing efficient quantum circuits that approximate the Hayashi measurement for optimal pure-state estimation. We encode states in the symmetric subspace into bosonic occupation modes, realize the measurement by heterodyne detection and normalization, and approximate this procedure on qubits using the quantum Hermite transform. Combined with our symmetry-compatible purification and dilation circuits, this yields query-optimal and gate-efficient learners of permutation-covariant states and channels with gate complexity $O_d(\mathrm{poly}(k,\varepsilon^{-1},\logη^{-1}))$.

</details>


### [98] [Bounds on adiabatic path geometry from the width class of the gap profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39286)
*Mancheon Han,Sangkook Choi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用能隙宽度函数 μ_<(γ) 刻画能隙剖面（宽度类 p），改进了绝热演化路径长度 L 与总曲率 K 的上界，并给出演化时间 T 的界与紧致性证明，应用于 Grover 搜索、XXZ 自旋链和分子电子哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热演化所需时间 T 受能隙最小值和路径几何（长度 L、总曲率 K）控制，但传统估计只依赖最小能隙 Δ_*，给出 L=O(Δ_*^{-1/2})、K=O(Δ_*^{-1})，在许多实际能隙剖面下过于宽松，需要更精细的几何量来刻画。

Method: 引入能隙低于 γ 的区域宽度 μ_<(γ)。定义宽度类 p：μ_<(γ) 至少以 γ^{1/p} 速度下降。典型避免交叉属 p=1。基于宽度类重新推导 L 与 K 的界，并对演化时间采用两种调度（恒定 s 速率、恒定几何速度）进行估计。对任意二次连续可微 H(s) 证明 L 的界，对仿射 H(s) 证明 K 的界。

Result: p=1 时 L=O(√(log Δ_*^{-1}))、K=O(log Δ_*^{-1})；p>1 时幂律指数为 L 的 (p-1)/(2p)、K 的 (p-1)/p。p=1 时恒定 s 速率下 T=O(Δ_*^{-2})，恒定几何速度下 T=O(Δ_*^{-1})（至多对数修正）。证明了对所有整数 p≥1，L 关于 Δ_* 的标度紧致，以及 p=1 时 K 的标度紧致。在绝热 Grover 搜索、XXZ 自旋链、分子电子哈密顿量上验证了界。

Conclusion: 能隙宽度类 p 是刻画绝热路径几何与演化时间的有效统一框架，能显著收紧传统仅依赖最小能隙的界，并具有标度意义上的紧致性，适用于多类物理系统。

Abstract: In an adiabatic evolution, the wave function follows the path of ground states $|Φ_0(s)\rangle$ of a Hamiltonian $H(s)$ as $s$ is tuned from $0$ to $1$. The geometry of this adiabatic path is described by quantities such as its length $L=\int_0^1 \|\partial_s |Φ_0(s)\rangle\|\,ds$ and its total curvature $K$. This geometry affects the evolution time $T$. For instance, $T$ is at least of order $L/Δ_*$, where $Δ_*$ is the minimum energy gap over $s\in[0,1]$. The traditional approach gives $L=\mathcal{O}(Δ_*^{-1/2})$ and $K=\mathcal{O}(Δ_*^{-1})$, which are loose in many cases. We improve these bounds by using $μ_<(γ)$, the width of the region in $s$ on which the gap is below $γ$. The gap profile is in width class $p$ when $μ_<(γ)$ falls at least as fast as $γ^{1/p}$. A typical avoided crossing is in width class $p=1$, which gives $L=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log Δ_*^{-1}})$ and $K=\mathcal{O}(\log Δ_*^{-1})$. A profile in width class $p>1$ gives power laws with exponents $(p-1)/(2p)$ for $L$ and $(p-1)/p$ for $K$. The width class also bounds the evolution time. At $p=1$ we have $T=\mathcal{O}(Δ_*^{-2})$ with a schedule that advances $s$ at a constant rate, and $T=\mathcal{O}(Δ_*^{-1})$ up to a polylogarithmic correction with a schedule that traverses the path at a constant geometric speed. The bounds on $L$ hold for any twice continuously differentiable $H(s)$, and the bounds on $K$ for affine $H(s)$. We also prove the tightness of scaling of $L$ in $Δ_*$ for every integer $p\ge1$, and the scaling of $K$ at $p=1$. We demonstrate the bounds on the adiabatic Grover search, the XXZ spin chain, and molecular electronic Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [99] [Resource-Tunable Quantum Circuit Implementation of Nonlinear Element-Wise Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39295)
*Chunlin Yang,Yuqi Li,Hongmei Yao,Zhaobing Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种资源可调的量子框架，通过将高次多项式分解为多个低次因子的乘积，以并行度 m 控制电路深度与查询/门/辅助比特等资源的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习与量子数值计算中的非线性变换可归结为逐元素多项式逼近的实现。已有工作解决了可行性并提升了查询或辅助空间效率，但电路深度随多项式次数线性增长，成为瓶颈。

Method: 将 (2^d-1) 次多项式分解为 m 个低次因子的乘积，m 作为并行度参数在 1 到 2^d-1 间变化；通过调节 m 在查询复杂度、额外门复杂度、辅助比特数与归一化之间进行权衡；并分析多项式逼近误差与实现误差的影响。

Result: m=1 时对应二叉树实现；m=2^d-1 时可实现 O(n+d) 深度的高并行查询构造，且查询复杂度达到查询下界。框架在 sigmoid、tanh 等非线性激活函数及逐像素图像变换上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了深度与资源之间的可调折衷，突破了电路深度随次数线性增长的局限，适用于量子机器学习中的非线性变换与图像处理任务。

Abstract: Nonlinear transformations are important in quantum machine learning and other quantum numerical applications, which can be reduced to the implementation of element-wise polynomials through polynomial approximation. Existing works establish the feasibility of transformations and improve query or auxiliary-space efficiency, but circuit depth remains linear in scale with degree. In this work, we develop a resource-tunable quantum framework that decomposes a $(2^d-1)$-degree polynomial into $m$ lower-degree factors, with $m$ controlling the degree of parallelism. By varying $m$ from $1$ to $2^d-1$, the framework provides a trade-off among query complexity, additional gate complexity, ancilla count, and normalization, ranging from a binary-tree implementation to a $\mathcal{O}(n+d)$-depth construction with highly parallel oracle queries that match the query lower bound. The effects of polynomial approximation and implementation errors are further analyzed. We demonstrate the framework for nonlinear activation functions such as sigmoid and tanh, as well as pixel-wise image transformations.

</details>


### [100] [SQD-Agent: LLM-driven agentic framework for Quantum Chemistry workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39302)
*Kislaya Tiwari,Anupama Ray*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 SQD Agent，一个基于 LLM 的智能体框架，可将自然语言意图自动转化为用于量子化学的 Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) 可执行混合量子-经典工作流，降低量子应用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法与硬件快速发展，但将领域问题转化为可执行的混合量子-经典工作流存在显著壁垒：应用研究者需具备量子算法知识、量子编程细节及硬件感知的系统集成能力。与此同时，LLM 智能体在自然语言理解、复杂工作流推理及代码生成方面能力日益增强，为自动化这一转化过程提供了契机。

Method: 提出 SQD Agent，采用模块化、可扩展架构。其核心是利用 LLM 智能体解析自然语言用户意图，并翻译为基于 SQD 算法族的量子化学可执行工作流；支持异构量子后端、经典求解器与工作流组件的无缝集成；并内置交互式功能，包括按需性能剖析、瓶颈分析、资源优化、智能结果缓存与收敛可视化。

Result: 框架可支持量子化学实验、真实量子硬件上的误差缓解，以及针对候选缓解方案在潜在误差恢复行为与计算预算方面的分析，帮助用户理解实际权衡并决定后续实验策略。同时显著降低所需人工专业知识与配置开销。

Conclusion: SQD Agent 通过 LLM 驱动的自动化翻译，简化了面向量子新手的混合量子-经典实验流程，并凭借模块化架构与交互式分析能力，为快速演进的量子生态提供适应性支持。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms and quantum hardware are advancing towards a promising paradigm for scientific applications. However, translating domain-specific problems into executable hybrid quantum-classical workflows remains a significant barrier for application researchers due to the required expertise in quantum algorithms, nuances in quantum programming, and hardware-aware system integration. At the same time, AI and primarily LLM based agents are increasingly capable of interpreting natural-language intent, reasoning over complex workflows, and translating high-level objectives into executable code and building computational pipelines. In this work, we introduce SQD Agent, an LLM-based agentic framework that translates natural-language user intent into executable workflows for Quantum Chemistry applications where algorithms from the Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) family are used. By automating this translation, SQD Agent reduces the level of human expertise and configuration overhead required, thereby simplifying experimentation in hybrid quantum-classical settings for application researchers new to quantum. SQD Agent adopts a modular and extensible architecture that supports seamless integration of heterogeneous quantum backends, classical solvers, and workflow components, ensuring adaptability to rapidly evolving quantum ecosystems. The framework further incorporates interactive capabilities for on-demand profiling, bottleneck analysis, resource optimization, intelligent result caching, and convergence visualization. Key features include quantum chemistry experiments, error mitigation on real quantum hardware, together with analysis of candidate mitigation schemes in terms of their potential error-recovery behavior and computational budget, helping users understand their practical trade-offs and decide which strategies to explore in subsequent experiments.

</details>


### [101] [A Dichotomy for MIP* in the Presence of Unital Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39318)
*Yangjing Dong,Zhenyu Jiang,Minglong Qin,Penghui Yao,Haigang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文去掉了常数答案数限制，对任意多副本EPR对（unital噪声，即最大混合边缘的两比特态ψ）下的$\mathsf{MIP}^\psi[\text{poly},\text{poly}]$给出了完整二分法：若ψ可分离或$\rho_{\max}(\psi)<1$则为NEXP，若ψ纠缠且$\rho_{\max}(\psi)=1$则为RE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明噪声EPR态（$\rho_{\max}<1$）使$\mathsf{MIP}^\psi[\text{poly},O(1)]=\mathsf{NEXP}$，而$\mathsf{MIP}^{\text{EPR}}[\text{poly},O(1)]=\mathsf{RE}$；但并非所有噪声都降低量子最大相关（如dephasing后$\rho_{\max}$仍为1）。作者希望去掉常数答案限制，对一般unital噪声下的多副本情形建立完整的复杂度二分刻画。

Method: （1）在Pauli基中不直接截断正算子，而是近似合适的平方根分解，构造保正性的低次近似框架，同时达到低Pauli次数、正性以及对整个POVM的全局控制；（2）提出Pauli folding维度约简方法，其核心是随机Pauli哈希的几乎可乘性定理，使非交换乘积、正性与归一化在压缩后以可控误差保持。

Result: 证明$\mathsf{MIP}^\psi[\text{poly},\text{poly}]=\mathsf{MIP}=\mathsf{NEXP}$（当ψ可分离或$\rho_{\max}(\psi)<1$）；$\mathsf{MIP}^\psi[\text{poly},\text{poly}]=\mathsf{MIP}^\psi[\text{poly},O(1)]=\mathsf{RE}$（当ψ纠缠且$\rho_{\max}(\psi)=1$）。

Conclusion: 在多副本、多项式答案的一般设定下，量子最大相关是否为1完全决定了$\mathsf{MIP}^\psi$的复杂度类，给出完整二分法；所发展的保正低次近似框架与Pauli folding维度约简方法在量子信息与量子复杂度领域具有更广泛的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: A recent work studied quantum two-prover one-round proof systems $\mathsf{MIP}^ψ[\text{poly},O(1)]$. It showed that when $ψ$ is a noisy EPR state with quantum maximal correlation $ρ_{\max}(ψ)<1$, the resulting complexity class is equivalent to $\mathsf{NEXP}=\mathsf{MIP}$. In contrast, $\mathsf{MIP}^{\text{EPR}}[\text{poly},O(1)]=\mathsf{RE}$. Not all noise, however, decreases quantum maximal correlation. A basic example is dephasing noise: a dephased EPR pair retain quantum maximal correlation $1$. In this work, we remove the constant-answer restriction and establish a complete dichotomy for $\mathsf{MIP}^ψ[\text{poly},\text{poly}]$, where the provers share arbitrarily many copies of EPR pairs subject to unital noise. Equivalently, let $ψ$ be a fixed two-qubit state with maximally mixed marginals. We prove that \[\mathsf{MIP}^ψ[\text{poly},\text{poly}]=\mathsf{MIP} =\mathsf{NEXP},~\text{if $ψ$ is separable or $ρ_{\max}(ψ)<1$};\]\[\mathsf{MIP}^ψ[\text{poly},\text{poly}]=\mathsf{MIP}^ψ[\text{poly},O(1)]=\mathsf{RE},\text{if $ψ$ is entangled and $ρ_{\max}(ψ)=1$.}\] Beyond the complexity classification, our proof develops two techniques that may be useful more broadly in quantum information and quantum complexity. First, we introduce a positivity-preserving low-degree approximation framework for quantum measurements: instead of truncating positive operators directly in the Pauli basis, we approximate suitable square-root factorizations, simultaneously achieving low Pauli degree, positivity, and global control over an entire POVM. Second, we develop a new dimension-reduction method, called Pauli folding, for low-degree positive operators. Its key ingredient is an almost-multiplicativity theorem for randomized Pauli hashing, which allows noncommutative products, positivity, and normalization to survive compression with controlled error.

</details>


### [102] [Information Potential: A Variational Approach to Quantum Information Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39328)
*Penghui Yao,Yifan Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"信息势"（information potential）变分方法，将量子信息复杂度（QIC）与二次型联系，从而简化QIC分析并证明量子通信下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QIC是证明量子通信复杂度的有力工具且等于均摊量子通信复杂度，但因其为量子条件互信息之和而难以分析估计，亟需新的分析手段。

Method: 受量子相对熵预解表示的启发，提出"信息势"变分方法，将单条消息的QIC转化为二次型，并用QIC界定交互式量子协议中势的累计正增量，从而把势增长的下界转化为QIC和量子通信复杂度的下界。

Result: 给出两比特AND函数QIC的最优Ω(1/r)下界；r轮Set-Disjointness量子通信复杂度的最优Ω(n/r)下界，解决了FOCS 2015的公开问题；证明有限轮Set-Disjointness量子通信复杂度的直和定理；并建立非对称量子通信复杂度的近乎紧致权衡(q_A+1)(q_B+1)=Ω(n)。

Conclusion: 信息势方法有效克服了QIC难以分析的困难，不仅给出多个最优下界，还解决了公开问题并建立了新权衡关系，展示了该变分框架在量子通信复杂度中的强大适用性。

Abstract: Quantum information complexity (QIC), introduced by Touchette [Touchette, STOC 2015], has been shown to be one of the most powerful methods for proving quantum communication complexity and has also been shown to be equal to amortized quantum communication complexity. Unfortunately, QIC is generally hard to analyze because it is a sum of quantum conditional mutual information terms, which are difficult to estimate. In this work, we introduce a new variational approach, the information potential, for analyzing QIC and proving quantum communication lower bounds inspired by the resolvent representation for quantum relative entropy. This approach connects the QIC of individual messages to a quadratic form, making it more amenable and thus enables us to bound the cumulative positive increments of the potential throughout an interactive quantum protocol by its QIC. Lower bounds on the growth of the potential therefore translate into lower bounds on both QIC and quantum communication complexity.
  As an application, we give an optimal $Ω(1/r)$ lower bound on the QIC of the two-bit $\mathsf{AND}$ function as well as an optimal $Ω(n/r)$ lower bound on the quantum communication complexity of $r$-round Set-Disjointness, answering an open problem in~[Braverman, Garg, Ko, Mao, Touchette FOCS 2015]. Moreover, we further prove a direct-sum theorem for bounded-round quantum communication complexity of Set Disjointness. With the tight bound on the QIC of $\mathsf{AND}$ function, we further establish a nearly tight tradeoff for the asymmetric quantum communication complexity of $\mathsf{Set} \mathsf{Disjointness}$: $(q_A+1)(q_B+1)=Ω(n)$, where $q_A$ and $q_B$ denote the total numbers of qubits sent by Alice and Bob, respectively.

</details>


### [103] [Complete product-state contact set and optimality of the canonical three-qubit Shifts witness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39332)
*Bing-Dong Wan,Yu-Chen Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文完整刻画了三量子比特 Shifts 不可扩展乘积基（UPB）投影算符取最小乘积态期望值 λ=1−3√6/8 的全部复乘积态，证明等号集恰由八条乘积射线组成，并据此证明关联的投影算符纠缠见证满足 spanning 判据、是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前仅知道 Shifts UPB 投影算符的最小乘积态期望值为 λ_Shifts=1−3√6/8，并只给出了四个达到该值的乘积态。由于乘积射线集合是否张满 Hilbert 空间直接决定投影算符见证的最优性（即能否减去非零半正定算符而保持 block positivity），仅有四个达到态不足以作出该判断，因此需要完整确定等号集。

Method: 作者对三量子比特复乘积态做精确的相位分析，结合 Gröbner 基对所有 28 个有限实驻点进行分类，并完整处理射影边界，从而穷尽所有取最小值的乘积态。

Result: 等号集恰含八条乘积射线：两条在三个量子比特的循环置换下不变，另有两条大小为三的循环轨道。这八条接触射线张满整个三量子比特 Hilbert 空间，而先前已知的四条射线则不能。因此相应的投影算符见证满足 spanning 判据且为最优：不存在非零半正定算符可被减去而仍保持 block positivity。

Conclusion: 作为推论，对白噪声族 ρ(p)=(1−p)ρ_Shifts+pI/8（ρ_Shifts 为互补投影算符 Shifts 态），同一见证可在 p<2−3√6/4≈16.29% 时检测到纠缠。

Abstract: For the three-qubit Shifts unextendible product basis (UPB), the minimum product-state expectation value $λ_{\mathrm{Shifts}}=1-3\sqrt6/8$ of the UPB projector is known, and four attaining product states were exhibited previously. We determine the complete equality set over all complex product states and prove that it consists of exactly eight product rays: two rays invariant under cyclic permutation of the three qubits and two cyclic orbits of size three. The proof combines exact phase analysis, a Gröbner-basis classification of all 28 finite real stationary points, and a complete treatment of the projective boundaries. Crucially, the eight contact rays span the full three-qubit Hilbert space, whereas the four previously known rays do not. The complete equality classification therefore establishes that the associated projector witness satisfies the spanning criterion and is optimal in the sense that no nonzero positive semidefinite operator can be subtracted while preserving block positivity. As a corollary, for the white-noise family $ρ(p)=(1-p)ρ_{\mathrm{Shifts}}+p\mathbb I/8$, where $ρ_{\mathrm{Shifts}}$ is the complementary-projector Shifts state, the same witness detects entanglement for $p<2-3\sqrt6/4\approx16.29\%$.

</details>


### [104] [Constant-Overhead Injection into Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39345)
*Louis Golowich,Venkatesan Guruswami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了常数空间与时间开销的容错量子态注入/射出方案，并基于经典LDPC码的高维超图积构造常速率量子LDPC码，再通过内码级联实现抗均匀噪声的容错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码中如何以常数开销容错地将裸物理量子比特编码进码块（注入）以及将码态解码回裸量子比特（射出），是实现可扩展量子计算的关键操作；已有方案往往开销随规模增长，缺乏常数开销且单发（single-shot）的容错注入/射出机制。

Method: 1. 构造一族常速率量子纠错码，支持裸物理量子比特的容错注入与码态的容错射出；2. 证明在电路级局部随机噪声下，注入与射出以每个量子比特仅一个小常数的不可避免错误概率成功；3. 同时给出容错纠错与码态制备方案；4. 所有gadget均为常数电路深度（即single-shot），物理量子比特数随逻辑量子比特数线性增长，假设可并行运行多项式规模的无噪声经典电路；5. 码的构造采用经典LDPC码的高维超图积，其中经典LDPC码为Spielman线性时间可编码码（STOC'95）的简化版本；6. 因所得乘积码仅能抵抗各量子比特非均匀概率的物理错误，进一步用不同规模的内码进行级联以获得抗均匀噪声的容错性。

Result: 成功构造出首个已知的常数空间与时间开销的容错注入方案；证明注入与射出在电路级局部随机噪声下成功，且每个量子比特的损坏概率仅为不可避免的小常数；给出constant深度（single-shot）的容错纠错与码态制备gadget；物理量子比特数随逻辑量子比特数线性增长；所得乘积码可抵抗非均匀概率错误，经内码级联后可抗均匀噪声。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了常数开销的容错量子态注入与射出，并配套给出single-shot容错纠错与态制备方案。核心构造基于经典LDPC码的高维超图积（源自Spielman线性时间可编码码的简化版），并通过与内码级联将抗非均匀噪声的乘积码提升为抗均匀噪声的容错码，为可扩展容错量子计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: We construct the first known fault-tolerant scheme for injecting states into quantum error-correcting codes with constant space and time overhead. That is, we construct a family of constant-rate quantum error-correcting codes for which a set of bare physical qubits can be injected, i.e. fault-tolerantly encoded, into a code block. Similarly, a code state can be ejected, i.e. fault-tolerantly decoded, back into bare physical qubits. We show that these injection and ejection procedures succeed under circuit-level locally stochastic noise, while incurring just a small constant probability of corrupting each qubit, which is unavoidable for bare physical qubits. We also show how to perform fault-tolerant error correction and code-state preparation under locally stochastic noise. All of our gadgets can be implemented with constant quantum circuit depth (i.e. are single-shot), and with a number of physical qubits growing linearly with the number of logical qubits, assuming the ability to run polynomial-sized noiseless classical circuits on the side.
  We construct our quantum codes by taking a high-dimensional hypergraph product of classical LDPC codes, which in turn are a simplified version of Spielman's linear-time encodable codes (STOC'95). As our resulting product codes are only resilient to physical errors occurring with non-uniform probabilities across qubits, we then show how to concatenate with inner codes of various sizes to obtain fault-tolerance against uniform noise.

</details>


### [105] [Efficient Learning of Fermionic Magic States under Free-Fermion Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39372)
*Jiwon Heo,Myeongjin Shin,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知粒子数守恒自由费米子演化下费米子魔态族的高效学习，利用粒子约化密度矩阵（RDM）的谱结构分离单块与多块贡献以重构隐藏块结构，算法仅需单拷贝测量且具多项式样本与计算复杂度，并证明每块粒子数上界对应的RDM阶数是必要且充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子魔态在量子计算中具有重要资源价值，但在未知自由费米子演化下学习此类非高斯态极具挑战。作者希望探究：大量非高斯块是否能与高效状态学习兼容，并寻找一种无需先验块分解与演化知识的重构方法。

Method: 利用粒子约化密度矩阵（RDM）的谱结构，将来自单个块的贡献与涉及多个块的贡献分离，从而重构隐藏的块结构。在每块粒子数有固定上界的条件下，算法采用单拷贝测量，具有多项式样本复杂度与经典计算复杂度，可在指定保真度与高概率下恢复状态的紧凑描述；重构仅需不超过该上界的RDM。

Result: 算法在无块分解与演化先验知识的情况下实现高效学习，样本与计算复杂度均为多项式；证明该RDM阶数在一般情况下是必要的，即族中存在两个正交态在所有更低阶RDM上完全相同；表明大量非高斯块可与高效状态学习相兼容。

Conclusion: 粒子RDM阶数构成费米子魔态族在未知自由费米子演化下可学习性的充分必要条件，非高斯块数量众多并不必然妨碍高效学习。该结果将RDM谱结构与块结构重构相连接，为量子态学习与量子资源理论提供了新视角。

Abstract: We establish efficient learning for a family of fermionic magic states under unknown number-conserving free-fermion evolution. Each input block has a definite particle number and is a superposition of Fock states, with occupied mode sets disjoint both within and across blocks. The key idea is to exploit the spectral structure of particle reduced density matrices (RDMs) to separate contributions from individual blocks from those involving several blocks, allowing us to reconstruct the hidden block structure. For a fixed upper bound on the particle number per block, our algorithm uses single-copy measurements and polynomial sample and classical computational complexity to recover a compact description of the state with prescribed fidelity and high probability, without prior knowledge of the block decomposition or the evolution. RDMs up to this upper bound suffice for reconstruction. We further show that this RDM order is necessary in general: two orthogonal states in the family can have identical RDMs at every lower order. These results show that an extensive number of non-Gaussian blocks can be compatible with efficient state learning.

</details>


### [106] [Spectrally Selective Charging of an Interacting Quantum Battery via an Anharmonic Mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39393)
*Sujay Mondal,Anumita Mukhopadhyay,Siddhartha Dutta,Abhijit Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一种有限时间量子电池充电架构：被驱动的二能级工作系统通过弱非简谐三能级中介体，将非平衡资源传输给相互作用双量子比特电池，实现光谱选择性充电与循环级能量平衡闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在单一架构中同时整合非平衡资源生成、光谱选择性、相干多能级充电以及热力学循环闭合，从而推进有限时间量子电池的实用化设计。

Method: 构建由驱动二能级工作系统、弱非简谐三能级中介体和相互作用双量子比特电池组成的充电架构；利用有限时间驱动产生相干性与ergotropy，通过激发数守恒划分动力学扇区；中介体提供光谱选择性通道（下跃迁实现单激发转移，初始加载激活交叉共振双激发级联）；并进行失谐扫描与全混合态动力学分析。

Result: 下跃迁可实现近完全的单激发转移且中介体残余能量可忽略；初始中介体加载可激活通向双激发电池态的交叉共振双激发级联，但因有效三态链的集体矩阵元不等而转移效率略有下降；失谐扫描量化了光谱容差；全混合态动力学揭示出不同的能量转移与ergotropy转移分布；有限时间压缩、热重置与开关功使循环级能量平衡闭合至数值精度。

Conclusion: 该方案在单一架构内成功结合了非平衡资源生成、光谱选择性、相干多能级充电与热力学循环闭合，为有限时间量子电池的协同设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate a finite-time quantum-battery charging architecture in which a driven two-level working system transfers nonequilibrium resources to an interacting two-qubit battery through a weakly anharmonic three-level mediator. Finite-time driving generates coherence and ergotropy, while excitation-number conservation organizes the transfer into distinct dynamical sectors. The mediator then provides spectrally selective charging channels: its lower transition enables nearly complete single-excitation transfer with negligible residual mediator energy, whereas initial mediator loading activates a cross-resonant two-excitation cascade toward the doubly excited battery state. The latter exhibits a modest reduction in transfer efficiency due to the unequal collective matrix elements of the effective three-state chain. Detuning scans quantify the spectral tolerance, and the full mixed-state dynamics reveals distinct energy- and ergotropy-transfer profiles. Finite-time compression, thermal reset, and switching work close the cycle-level energy balance to numerical precision. The resulting scheme combines nonequilibrium resource generation, spectral selectivity, coherent multilevel charging, and thermodynamic cycle closure within a single architecture.

</details>


### [107] [Logical Operator Decomposition for Distance Analysis of Bivariate Bicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39395)
*Mohammad Rowshan,Simon Devitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过商环 K/S 的短正合序列给出 BB 量子码 Z-逻辑的显式基、维数公式与分量距离恒等式 d_Z=min(d_A,d_C)，证明 r_A=r_C=k/2，并借簇搜索算得六个标准 BB 码的距离 4,6,10,10,12,18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双变量自行车（BB）量子码是重要的有限长量子 LDPC 码族，但其最小距离通常依赖数值求解，无法从定义多项式直接读出，缺乏代数刻画。

Method: 在 F_2[x,y]/(x^l-1,y^m-1) 上研究 Z-逻辑商 K/S，构造短正合序列：核为 annihilator 商、余核为 colon 商；利用有限群代数的 Frobenius 结构证明维数与 r_A=r_C=k/2；基于“最小权逻辑算子的真子集具有非零 syndrome”性质设计平移锚定簇搜索。

Result: 得到显式逻辑基、维数公式及 d_Z=min(d_A,d_C)；证明所有 BB 码（含重复根情形）满足 r_A=r_C=k/2；算出六个标准 BB 码（长度 18 至 288）的距离为 4,6,10,10,12,18，并枚举全部最小权逻辑算子；普查显示仅 [[108,8,10]] 的距离在单一分量中取得（d_C=10, d_A=12）。

Conclusion: K/S 的短正合序列为 BB 码提供了纯代数的最小距离框架，澄清了逻辑类的代数分量与最轻代表支撑形状的区别，并给出可证明精确距离的高效搜索方法。

Abstract: Bivariate bicycle (BB) quantum codes are a prominent finite-length family of quantum low-density parity-check codes, but their minimum distance is usually established numerically rather than read from the defining polynomials. We study the $Z$-logical quotient $K/S$ over $\mathbb F_2[x,y]/(x^\ell-1,y^m-1)$ and show that it fits into a short exact sequence with an annihilator quotient as kernel and a colon quotient as cokernel. The sequence gives an explicit logical basis, a dimension formula, and a componentwise distance identity $d_Z=\min(d_{\mathcal A},d_{\mathcal C})$. Using the Frobenius structure of the finite group algebra, we prove $r_{\mathcal A}=r_{\mathcal C}=k/2$ for every BB code, including repeated-root cases. The algebraic component of a logical class is distinct from the support shape of its lightest representatives: an annihilator class can have a lighter two-block representative, and a colon class can have a one-sided minimum. For lower bounds we show that every proper subset of a minimum-weight logical operator has nonzero syndrome, and that this property persists inside the colon component but not inside the annihilator component. A translation-anchored cluster search built on it proves the distances $4,6,10,10,12,18$ of the six standard BB codes of lengths $18$ to $288$ and enumerates every minimum-weight logical operator. The resulting census shows that $[[108,8,10]]$ is the only one of the six whose distance is attained in a single component, with $d_{\mathcal C}=10$ and $d_{\mathcal A}=12$.

</details>


### [108] [Genuine Multipartite Nonlocality Is Fermionic Magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39411)
*Jamal Slim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出费米子宇称将自由费米子的贝尔非局域性限制为等价于一个纠缠对，且永不为真正多体非局域；真正多体非局域性恰是通用费米子计算的资源，并在IBM 48量子比特处理器上予以认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由费米子可被经典计算机模拟，而仅需一个非高斯态即可实现通用量子计算；作者希望探究这一计算能力边界是否同样对应着量子非局域性（贝尔非局域性）的边界。

Method: 以费米子量子比特为观测量，分析自由费米子态（含任意多方可共享情形）所携带的贝尔非局域性；从费米子宇称守恒出发论证其非局域性上限；并在IBM量子处理器的48个量子比特上对真正多体非局域性进行认证。

Result: 自由费米子所携带的贝尔非局域性至多等同于一对其纠缠对，无论多少方共享均如此；它们从不表现出真正的多体非局域性；费米子宇称是根本原因；真正多体非局域性无法由任何平均场态产生，并在48量子比特IBM处理器上被成功认证。

Conclusion: 费米子宇称划定了自由费米子的非局域性上限，而真正多体非局域性正是通用费米子计算的资源，无法源于任何平均场态，并已在48量子比特IBM设备上得到实验认证。

Abstract: A system of free fermions can be simulated on a classical computer, and a single non-Gaussian state makes it a universal quantum computer. We show that the same line bounds quantum nonlocality. Measured as fermionic qubits, free fermions carry the Bell nonlocality of one entangled pair, no more, however many parties share them, and are never genuinely multipartite nonlocal. Fermion parity is the reason. Genuine multipartite nonlocality is thus the resource of universal fermionic computation, cannot arise from any mean-field state, and we certify it on $48$ qubits of IBM processors.

</details>


### [109] [Parallel classical simulation of noisy shallow circuits: no quantum advantage in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39448)
*Robert Koenig,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维近邻双比特门量子电路在逐层去极化噪声下的可模拟性，给出并行采样算法，并证明含噪常深度电路可被随机AC⁰电路以常数误差模拟，从而排除相应量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果表明二维、三维含噪浅层量子电路相对经典电路具有无条件量子优势，需要判断一维情形是否同样成立，因此需要分析一维近邻含噪量子电路能否被高效经典模拟。

Method: 考虑n个量子比特排成直线、d层近邻双比特门、每层前各比特以常数概率独立去极化的电路。利用去极化噪声将一维电路切成宽度对数的独立片段，对各片段并行精确采样，并构造随机AC⁰电路模拟输入输出行为。

Result: 给出随机并行采样算法，以总变差误差δ采样输出分布，并行运行时间为2^{O(d)}loglog(n/δ)，初等实数算术运算量为n2^{O(d)}；无噪声时得到精确采样器，并行时间O(log n)。对常数深度，证明含噪电路输入输出行为可由多项式规模、常数深度、无界扇入AND/OR与NOT的随机AC⁰电路以常数误差复现，故一维含噪常深度量子电路求解的关系问题也可由随机AC⁰电路以基本相同成功概率求解。

Conclusion: 一维含噪浅层量子电路不具备同类门集合下二维、三维含噪浅层电路的无条件量子优势，其计算能力可被随机AC⁰电路在常数误差意义下匹配。

Abstract: We consider quantum circuits consisting of $d$ layers of nearest-neighbor two-qubit gates acting on $n$ qubits arranged on a line, where every qubit is independently depolarized with a constant probability before each layer. We describe a randomized parallel algorithm which samples from the output distribution of any such circuit to within total variation error $δ$, with parallel runtime $2^{O(d)}\log\log(n/δ)$ and $n 2^{O(d)}$ elementary real-arithmetic operations. Without noise, the same approach gives an exact sampler with parallel runtime $O(\log n)$. For constant depth, we further show that the input/output behavior of the noisy quantum circuit is reproduced up to a constant error by a randomized $\mathsf{AC}^0$-circuit, that is, a Boolean circuit of polynomial size and constant depth with unbounded fan-in AND and OR gates and NOT gates. Consequently, every relation problem solved by a noisy constant-depth quantum circuit in one dimension is also solved, with essentially the same success probability, by a randomized $\mathsf{AC}^0$-circuit. This rules out, for noisy circuits in one dimension, the unconditional quantum advantage established for noisy shallow circuits in two and three dimensions, where polynomial-size classical circuits over the same gate set require depth $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$. Our algorithm exploits the fact that depolarizing noise cuts a one-dimensional circuit into independent pieces of logarithmic width, each of which can be sampled exactly in parallel.

</details>


### [110] [From Wavefunction Regularity to Eigenvector Conditioning: Accuracy--Cost Trade-offs of Quantum Algorithms for Non-Hermitian Transcorrelated Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39452)
*Cheng-Lin Hong,Emiel Koridon,Paul K. Faehrmann,Thomas D. Kühne,Stefano Polla,Werner Dobrautz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究transcorrelated(TC)方法在电子波函数尖点结构下的哈密顿量性质与量子算法复杂度，通过可解模型导出能量误差渐近标度，并分析非正规算子的特征向量条件数与查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-电子库仑奇异性导致波函数尖点结构，限制了波函数正则性并减慢平滑基展开的收敛速度；TC方法虽加速收敛至完备基极限，但产生的哈密顿量非厄米、非正规，限制了标准量子算法的直接应用，且若干量子算法的复杂度显式依赖于特征向量条件数与特征值敏感性，亟需理解这些量。

Method: 分两阶段：首先研究一个可解模型，隔离transcorrelation对基收敛和特征向量条件数的影响，考察两种单参数Jastrow关联因子；随后将分析推广至二次量子化电子结构哈密顿量，并利用粒子数扇区诊断区分目标扇区与辅助扇区的条件数。

Result: 推导出渐近能量误差标度：裸投影为N^{-1/2}，两种TC关联因子在一般固定参数下均为N^{-3/2}（N为基组大小）；并证明该更快收敛可带来渐近查询复杂度上界的多项式改进。在二次量子化情形下，通过粒子数扇区诊断区分了目标与辅助扇区的条件数。

Conclusion: 本文为理解TC哈密顿量中精度-成本权衡这一核心问题提供了框架。

Abstract: The electron--electron Coulomb singularity forces cusp structure in electronic wavefunctions. This cusp structure limits wavefunction regularity and slows the convergence of expansions in smooth bases, requiring large basis sets to reach a target accuracy. The transcorrelated (TC) method incorporates this short-range behavior into the Hamiltonian, thereby improving the regularity of the transformed wavefunction and accelerating convergence toward the complete-basis-set limit. The resulting Hamiltonian is non-Hermitian and non-normal, which limits the direct applicability of standard quantum algorithms. For such non-normal operators, the complexity of several recent quantum algorithms depends explicitly on quantities related to eigenvector conditioning and eigenvalue sensitivity. Understanding these quantities is essential for assessing both algorithmic complexity and quantum resource requirements. In this work, we address these issues in two stages. We first study a solvable model, which provides a controlled setting for isolating how transcorrelation affects basis convergence and eigenvector conditioning. Within this setting, we investigate two distinct one-parameter Jastrow correlators. We derive the asymptotic energy-error scaling: $N^{-1/2}$ for the bare projection and $N^{-3/2}$ for both TC correlators at generic fixed parameters, with $N$denoting the basis size. Furthermore, we show that this faster convergence can yield a polynomial improvement in the asymptotic query-complexity upper bound. We then extend the analysis to second-quantized electronic-structure Hamiltonians and use particle-number-sector diagnostics to distinguish target-sector from auxiliary-sector conditioning. Our work provides a framework for understanding the central accuracy--cost trade-off in TC Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [111] [Generation of large-amplitude squeezed cat states with near-unity efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39482)
*Simon K. Yung,Matthew S. Winnel,Timothy C. Ralph,Jie Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种全光学方案，利用量子非破坏相互作用与光子数测量，以接近单位概率确定性制备大振幅猫态，且无需对特定光子数结果进行后选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP编码是连续变量容错量子计算的重要候选，但光子架构中高质量资源态制备困难；现有光学猫态尺寸有限且方法高度概率性，亟需高概率、大振幅猫态制备方案。

Method: 基于全光学架构，采用量子非破坏（QND）相互作用与光子数测量。协议不后选择任何特定光子数结果，所有测量结果均累加至累积光子数，重复执行直至达到设定阈值。

Result: 该方案能以接近单位概率制备大振幅猫态，并证明其对真实水平的光子损耗（光学系统主要误差来源）具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了主动高斯操作在态制备中的作用，为利用玻色子码实现高效量子纠错铺平道路。

Abstract: The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill encoding has emerged as a leading candidate for fault-tolerant quantum computation with continuous variables. For photonic architectures, the major challenge is preparing high-quality resource states, which can be deterministically synthesised from many large-amplitude cat states. Thus far, only modest-sized optical cat states have been prepared experimentally, and the implemented methods are highly probabilistic. We propose an all-optical scheme utilising quantum non-demolition interactions and photon-number measurements to prepare large-amplitude cat states with near-unity probability. Importantly, no particular photon-number outcome is postselected: all outcomes contribute to the accumulated photon number, with the protocol repeated until a required threshold is reached. We demonstrate that the scheme is robust to realistic levels of photon-loss, the dominant source of error in optical systems. Our results highlight the power of active Gaussian operations for state preparation and pave the way for efficient quantum error correction using bosonic codes.

</details>


### [112] [On quantum interactive proofs with a laconic prover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39495)
*Zihan Hu,Yupan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带有简洁证明者的两消息量子交互证明系统，定义类 ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-bit}}(2)$（$\ell$ 为证明者回复长度），并用多状态可区分性对其给出完整刻画，证明 QSD 为该类在单比特情形下的完全问题；同时给出若干塌缩到 $\sf QSZK$ 与 $\sf BQP$ 的"易"区域，并改进了 Sahai–Vadhan 的极化引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下 Goldreich、Vadhan、Wigderson 用简洁证明者（对数通信）刻画了相应可验证问题类；量子两消息类比中，单比特回复已包含 $\sf QSZK$，但若把验证者问题限制为经典公共硬币，类塌缩为 $\sf BQP$（Beigi–Shor–Watrous）。本文旨在系统研究量子公共硬币与量子消息下、按证明者回复长度分层的两消息量子交互证明，厘清 $\sf QSZK$ 之上的精细结构，并解决若干遗留的极化与塌缩问题。

Method: 1. 定义类 ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-bit}}(2)$，其中 $\ell$ 为证明者回复的比特长度，允许验证者发送量子问题。2. 建立该类与多状态可区分性（Multi-State Distinguishability）之间的完全性刻画，并将经典量子态可区分性 QSD 归约为单比特情形，从而证明 QSD 是 ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$-完全问题。3. 分析"易"区域：证明当 $a(n)-b(n)\geq 1/O(\log n)$ 时 QSD$[a,b]\in\sf QSZK$；构造从 ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-bit}}[2,c,s]$ 到 ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$ 的答案压缩归约，导出条件 $2c>(1+2^{\ell/2})s$ 下的塌缩。4. 引入量子公共硬币子类 ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[\ell]$（验证者问题为 EPR 对的一半），研究其塌缩行为。

Result: 得到三条主要结果：(1) ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-bit}}(2)$ 由多状态可区分性完全刻画，QSD 为 ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$-完全，且因 QSD 是 $\sf QSZK$-难，故 $\ell\geq 2$ 时 ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-bit}}(2)$ 位于 $\sf QSZK$ "刚好之上"。(2) 给出若干塌缩到 $\sf QSZK$ 的易区域：$a-b\geq 1/O(\log n)$ 时 QSD$[a,b]\in\sf QSZK$，以及 $2c>(1+2^{\ell/2})s$ 时相应类塌缩；改进的极化方法同时适用于 SD 与 $\sf SZK$，解决了 Sahai–Vadhan (JACM 2003) 的公开问题。(3) 量子公共硬币使交互无用：常数间隙下 ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[O(\sqrt{\log n})]\subseteq\sf BQP$。

Conclusion: 本文通过 ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-bit}}(2)$ 给出了量子两消息交互证明在简洁证明者下的精细分层：单比特回复即对应 QSD（$\sf QSZK$-难），多比特回复位于 $\sf QSZK$ 之上；在参数满足一定阈值时类塌缩到 $\sf QSZK$；而当验证者问题为量子公共硬币（EPR 对的一半）时，交互失效并塌缩到 $\sf BQP$。所提出的改进极化引理还回答了经典 $\sf SZK$/$SD$ 中的公开问题。

Abstract: Interactive proof systems with a laconic prover, studied by Goldreich, Vadhan, and Wigderson (CC, 2002), capture problems verifiable with logarithmic prover communication in the classical setting. For two-message quantum analogs, even a single-bit prover response contains quantum statistical zero-knowledge ($\sf QSZK$), introduced by Watrous (FOCS 2002). However, restricting the verifier's question to classical public coins collapses the corresponding class to $\sf BQP$, as shown by Beigi, Shor, and Watrous (ToC, 2011).
  We further study two-message quantum interactive proof systems with a laconic prover. To this end, we introduce the class ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$, where $\ell$ is the length of the prover's response, and establish:
  1. A natural complete characterization of ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ by Multi-State Distinguishability. In particular, Quantum State Distinguishability (QSD) is ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$-complete. Since QSD is $\sf QSZK$-hard, our result places ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$, for $\ell\geq 2$, in a landscape "just above" $\sf QSZK$.
  2. Easy regimes for ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ collapsing to $\sf QSZK$. We prove that QSD$[a,b]$ (and thus ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}[a,b]$) is in $\sf QSZK$ when $a(n)-b(n)\geq 1/O(\log n)$, and combine this with an answer compression from ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}[2,c,s]$ to ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$ to obtain another easy regime when $2c>(1+2^{\ell/2})s$. Remarkably, our improved polarization applies to SD and $\sf SZK$, resolving an open problem in Sahai and Vadhan (JACM, 2003).
  3. Quantum public coins also make the interaction useless: ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[O(\sqrt{\log{n}})]$ with constant gap is in $\sf BQP$, where ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[\ell]$ is a subclass of ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ in which the verifier's question is exactly halves of EPR pairs.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum double lock-in detection via sequential orthogonal quantum mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39498)
*Min Zhuang,Sijie Chen,Jiahao Huang,Chaohong Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅通过单次量子干涉测量实现量子双锁定检测的通用协议，利用顺序正交周期多脉冲序列，可从单次干涉中提取振荡信号的振幅、频率和初相位完整信息；当输入为GHZ态并施加两次基于相互作用的操作时，三个参数的测量精度均可逼近海森堡极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡信号的高精度测量是基础科学与实用技术中的普遍且关键任务，量子锁定检测是有效手段。当振荡信号初相位未知时，需用量子双锁定检测提取振幅、频率与初相位的完整信息。但传统量子双锁定检测需要两次独立的量子干涉测量，每次都要经历态制备与读出，而实际实验中态制备时间甚至长于传感时间，导致实验资源消耗大。因此，如何在仅用单次量子干涉测量的前提下仍能提取完整信息，是亟待解决的挑战。

Method: 提出一种通用协议：在顺序正交周期多脉冲序列下，仅通过单次量子干涉测量即实现量子双锁定检测。具体地，采用Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger（GHZ）态作为输入态，并在干涉过程中施加两次基于相互作用的操作，从而在单次干涉中同时编码并读出振幅、频率和初相位信息。

Result: 在该协议下，频率、振幅和初相位的测量精度均可逼近海森堡极限（Heisenberg limit）。

Conclusion: 研究为利用单次量子干涉测量振荡信号开辟了新途径，并提供了一种实现交替信号海森堡极限检测的可行方法，有助于节省实验资源并提升测量精度。

Abstract: High-precision measurement of oscillating signal is a ubiquitous issue in fundamental science and a critical task in practical technologies. In quantum metrology, quantum lock-in detection provide an efficient method for measuring such signal. In general, when the initial phase of the oscillating signal is unknown,quantum double lock-in detection can effectively extract complete information about the signal's amplitude, frequency, and initial phase. Conventional quantum double lock-in detection requires two individual quantum interferometry, each of which must undergo state preparation and readout. However, the time for state preparation and readout need not be negligible in practical experiments. In particular, the time for state preparation is longer than the time for sensing. To save experimental resources, it is challenging to achieve quantum double lock-in detection just via a single quantum interferometry while still extracting complete information about the signal's amplitude, frequency, and initial phase. Here, we present a general protocol for achieving a quantum double lock-in detection just via a single quantum interferometry under a sequential orthogonal periodic multipulse sequences. In particular, if the input state is a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state and two interaction-based operations are applied during interferometry, the measurement precisions for frequency, amplitude, and initial phase can both approach the Heisenberg limit. Our study paves a new way for measuring oscillating signals with a single quantum interferometry, and provides a feasible method for achieving Heisenberg-limited detection of alternating signals.

</details>


### [114] [Layer codes as quantum memories: syndrome extraction, thresholds and idle robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39522)
*Zhu Sun,Zhenyu Cai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对layer code进行了电路级噪声下的主动存储评估，提出了无解码器的调度器并生成电路，结果显示layer code在闲置鲁棒性上优于表面码，但代价是更高的操作频率和物理量子比特数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Layer code是三维局部CSS码，由CSS输入码与未旋转表面码片准级联而成，此前的研究仅描述了码本身，而未涉及其测量电路。本文旨在评估其在电路级噪声下作为主动存储的性能。

Method: 首先设计综合提取电路，由于连接码片的结稳定子无法借用表面码的CNOT调度，作者提出一种无解码器的调度器：先约束hook传播，再压缩深度。为四种输入码生成了十八个layer code的电路，并与同距离表面码在相同模拟与解码条件下进行比较。

Result: 在[[4,2,2]]输入码上，电路未因hook错误损失距离；相比通用CSS码调度器，逻辑错误率更低，且每轮CNOT深度约为其一半。主族电路级阈值达5.06(7)×10^{-3}。闲置鲁棒性上，同距离下layer code可容忍的综合征提取间隔为旋转表面码的1.7-3.0倍；代价是单位时间操作数多1.7-5.0倍、物理量子比特数约多五倍（均为每逻辑量子比特）。对跨平面CNOT的建模表明其速度比保真度更重要。

Conclusion: Layer code在电路级噪声下作为主动存储具有优势，特别是在闲置鲁棒性方面，但需以更高的操作频率和物理开销为代价；跨平面CNOT的速度是影响性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Layer codes are three-dimensional local CSS codes of check weight at most six, obtained by quasi-concatenating a CSS input code with unrotated surface code patches. What is established about them describes the code, not the circuit that measures it. In this work, we evaluate them as active memories under circuit-level noise. Doing so first requires a syndrome extraction circuit, and the junction stabilizers that couple the patches rule out borrowing a surface code patch's CNOT schedule. We present a decoder-free scheduler that constrains hook propagation first and compresses depth afterwards, generate circuits for eighteen layer codes across four input codes, and compare them with surface codes of the same distance, simulated and decoded in the same way. The circuits lose no distance to hook errors on [[4,2,2]]-input codes wherever a circuit-distance proof is affordable, and reach a lower logical error rate than those of a general-purpose scheduler for CSS codes at roughly half its CNOT depth per round. Our primary family built from a [[4,2,2]] input code reaches a circuit-level threshold of $5.06(7)\times 10^{-3}$. The layer code outperforms the surface code in terms of idle robustness: at the same distance it tolerates an interval between syndrome extraction rounds 1.7-3.0 times longer than the rotated surface code. This comes at the cost of 1.7-5.0 times more operations per unit time and approximately five times more physical qubits, both per logical qubit. Finally, we model the cost of the beyond-2D cross-plane CNOTs that the construction demands, and find that their speed matters far more than their fidelity.

</details>


### [115] [Rate 1/5 Non-Malleable Codes against Entangled Split-State Tampering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39589)
*Naresh Goud Boddu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了针对经典消息的高效信息论不可延展码，可抵御两个不通信的局域量子篡改操作（允许任意预共享纠缠），速率为1/5−ξ，错误为2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}，解决了纠缠两分状态模型下最坏情形经典消息的常数速率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠两分状态模型下，针对最坏情形（而非均匀随机）经典消息的不可延展码能否达到常数速率此前未解决。已有工作（Batra、Boddu、Jain）仅对均匀随机消息实现速率趋近1/5。

Method: 沿用Batra–Boddu–Jain基于置换的两分构造架构，将置换的均匀消息输入替换为"指定消息‖新鲜均匀填充"。核心贡献是针对该修改给出最坏情形安全性归约。

Result: 对任意足够小的固定ξ>0及充分大的首份长度n，码速率至少1/5−ξ，具有完美正确性，错误为2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}；安全性对每条消息成立，且对每次攻击存在单一的消息无关模拟器。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了纠缠两分状态模型下最坏情形经典消息的常数速率问题，将速率1/5的构造从均匀随机消息推广到最坏情形消息，并给出了有效的安全性归约。

Abstract: We construct efficient information-theoretic non-malleable codes for classical messages that are secure against two noncommunicating local quantum tampering operations with arbitrary pre-shared entanglement. For every sufficiently small fixed $ξ>0$ and all sufficiently large first-share lengths $n$, the codes have rate at least $1/5-ξ$, perfect correctness, and error $2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}$. Security holds for every message, with a single message-independent simulator for each attack. This resolves the constant-rate question for worst-case classical messages in the entangled two-split-state model.
  Our construction builds on the permutation-based two-split construction of Batra, Boddu, and Jain, which achieves rate approaching $1/5$ for uniformly random messages. We retain their architecture but replace the uniform message input to the permutation with a prescribed message concatenated with fresh uniform padding. Our main contribution is a worst-case security reduction for this modification.

</details>


### [116] [The Complexity of Single-Interaction Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39592)
*Elad Hecker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究单一相互作用哈密顿量(SIH)模型的复杂度,证明任意有限局部相互作用字母表都可编译为单一相互作用,从而将k-Local Hamiltonian归约到SIH_{3k+3},并给出SIH_3的QMA完全性及若干扩展结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在每项均为同一固定相互作用的无权出现这一限制下,Local Hamiltonian问题的复杂度。核心问题是:独立选择的局部矩阵与独立可调的耦合强度,是否为广泛的哈密顿量复杂度现象所必需。作者希望通过构造单一相互作用的精确编译器来回答这一问题。

Method: 主要技术工具是一个精确编译器,可将任意固定的有限局部相互作用字母表转换为单一相互作用:在指定辅助子空间上精确复现源哈密顿量,并在选定分离尺度以下以重数保持完整谱。结合有限字母表归一化,得到从k-Local Hamiltonian到SIH_{3k+3}的多项式时间通用归约(仅差已知全局能量尺度与逆多项式近似)。更精细的构造给出SIH_3与几何局域SIH_8(有界度与相互作用范围)的QMA完全性。

Result: 得到SIH_3的QMA完全性,以及有界度、有限相互作用范围的几何局域SIH_8的QMA完全性;在stoquastic情形,获得Stoq-SIH_3的StoqMA完全性与1-Pinned Stoq-SIH_4的QMA完全性。还建立了依赖于系统规模的一致相互作用下的硬度结果,并导出哈密顿量量子PCP猜想、无阻挫、指数精度和引导型Local Hamiltonian问题的单一相互作用表述。

Conclusion: 结果表明,独立选择的局部矩阵与独立可调的耦合强度,对于广泛的哈密顿量复杂度现象而言并非必要;单一固定相互作用即可承载QMA完全性、StoqMA完全性以及多种复杂性问题(如量子PCP猜想)的全部现象。

Abstract: We study the complexity of Local Hamiltonian problems under the restriction that every local term is an unweighted occurrence of the same fixed interaction. In the resulting Single-Interaction Hamiltonian (SIH) model, once the interaction is fixed, the Hamiltonian is specified entirely by its ordered interaction hypergraph.
  Our main technical tool is an exact compiler that converts any fixed finite alphabet of local interactions into a single interaction. On a designated auxiliary subspace, the compiled Hamiltonian reproduces the source Hamiltonian exactly, while the complete spectrum below a chosen separation scale is preserved with multiplicity. Combined with a finite-alphabet normalization, this gives a universal polynomial-time reduction from $k$-Local Hamiltonian to SIH$_{3k+3}$ up to a known global energy scale and inverse-polynomial approximation.
  Sharper constructions give QMA-completeness of SIH$_3$ and of geometrically local SIH$_8$ with bounded degree and interaction range. In the stoquastic setting, we obtain StoqMA-completeness of Stoq-SIH$_3$ and QMA-completeness of 1-Pinned Stoq-SIH$_4$. We also establish hardness results for uniformly system-size-dependent interactions and derive single-interaction formulations of the Hamiltonian quantum PCP conjecture, frustration-free, exponentially precise, and guided Local Hamiltonian problems. These results show that independently chosen local matrices and independently tunable coupling strengths are not necessary for a broad range of Hamiltonian-complexity phenomena.

</details>


### [117] [Thermodynamics of Ahn--Doherty--Landahl Continuous Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39597)
*Juan Garcia-Nila,Pedro B. Melo,Lucas Johns*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将基于测量的连续量子纠错建模为信息引擎，区分系统侧与控制器侧功率，并在单比特ADL协议与三比特重复码中揭示逻辑稳定与能量代价之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子纠错以连续综合征提取与实时哈密顿反馈替代离散测量与恢复操作，但其所需的热力学资源尚不明确；本文旨在量化该过程消耗的物理资源。

Method: 将基于测量的连续量子纠错形式化为信息引擎，区分反馈场与被保护系统间能量转移对应的系统侧功率，以及反馈哈密顿量对应的控制器侧功率；框架先在单比特Ahn-Doherty-Landahl（ADL）协议中建立，再推广至连续稳定子监测下的三比特重复码。

Result: 数值模拟表明，增大反馈强度可提高稳态保真度并降低条件态熵；但两部分的能量贡献在保真度开始饱和后仍持续增大。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了逻辑稳定与连续反馈所需能量资源之间的直接权衡，并与量子测量和量子Zeno稳定化中已有的能量—精度权衡密切相关。

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction (CQEC) replaces discrete syndrome measurements and recovery operations with continuous syndrome extraction and real-time Hamiltonian feedback. Here we investigate the thermodynamic resources required by this process by formulating measurement-based continuous quantum error correction as an information engine. We distinguish the system-side power associated with energy transfer between the feedback field and the protected system from the controller-side power associated with the feedback Hamiltonian. The framework is first developed for the one-qubit Ahn-Doherty-Landahl (ADL) protocol and subsequently extended to the three-qubit repetition code under continuous stabilizer monitoring. Numerical simulations show that increasing the feedback strength improves the steady-state fidelity and reduces the conditional-state entropy, while both energetic contributions continue to increase in magnitude after the fidelity begins to saturate. These results expose a direct tradeoff between logical stabilization and the energetic resources required for continuous feedback, closely related to previously established energy--precision tradeoffs in quantum measurement and quantum-Zeno stabilization.

</details>


### [118] [Warm-Start Iterative QITE for Distribution Network Reconfiguration via Branch-Exchange Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39602)
*Noah Crum,Pete Pritchard,Tanner Rase,Phillip C. Lotshaw,Shaked Regev,Claudio Girotto,Ananth Kaushik,Paul Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典算法求解配电网重构：以辐射性保持的支路交换编码结合迭代热启动量子虚时演化，最小化有功线损，在10个算例上多数达到或接近参考最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网重构是配电网上的组合优化问题，需在保持辐射状拓扑约束下最小化有功线损，传统方法在规模与约束处理上存在困难，故探索量子-经典混合求解途径。

Method: 将重构建模为组合优化；采用定宽二进制表示顺序支路交换动作的编码，保证每个寄存器输出均解码为辐射状配置；量子子程序使用迭代热启动量子虚时演化，并由基于经典求解的交流潮流（ACPF）标签拟合的代理模型提供先验信息；在给定ACPF求解次数与电路采样预算耗尽后，返回所见过的最低损耗配置；另给出两种缩减问题规模的方法以扩展到更大网络。

Result: 在10个测试算例（7个常用基准算例及3个由这些及类似标准系统修改而来的算例）上进行验证：6个算例成功达到最小损耗配置；其余4个算例所得配置损耗比报道的参考最小损耗高0.2%至2.18%。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典算法结合辐射性保持编码与热启动量子虚时演化，能有效求解配电网重构并接近或达到参考最优，且所提规模缩减方法支持向更大网络扩展。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for distribution network reconfiguration, a combinatorial optimization problem on power distribution networks, that combines a radiality preserving branch-exchange encoding with iterative warm-start quantum imaginary-time evolution to minimize active line losses. The encoding uses a fixed-width binary representation of sequential branch-exchange actions, ensuring that every register outcome decodes to a radial configuration. The quantum subroutine is informed by a surrogate model fit to classically-solved alternating current power flow (ACPF) labels. After a set budget of ACPF solves and circuit samples is exhausted, the algorithm returns the lowest-loss configuration seen. We employ our algorithm on ten test cases, including seven commonly-used benchmark cases and three cases we modified from these and similar standard systems. We include two methods to reduce problem size, which enable extensions of the algorithm to larger networks. We successfully reach minimal-loss configurations for six cases and find configurations with losses from 0.2 percent to 2.18 percent above the reported reference minimal loss for the other four.

</details>


### [119] [Weakly Measured Loops for Quantum Amplitude Amplification: Oracle Savings and Adaptive Search with Unknown Target Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39621)
*Wang Fang,Chris Heunen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类将Grover旋转与可调弱测量交替循环的量子振幅放大算法，成功测量即终止，失败则从测量后状态续跑。作者给出期望Grover迭代数的领先项：已知p时达(π/8+1/4+o(1))/√p；仅知下界p0时最优期望迭代上界为(1/(2√2))(1/√p+1/√p0)；完全未知p时以κ(t)=Θ(1/t)为临界尺度，得到闭式Gamma函数成本C(b)，数值最优b≈5.2、C(b)/2≈1.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Grover算法及其重启版本在振幅放大中期望迭代常数为π/4（或更高），缺乏可证明的oracle节省与显式控制机制。作者希望引入弱测量作为固定点控制手段，在保持Θ(1/√p)搜索尺度的同时降低期望迭代常数。

Method: 构造弱测量条件循环算法：交错执行普通Grover旋转与可调弱测量，失败后从测量后状态继续（不重启）。用连续角度分析刻画已知p的精确弱测量条件循环；对仅知下界p0的情形，建立离散Lyapunov方程求各固定测量强度的精确期望oracle成本，并推导闭式最优强度；对完全未知p，确定κ(t)=Θ(1/t)为临界尺度，重点分析κ_b(t)=min{1/2,b/t}。

Result: 已知p且状态接近π/4角度时，算法用(π/8+1/4+o(1))/√p次迭代，优于标准Grover常数π/4和优化重启Grover，并与连续角度分析的下确界吻合。仅知p0时，用由p0选出的强度，期望迭代数至多(1/(2√2))(1/√p+1/√p0)，且在承诺边界p=p0处领先常数为1/√2。完全未知p时，对每个固定b>2严格导出闭式Gamma函数C(b)，期望迭代为(C(b)/2+o(1))/√p；数值最小化得b≈5.2、C(b)/2≈1.01。

Conclusion: 弱测量保持Θ(1/√p)的搜索尺度，同时提供显式固定点控制机制并可证明地节省oracle调用；在已知、仅知下界及完全未知目标概率三种情形下均给出领先常数的最优或近似最优刻画。

Abstract: We present a family of quantum amplitude amplification algorithms that interleave ordinary Grover rotations with tunable weak measurements in loops. A successful measurement yields the target state and stops the algorithm, while after a failure, the loop resumes from the post-measurement state without restarting. We express the leading costs in expected Grover iterations as $p \to 0$. For a known target probability $p$, an exact weak measurement-conditioned loop for states near the angle $π/4$ uses $(π/8+1/4+o(1))/\sqrt{p}$ iterations, improving on the standard Grover constant $π/4$ and on optimised restart Grover, and matching the corresponding infimum in our continuous-angle analysis. When only a lower bound $0 < p_0 \leq p < 1/2$ is available, a discrete Lyapunov equation gives the exact expected oracle cost for every fixed measurement strength and a closed-form optimal strength. Using the strength selected from $p_0$, the expected number of Grover iterations is at most $\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_0}})$, where the expected cost decreases as the actual $p$ increases, and gives the leading constant $1/\sqrt{2}$ at the promise boundary $p = p_0$. For completely unknown $p$, we identify measurement strength $κ(t)=Θ(1/t)$ as the critical scale within the regular schedules considered here and analyse $κ_b(t)=\min\{1/2,b/t\}$. For each fixed $b > 2$, we rigorously derive a closed-form Gamma-function expression $C(b)$ giving $(C(b)/2+o(1))/\sqrt{p}$ expected iterations. Numerical minimisation of this explicit expression gives $b \approx 5.2$ and $C(b)/2 \approx 1.01$. The results show that weak measurements preserve the $Θ(1/\sqrt{p})$ search scale while adding an explicit fixed-point control mechanism and provable oracle savings.

</details>


### [120] [Phase-sensitive avalanche quantum sensing of sub-shot-noise fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39650)
*Nikolai D. Klimkin,Misha Ivanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用光腔中原子云在宇称禁戒跃迁条件下的雪崩式放大机制，将低于量子散粒噪声极限的光信号放大两个数量级，同时保留原始弱光波赋予原子的相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在精密测量中，探测低于量子散粒噪声极限的微弱光信号是核心挑战。传统方法受限于散粒噪声，且常规放大过程往往破坏信号所携带的相位信息，因此亟需一种既能非微扰放大又保持相位的新机制。

Method: 将原子云囚禁于光子腔中，并把腔模调谐至宇称禁戒跃迁；在连续波激光泵浦下，通过数值模拟研究原子云对腔场变化的响应，分析其产生的自发放大发射对初始亚散粒噪声涨落的放大行为。

Result: 原子云产生的发射将初始的亚散粒噪声涨落放大约两个数量级；该放大在频率上可广泛调谐，且不依赖于原子能级结构；更重要的是，该放大机制保留了原始超弱光波赋予原子的相位，相比常规方案具有质的提升。

Conclusion: 该发现对量子态表征和弱经典信号探测均具有重要意义，为超越散粒噪声极限的光波传感提供了一条可调谐且保相的新途径。

Abstract: Avalanche-based detection of small perturbations is commonplace in precision measurement devices from Geiger counters to single-photon avalanche detectors. Here, we expand this principle to sensing of light waves below the quantum shot noise limit, demonstrating numerically how atomic clouds trapped in photonic cavities can exhibit non-perturbative sensitivity to changes in the cavity field when the cavity mode is tuned to a parity-prohibited transition. We find that, when pumped by a continuous-wave laser, the atomic cloud creates emissions which amplify the initially sub-shot-noise fluctuation by two orders of magnitude. Crucially, this amplification is broadly tunable with respect to frequency and independent of atomic energy structure. Even more strikingly, our amplification mechanism preserves the phase imparted on the atoms by the original ultra-weak light wave, marking a qualitative improvement over conventional protocols. Our finding has implications for both quantum state characterization and detection of weak classical signals.

</details>


### [121] [QMA(2) with Limited Shared Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39668)
*Alex Della Schiava,Ranitha Mataraarachchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在最多对数个共享EPR对的情况下，QMA(2)的计算能力不变，即QMA(2;h)=QMA(2)（h=O(log n)），并给出EPR预算的单调性，将其推广至LOCC准备模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson等人提出的QMA(2;h)模型考察两个证明者共享EPR对时计算能力的变化，但其核心开放问题是：纠缠究竟在多大预算下才会增强QMA(2)的能力，以及对数级纠缠是否已被QMA(2)本身所涵盖。

Method: 通过使用四个非纠缠见证的模拟，结合Harrow-Montanaro等式QMA(4)=QMA(2)（FOCS 2010），证明h=O(log n)时QMA(2;h)=QMA(2)；再利用输入填充（input padding）与单调性QMA(2;h)⊆QMA(2;H)（h≤H，保持完备性与可靠性）进行推广；最后将结果扩展至证明者可用LOCC进行见证准备的变体模型。

Result: 证明QMA(2;h)=QMA(2)（h=O(log n)）；证明EPR预算的单调性并保持完备性与可靠性；由填充论证得出，若对任意固定ε>0有QMA(2;n^ε)=QMA(2)，则对所有多项式有界预算均成立，从而解决Aaronson等人的开放问题；在LOCC模型中，对数规模见证与反多项式间隔下，两模型在h=O(log n)时仍等价于非纠缠情形。

Conclusion: 对数级共享纠缠不足以增强QMA(2)的能力；EPR预算与输入填充的单调性表明，确定任一多项式（甚至n^ε）预算下的等价性即足以推出全多项式预算情形。在LOCC模型中，若将等价性扩展至任何超对数EPR预算，将蕴含NP⊆BQP，说明该扩展极可能超出当前技术能力或具有强复杂性后果。

Abstract: A $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ protocol involves two provers submitting unentangled witnesses to a polynomial-time quantum verifier. In The Power of Unentanglement (ToC, 2009), Aaronson et al. proposed $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)$, a variant of $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ in which the two provers may share $h$ EPR pairs. Our main result shows that the power of $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ remains unchanged for up to logarithmically many shared EPR pairs: $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for $h=O(\log n)$, where $n$ is the input length. The result follows from a simulation using four unentangled witnesses, combined with the Harrow-Montanaro equality $\mathsf{QMA}(4)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ (FOCS, 2010).
  We also prove monotonicity in the EPR budget: $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}(2;H)$ for $h\le H$, preserving completeness and soundness. Combined with input padding, this shows that establishing $\mathsf{QMA}(2;n^\varepsilon)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for any fixed $\varepsilon>0$ would imply equality for every polynomially bounded budget, resolving the open problem raised by Aaronson et al.
  Finally, we extend these results to a variant of the model in which the provers may use local operations and classical communication (LOCC) during witness preparation. For logarithmic-size witnesses and inverse-polynomial gaps, both models remain equivalent to their unentangled counterpart when $h=O(\log n)$. We show that extending this equivalence to any superlogarithmic EPR budget in the LOCC model would imply $\mathsf{NP}\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}$.

</details>


### [122] [Separable decompositions of 2xn states with operator Schmidt rank three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39669)
*Perrine Vantalon,Nicolas Macris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了算子Schmidt秩为3的qubit-n能级二分态可分解为rank(ρ)个纯积态或至多n+1个混积态的凸混合，后者为紧界且证明具有构造性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二分量子态分解为积态所需的最少项数（即分解长度），是量子信息中刻画纠缠结构的基础问题。已有工作主要处理纯积态分解，而混积态分解及紧界尚未充分解决。

Method: 针对算子Schmidt秩为3的qubit与n能级耦合态，构造由态显式确定的算子的酉膨胀，通过对合适酉膨胀进行特征分解来获得分解，从而给出构造性算法。

Result: 任何算子Schmidt秩为3的qubit-n能级态ρ可写成rank(ρ)个纯积态的混合，也可写成至多n+1个混积态的混合；n+1这一上界是紧的，存在无法用更少项分解的态。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了纯积态分解长度的已知结果，同时首次系统处理混积态分解；对纯积态情形给出的分解与现有构造不同，为量子态分解理论提供了新视角与工具。

Abstract: We prove that every bipartite state $ρ$ of a qubit coupled to an $n$-level system whose operator Schmidt rank equals three can be written as a mixture of $\text{rank}(ρ)$ pure product states, and as a mixture of at most $n+1$ mixed product states in general. The second result is tight in the sense that there exist states that cannot be decomposed with a smaller number of terms. Our proof gives constructive methods to obtain the decompositions. It involves eigendecompositions of suitable unitary dilations of an operator explicitly determined by the state. Our framework recovers previously known results for the length of decompositions into pure product states and is also able to deal with decompositions into mixed states. For pure product states, our decomposition is different from existing ones.

</details>


### [123] [Polylog-depth Quantum Thermal Simulation via Local Recovery Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39674)
*Hideaki Hakoshima,Atsushi Iwaki,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文给出了在任意固定空间维度下，以多对数电路深度制备非对易局域哈密顿量量子热态的充分条件，并构造了基于局域Petz恢复映射的制备电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热态的制备是量子算法与量子模拟的核心问题，但一般情况下非对易局域哈密顿量的吉布斯态难以高效制备。作者希望建立热平衡中关联结构的静态性质与热态可高效制备性之间的联系。

Method: 作者提出充分条件，结合约化密度矩阵有效相互作用的局域性与稳定性界、定量高温条件，以及可访问的粗粒化经典参考哈密顿量。利用空间局域化的Petz恢复映射构造制备电路，借助微观哈密顿量导出的量子修正提升恢复精度，同时保持经典参考的粗粒度。

Result: 当经典参考可局域生成时，经典预处理代价为N^{1+o(1)}（N为格点数，达到逆多项式全局精度）。预处理后的电路使用N·polylog(N/ε)个门与量子比特制备正则纯化态，其中ε为制备误差。

Conclusion: 该工作将热平衡关联的组织方式与量子力学允许操作下热态的高效实现这两个基本问题联系起来，赋予平衡局域性可构造的计算解释，并在量子算法设计中确立其物理基础作用。

Abstract: We establish sufficient conditions for preparing quantum thermal states of noncommuting local Hamiltonians with polylogarithmic circuit depth in arbitrary fixed spatial dimension. Our conditions combine locality and stability bounds on the effective interactions of reduced density matrices of a Gibbs state with a quantitative high-temperature condition and access to coarse classical reference Hamiltonians. When these references can be generated locally, the classical preprocessing cost is $N^{1+o(1)}$ for $N$ sites at inverse-polynomial global accuracy. We construct the preparation circuit from spatially localized Petz recovery maps. Quantum corrections derived from the microscopic Hamiltonian enable accurate recovery while the classical references remain coarse. After preprocessing, the resulting circuit prepares the canonical purification using $N\operatorname{polylog}(N/\varepsilon)$ gates and qubits, where $\varepsilon$ is the preparation error. These results link two fundamental questions: how correlations are organized in thermal equilibrium, and how efficiently the corresponding states can be realized through operations allowed by quantum mechanics. By translating static equilibrium structure into explicit preparation circuits, they give equilibrium locality a constructive computational interpretation and a physically grounded role in quantum algorithm design.

</details>


### [124] [Scaling Laws of Quantum Networks: An Entanglement Transport Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39690)
*Jaemin Kim,Petar Popovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"纠缠输运"指标与双层框架，系统刻画量子网络中纠缠分发性能随节点数N与用户数M的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向多用户量子网络架构，核心问题是纠缠分发性能如何随网络节点数N与活跃用户数M扩展；但既有研究目标、资源假设与归一化方式各异，缺乏可比性。为在多人需求下计入传输距离，需建立统一的度量与框架。

Method: 定义"纠缠输运"：将每个满足固定保真度阈值的已交付Bell对按其端点间距加权。构建双层框架：(i) 资源层，在不同聚合机制下度量每个激活时隙的容量；(ii) 物理层，在实现约束下度量每秒服务量。在匹配实现且每秒激活时隙数有界时，以渐进聚合容量给出服务上限，而可达服务需单独确立。在扩展蜂窝晶格（单一随机会话）与同质链上进行解析，并考察空间扩展下的含噪物理实现；采用固定窗口纠缠渗流构造。

Result: 扩展蜂窝晶格单一随机会话下，固定窗口纠缠渗流构造在阈值以下达到每激活时隙Θ(N^{-1})的纠缠输运，阈值以上为Θ(√N)。同质链上，渐进聚合容量超出固定窗口容量Θ(N^2/M)倍；空间扩展中含噪物理实现达到每秒Θ(M/N)的服务量。

Conclusion: 纠缠输运的标度不仅取决于连通性，还取决于资源聚合方式与物理实现；该双层框架为建立量子网络标度律提供了统一基础。

Abstract: As quantum networks move toward multi-user architectures, a central question is how the performance of entanglement distribution scales with the numbers of network nodes $N$ and active users $M$, yet existing studies use different objectives, resource assumptions, and normalizations. To account for delivery distance under multi-user demand, we define entanglement transport, which weights each delivered Bell pair meeting a fixed fidelity threshold by its endpoint separation. In a two-layer framework, (i) a resource layer measures capacity per activation slot under different aggregation regimes, and (ii) a physical layer measures service per second under implementation constraints. For matched implementations with a bounded number of activation slots per second, asymptotic-aggregation capacity sets a service ceiling, whereas achievable service must be established separately. On expanding honeycomb lattices with one random session, fixed-window entanglement-percolation constructions achieve $Θ(N^{-1})$ entanglement transport per activation slot below their thresholds and $Θ(\sqrt N)$ above them. For homogeneous chains, asymptotic aggregation exceeds fixed-window capacity by a factor $Θ(N^2/M)$, and in spatial expansion a noisy physical implementation achieves $Θ(M/N)$ service per second. These examples show that entanglement-transport scaling depends not only on connectivity but also on resource aggregation and physical implementation. The framework provides a common basis on which quantum-network scaling laws can be established.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum--classical break-even in electronic dynamics of macrocyclic molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39693)
*Shu Kanno,Kenji Sugisaki,Takashi Imamichi,Toshinari Itoko,Rei Sakuma,Hajime Nakamura,Qi Gao,Naoki Yamamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在化学动力学领域首次实现量子-经典"收支平衡"（break-even）：通过基于张量网络电路压缩的量子工作流，在IBM量子处理器上完成了12量子比特苯与120量子比特[60]轮烯的电子动力学模拟，其量子硬件墙钟时间与经典方法（Majorana传播、基于TDVP的矩阵乘积态演化）的估计时间相当，使量子硬件成为化学相关电子动力学的一种现实计算选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算在化学动力学中的实用价值需通过与经典方法在精度和计算时间上的直接对比来验证。已有电路压缩方法多局限于一维开放边界系统，难以应用到硬件原生但经典困难的周期性问题上；因此需要扩展压缩策略，并在真实量子硬件上展示可与经典高性能计算资源相媲美的时间尺度。

Method: 构建了基于张量网络电路压缩的电子动力学量子工作流，利用空间局域性将压缩从一维开放边界系统推广到周期性体系。针对一维周期哈密顿量：仅在小型空间区域内对短时演化进行经典优化，得到电路后在空间和时间上复制，从而构建全系统的长时动力学，而无需经典模拟相应的大规模演化。将该工作流应用于具有旋转周期性的环状共轭大环分子——12量子比特苯和120量子比特[60]轮烯，并将其电子结构嵌入IBM量子处理器的量子比特环路中。

Result: 对[60]轮烯，空穴掺杂非平衡动力学的QPU墙钟时间与两种经典方法（Majorana传播、基于含时变分原理的矩阵乘积态时间演化）的估计墙钟时间相当；这相当于数十至数百个计算节点（每节点36个CPU核），假设近理想并行扩展，使量子计算进入与常规并行高性能计算资源相当的时间区间。研究还展示了混合量子-经典计算在精度和计算时间两方面均为合理选择的场景。

Conclusion: 结果表明量子硬件有望成为化学相关电子动力学的一种现实计算选项，并实现了量子-经典break-even，为量子计算在化学模拟中的实用化提供了硬件层面的证据。

Abstract: Here we demonstrate quantum--classical break-even for chemical dynamics applications, reaching useful accuracy with a quantum-hardware wall-clock time comparable to that estimated for practical classical computing resources. Specifically, we construct a quantum workflow for electronic dynamics based on tensor-network circuit compression leveraging spatial locality, extending circuit compression beyond one-dimensional open-boundary systems. This enables application of the workflow to problems that are hardware-native but classically hard. The workflow targets one-dimensional periodic Hamiltonians: circuits classically optimized only for short-time evolution within a small spatial region can be replicated in both space and time to construct long-time dynamics of the full system without classically simulating the corresponding large-scale evolution. We apply the workflow to cyclic conjugated macrocycles with rotational periodicity, 12-qubit benzene and 120-qubit [60]annulene, by embedding their electronic structure onto loops of IBM quantum processors. For the annulene, the quantum-processing-unit (QPU) wall-clock times for hole-doped nonequilibrium dynamics became comparable to the estimated wall-clock time of two classical methods, Majorana propagation and matrix-product-state time evolution based on the time-dependent variational principle; this corresponds to tens to hundreds of compute nodes depending on the task (36 CPU cores per node), assuming near-ideal parallel scaling, bringing the quantum calculation into a wall-clock-time regime comparable to conventional parallel high-performance-computing resources. We also show a situation in which a hybrid quantum--classical calculation is a reasonable choice in terms of both accuracy and computational time. These results show that quantum hardware can become a realistic computational option for chemically relevant electronic dynamics.

</details>


### [126] [The Intrinsic Cost of Quantum Syndrome Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39705)
*Chia-Tung Chu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从稳定子群本身定义的、与生成元无关的内在相互作用度量，用以刻画任意稳定子码综合征提取所需的硬件交互量，并据此导出依赖于硬件连通性的精确综合征提取深度下界，同时给出常深度环形表面码反例及渐近紧致的空间-深度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 综合征提取是量子纠错的核心步骤，其所需时间受硬件连通性限制。DBT21和BFS23已建立综合征提取与量子硬件局域性约束之间的界限，但缺乏一个直接从稳定子群出发、不依赖于所选生成元的内在度量。论文旨在弥补这一缺口，并为硬件布局前的电路开销评估提供理论依据。

Method: 首先从稳定子群直接构造一个内在的相互作用度量，使其独立于描述码所用的生成集；随后利用该度量推导任意稳定子码在给定连通性图上精确综合征提取深度的硬件相关下界；并基于该度量构造环形表面码实例，实现常深度局域综合征提取。

Result: 该度量给出了硬件相关的精确综合征提取深度下界；构造的环形表面码具有常深度局域综合征提取，构成对DBT21推论1的反例；对固定的量子Tanner族，该度量加强了BFS23中明确陈述的深度界，并结合相互匹配的构造，建立了渐近紧致的空间-深度权衡。

Conclusion: 理论上，该结果将综合征提取所需信息与设备的通信容量联系起来；实践上，提供了在确定硬件布局之前评估不可避免电路成本的方法。

Abstract: How much time is fundamentally required to extract the syndrome of a stabilizer code on hardware with a given connectivity graph? Previous work by Delfosse--Beverland--Tremblay (2021) and Baspin--Fawzi--Shayeghi (2023) established bounds connecting syndrome extraction to locality constraints on quantum hardware. Here, we address this question by first formulating an intrinsic measure of the interaction required for syndrome extraction directly from the stabilizer group, independent of the generating set used to describe the code. We then show that this measure yields hardware-dependent lower bounds on exact syndrome-extraction depth for arbitrary stabilizer codes. Guided by this measure, we also construct annular surface-code examples with constant-depth local syndrome extraction that provide a counterexample to Corollary 1 of DBT21. Furthermore, for a fixed quantum Tanner family, this measure strengthens the depth bound explicitly stated in BFS23 and, together with our matching construction, establishes an asymptotically tight space--depth tradeoff. In theory, our results connect the information required by syndrome extraction to the communication capacity of a device; while in practice, they provide a way to assess unavoidable circuit costs before committing to a hardware layout.

</details>


### [127] [Complex Quantum Dynamics Versus Classical Simulability of Noisy Random Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39734)
*Anjali Waghmare,Sergii Strelchuk,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在含噪Clifford+T与近邻matchgate+SWAP线路中，动力学诊断量（magic、scrambling、anticoncentration、non-Gaussianity）与经典可模拟性可双向分离，因而噪声下测得的这些诊断量本身不构成经典难度的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势的核心前提是经典模拟量子线路的难度。magic、算子scrambling、anticoncentration、费米子非高斯性等是判断量子动力学复杂度的常用诊断量，且在纯态下部分已被严格关联到经典可模拟性。然而在噪声条件下，这些诊断量是否能可靠反映高效经典模拟的极限仍不清楚，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 作者研究了含噪的Clifford+T线路与最近邻matchgate+SWAP线路，对比动力学诊断量的行为与已知高效经典模拟算法的适用条件。通过分析Pauli谱的四阶统计量与二阶矩，解释诊断量与模拟算法之间的失配来源，并刻画局部噪声对不同阶统计量的抑制速率差异。

Result: 在含噪Clifford+T和最近邻matchgate+SWAP线路中，动力学诊断量与经典可模拟性可在两个方向上分离：诊断量可在经典模拟已变高效之后仍保持非平凡，也可在已知高效经典算法适用之前就变得平凡。失配根源在于二者探测的统计特性不同——magic与scrambling依赖Pauli谱的四阶统计量，而模拟算法主要依赖二阶矩，局部噪声以不同速率抑制这些量。

Conclusion: 在噪声量子设备上测得的动力学诊断量本身并不能作为经典难度的证据；评判量子优势须结合对经典模拟算法所依赖的低阶统计量的分析。

Abstract: Claims of quantum advantage rest on the classical hardness of simulating quantum circuits. Magic, operator scrambling, anticoncentration, and non-Gaussianity for fermionic circuits are standard diagnostics of complex quantum dynamics. For pure states, some of these have been rigorously connected to classical simulability. Whether these diagnostics reliably track the limits of efficient classical simulation under noise remains unclear. Here, we show that in noisy Clifford+$T$ and nearest-neighbour matchgate+SWAP circuits, dynamical diagnostics and classical simulability can separate in both directions. In particular, the diagnostics can remain nontrivial after classical simulation becomes efficient, or become trivial before known efficient classical algorithms apply. We trace this mismatch to the different statistical properties they probe: magic and scrambling depend on fourth-order statistics of the Pauli spectrum, whereas the simulation algorithms depend primarily on second-order moments, which local noise suppresses at different rates. Thus, dynamical diagnostics measured on a noisy quantum device do not by themselves constitute evidence for classical hardness.

</details>


### [128] [What makes a causal loop consistent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39735)
*Hippolyte Dourdent,Andreas Leitherer,Emanuel-Cristian Boghiu,Kyrylo Simonov,Ravi Kunjwal,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性经典过程在任意独立局域干预下产生有效概率（逻辑一致性）的条件，证明其等价于过程不含信号回路（允许因果回路），并给出首个基于事件、无需干预、非递归的一致性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果回路若要在任意独立局域干预下产生有效概率，必须满足逻辑一致性；既有研究提出的“无干预判据”会遗漏全局回路，需要一个更完备且不依赖干预的一致性刻画。

Method: 从确定性经典过程出发，分析逻辑一致性与信号回路的关系；将一致性与过程事件列表的“两两互斥且完备”性质相联系；并以事件集合的组合条件取代对干预的显式检验。

Result: 确定性经典过程逻辑一致当且仅当不含信号回路：循环信号被禁止，但循环因果不被禁止，从而允许不定因果序；逻辑一致性恰好等价于事件列表两两互斥且完备；原有无干预判据会遗漏全局回路，本文结果对其进行了修正；无因果序的确定性经典通信之所以一致，源于一种多体形式的 Specker 原理：若若干问题中任意两个可联合回答，则全部可联合回答。

Conclusion: 该工作将因果回路的逻辑一致性归结为事件层面的互斥与完备性，实现了无需干预、非递归的判据，并揭示了其背后对应多体 Specker 原理，从而澄清并修正了先前判据的不足。

Abstract: Causal loops escape paradoxes if they generate valid probabilities under arbitrary independent local interventions. We show that a deterministic classical process is logically consistent if and only if it contains no signaling loops: cyclic signaling is forbidden but cyclic causation is not, allowing indefinite causal order. Logical consistency is then found to hold exactly when the list of events of a process is pairwise exclusive and complete, providing the first event-based, intervention-free, non-recursive characterization of consistent causal loops.
