Abstract: We investigate the chiral separation effect (CSE) and the axial-partner susceptibility splittings within the three-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, providing two complementary aspects of axial dynamics in hot and dense magnetized matter. We compare a field-dependent scalar coupling, which leads to inverse magnetic catalysis (IMC), with a constant coupling, which exhibits magnetic catalysis (MC). In the weak-field limit, the CSE conductivity is suppressed at low temperature and approaches its chiral-limit value as the temperature increases. Its magnetic-field dependence is reversed between the IMC and MC scenarios. On the other hand, the susceptibility splittings $Δ_A = χ_{π^0} - χ_{δ^0}$ and $Δχ= χ_{π^0} -χ_{σ_l}$ are suppressed at high temperature or density, reflecting the substantial restoration of axial symmetries. Their difference develops a pronounced peak near the cross-over or first-order transition, implying that the $U(1)_A^{(0)}$ and $U(1)_A^{(3)}$ symmetries are restored at different temperatures or chemical potentials. Both the weak-field CSE and the IMC susceptibility splittings at vanishing chemical potential agree qualitatively with lattice-QCD calculations. We also provide model predictions for the susceptibility splittings at finite density.

</details>


### [318] [Schur elimination of brane-localised Higgs mass spectra, and the criteria for the $ε\to0$/$N\to\infty$ non-commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39910)
*Roberto Barceló,Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在五维标准模型扩展中，希格斯局域于额外维度上一点并与体费米子耦合；论文通过Schur补严格消去整个KK塔，将两种取极限顺序所导致的差异化为一个单和，并直接证明该和的不连续性，指出其出现只依赖两个条件：耦合足够尖锐使求和条件收敛，以及第二通道在接触点具互补行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算费米子质量谱需要边界正则化，而先求和KK塔再移除正则化与相反顺序可能给出不同答案（Barcelo:2014kha）；作者希望澄清这种顺序依赖的极限歧义何时出现、由什么机制决定，并判断该现象是否普遍存在于各类离散塔/接触相互作用构造中。

Method: 利用希格斯耦合仅通过两个重叠数到达任一KK模的局域化特征，将其视为对KK塔的线性耦合，用Schur补精确消去整座塔；由此把两种极限顺序之差的效应归结为单一求和，并对该求和的不连续性给出直接证明。

Result: 证明了该不连续性出现需同时满足两个条件：耦合足够尖锐，使求和仅为条件收敛；存在第二通道，其在接触点具有互补行为。两者均不依赖于额外维度或离散KK塔的存在；同一检验适用于普通散射中的接触相互作用、光学定理背后的虚部，以及seesaw、portal与clockwork构造。

Conclusion: 该极限顺序歧义并非五维局域希格斯模型特有，而是一个更普适的求和/正则化顺序问题；是否出现取决于上述两个条件是否满足，从而为判断各类接触相互作用与塔式构造中类似模糊性的作用提供了统一判据。

Abstract: In many 5D extensions of the Standard Model, the Higgs boson is localised to a single point along the extra dimension, where it couples to bulk fermions. Computing the fermion mass spectrum then requires a boundary regulator, and removing it before or after summing the Kaluza-Klein tower can give different answers, as shown in Ref.~\cite{Barcelo:2014kha}. Because the coupling reaches any KK mode through only two overlap numbers, the whole tower can be eliminated exactly by a Schur complement; the disagreement between the two orders of limit then reduces to a single sum, whose discontinuity we prove directly. This discontinuity needs two conditions to appear: a coupling sharp enough to leave the sum conditionally convergent, and a second channel with complementary behaviour at the point of contact. Neither condition depends on having an extra dimension, or even a discrete tower to sum. The same test applies to contact interactions in ordinary scattering, the imaginary part behind the optical theorem, and seesaw, portal, and clockwork constructions. Whether the two conditions are met determines whether a similar ambiguity plays any role.

</details>


### [319] [Electromagnetic probes of concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39939)
*Soham Banerjee,Mayank Singh,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon,Daniel Pablos,Jean-François Paquet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在协同的minijet-流体力学框架下，作者自洽地纳入逐事件初始态涨落与minijet-介质动力学相互作用，计算了直接光子和双轻子的产生，发现minijet对流体演化史的修改在电磁探针中留下可测量效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能重离子碰撞中，硬部分子（minijet）与体介质（夸克-胶子等离子体）的相互作用会影响系统时空演化。强子观测量对这类修改具有一定简并性（可通过调节剪切黏度和初始能量-动量张量归一化吸收），因此需要寻找能直接反映时空演化史的探针。电磁探针（直接光子和双轻子）在整个演化过程中持续发射，对底层时空历史敏感，有望成为区分硬部分子与QGP相互作用的有效手段。

Method: 采用协同的minijet-流体力学框架：用PYTHIA产生初始硬散射中的minijet；minijet比体介质热化更慢，可延续到流体力学阶段，并向演化中的等离子体沉积能量与动量，产生尾迹（wakes）并修改其时空演化。底层等离子体用IP-Glasma初始条件描述，配合MUSIC做流体力学演化，并用UrQMD进行强子输运。minijet与体介质同时演化，在此基础上综合计算直接光子和双轻子的产生。

Result: minijet会改变强子多重数和动量各向异性，但这种改变在很大程度上可通过调整剪切黏度和系统初始能量-动量张量的归一化来吸收。相比之下，电磁探针由于在整个演化过程中发射，对底层时空历史保持直接敏感性。计算显示，minijet对流体力力学演化的修改在光子和双轻子观测量中都产生了可测量的效应。

Conclusion: 电磁辐射是探测硬部分子与夸克-胶子等离子体之间相互作用的灵敏探针；直接光子和双轻子观测量的可测量效应确立了其作为研究minijet与QGP耦合的独特窗口的价值。

Abstract: We investigate direct photon and dilepton production in a concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics framework that consistently incorporates event-by-event initial-state fluctuations and dynamical minijet-medium interactions. Produced in initial hard scatterings, minijets thermalize more slowly than the bulk medium and persist into the hydrodynamic stage, where they deposit energy and momentum into the evolving plasma, generating wakes that modify its spacetime evolution. Minijets modify the hadronic multiplicity and momentum anisotropy in a manner that can largely be absorbed by adjusting the shear viscosity and the normalization of the system's initial energy-momentum tensor. In contrast, electromagnetic probes are emitted throughout the evolution and remain directly sensitive to the underlying spacetime history. We perform a comprehensive calculation of direct photon and dilepton production with the minijets and bulk medium evolved simultaneously, using PYTHIA to produce minijets, and describing the underlying plasma with IP-Glasma initial conditions, hydrodynamic evolution with MUSIC and hadronic transport with UrQMD. We find that the minijet-induced modification of the hydrodynamic evolution produces measurable effects in both photon and dilepton observables, establishing electromagnetic radiation as a sensitive probe of the interplay between hard partons and the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [320] [PINNing the pion: conformal deep learning for $F_π(s)$ and the $(g-2)_μ$ hadronic contribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40008)
*Mayank Goel,Subhadip Mitra,Monalisa Patra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出共形 z 平面中的物理信息神经网络（PINN），直接从第一性原理构造 π 介子电磁形状因子 F_π(s)，消除拟合模型依赖，并给出电荷半径、ρ 极点参数与缪子反常磁矩双π贡献的模型无关估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过唯象曲线拟合模型提取 π 介子电磁形状因子 F_π(s) 会引入模型依赖性、非物理赝像和运动学不一致性，需要一种不依赖拟合模型、由基本原理约束的构造方法。

Method: 在共形 z 平面上嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINN）：电荷归一化与 Schwarz 反射由构造本身保证；Cauchy-Riemann 解析性、色散关系、Watson 定理与微扰 QCD 渐近行为通过损失泛函引入，使 S 矩阵基本原理决定形状因子行为而数据仅作为约束。将割破的复平面映射到单位圆盘，以约束 Hessian 范数并防止神经正切核谱饥饿；除 e^+e^- 散射数据外，通过开关原生分离同位旋矢量形状因子，从而引入 τ 衰变数据并绕过模型依赖的同位旋破缺预修正。

Result: 网络自然地给出内部无零点的形状因子，并在解析性与色散约束下检验 ρ(770) 峰附近实验张力。获得模型无关估计：π 介子电荷半径 ⟨r_π^2⟩ = 0.435 ± 0.008_stat ± 0.007_cali fm²；第二黎曼面极点参数 m_ρ^pole = 761.72 ± 1.04 MeV、Γ_ρ^pole = 135.99 ± 1.20 MeV；双π对缪子反常磁矩的贡献 a_μ^{ππ} = (506.48 ± 2.02_stat ± 1.70_cali) × 10^{-10}。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了从第一性原理约束出发、模型无关地确定 π 介子电磁形状因子及其相关物理量的框架，同时验证了共形映射在深度网络优化中的稳定作用，并可用于检验实验数据与解析性、色散关系的一致性。

Abstract: Extracting the pion electromagnetic form factor $F_π(s)$ through phenomenological curve-fitting models introduces model dependence, unphysical artefacts, and kinematic inconsistencies. We introduce a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) embedded in a conformal $z$-plane that constructs $F_π(s)$ directly from first principles across spacelike and timelike domains: charge normalisation and Schwarz reflection are enforced by construction, while Cauchy-Riemann analyticity, dispersion relations, Watson's theorem, and perturbative QCD asymptotics enter through the loss functional. Thus, the fundamental S-matrix principles dictate the form factor's behaviour while data act as constraints. Mapping the cut complex plane onto the unit disk bounds the Hessian norm and prevents Neural Tangent Kernel spectral starvation, two known failure modes of deep-learning optimisation. Besides $e^+e^-$ scattering data, we also incorporate $τ$-decay data through a switch that isolates the pure isovector form factor natively, bypassing model-dependent isospin-breaking pre-corrections. The network organically yields an interior zero-free form factor, while the framework tests experimental tensions around the $ρ(770)$ peak against analyticity and dispersion constraints. We obtain model-independent estimates of the pion charge radius, $\langle r_π^2 \rangle = 0.435 \pm 0.008_{\text{stat}} \pm 0.007_{\text{cali}}$ fm$^2$, the second-sheet pole parameters, $m_ρ^{\text{pole}} = 761.72\pm 1.04$ MeV and $Γ_ρ^{\text{pole}} = 135.99 \pm 1.20$ MeV, and the two-pion contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment, $a_μ^{ππ} = (506.48 \pm 2.02_{\text{stat}} \pm 1.70_{\text{cali}}) \times 10^{-10}$.

</details>


### [321] [Production Shape Effects Beyond Rate Rescaling in Dark Photon Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40052)
*X. Zhong,H. Y. Qiu,X. Q. Shen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文定量检验了长寿命粒子搜寻中生产模型不确定性对实验接受度的影响，通过归一化比较暗光子轫致辐射的两种谱描述，发现NA62束流收集几何下接受度存在显著偏移（δ_NF>0.3），表明速率因子化假设不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命粒子搜寻中，生产模型不确定性不仅影响总产额，还会改变实验接受度所采样的运动学分布。现有做法常假设速率因子化（即接受度与生产模型无关），需检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 在共同物理区间内对谱进行归一化后，比较改进的Weizsäcker-Williams描述与有效准实（quasireal）近似对质子轫致辐射产生暗光子的处理。固定该区间内的产额，用接受度差异直接度量对速率因子化的偏离。在NA62束流收集几何中评估接受度偏移δ_NF，并考察不同有效QRA离壳标度（1.0、1.5、2.0 GeV）与孔径变化；同时分析公开NA62全选择接受度数据中Mixing与Bremsstrahlung两类产生的双μ子接受度比。

Result: 在NA62束流收集几何中，所有数值收敛网格点上中心计算给出δ_NF>0.3（在展示的NA62灵敏度重叠区内），且结果对QRA离壳标度及孔径变化稳健。公开NA62全选择接受度数据显示，Mixing与Bremsstrahlung两类的双μ子接受度比非常数，范围约0.58至4.23，对1的中位绝对偏离约0.79。

Conclusion: 投影判据为速率因子化提供了定量检验：归一化后的生产形状变化在接受度响应上的投影必须在所需精度下可忽略。若该条件不满足，则生产模型依赖必须通过接受度计算传播，或被当作接受度不确定度处理。

Abstract: Production model uncertainties in long-lived particle searches can affect both the inclusive yield and the kinematic distributions sampled by the experimental acceptance. We isolate the latter effect by comparing the modified Weizsäcker--Williams description with the effective quasireal approximation for dark photon production via proton bremsstrahlung after normalizing both spectra on a common physical domain. At fixed production yield within this domain, the resulting acceptance difference directly measures the departure from rate factorization. In the NA62 beam dump geometry, the central calculation gives a fractional acceptance shift $δ_{\rm NF}>0.3$ at every numerically converged grid point within the displayed NA62 sensitivity overlap. This conclusion persists for effective QRA off-shell scales of 1.0, 1.5, and 2.0~GeV and under the aperture variations considered. Separately, the public NA62 full selection acceptances give a nonconstant dimuon acceptance ratio between the Mixing and Bremsstrahlung production categories, ranging from approximately 0.58 to 4.23, with a median absolute departure from unity of approximately 0.79 over the analyzed HEPData grid. This provides complementary evidence that the published full selection acceptance depends on the production category. More generally, the projection criterion provides a quantitative test of rate factorization. The normalized production shape variation must have negligible projection onto the acceptance response at the required accuracy. When this condition is not satisfied, production model dependence must be propagated through the acceptance calculation or treated as an acceptance uncertainty.

</details>


### [322] [New Physics in $ b \to s τ^+τ^-$ Processes and Correlations with $R(D^{(*)})$ and $B\to K^{(*)}ν\barν$: An (SM)EFT Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40072)
*Guillermo Baltà,Andreas Crivellin,Joaquim Matias,Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新评估并更新了b→sτ+τ-跃迁的预言，基于数据驱动方法处理ψ(2S)共振，给出全运动学范围的B→K(*)τ+τ-和Bs→φτ+τ-分支比，并进行了TeV能标下维度6 SMEFT分析，指出当前数据偏好区域可使分支比增强数个量级，且未来LHCb、CMS、Belle II将探测相关参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: b→sτ+τ-是探测与第三代夸克和轻子相关新物理的敏感途径。R(D(*))持续异常及B+→K+νν̄速率增强的迹象，促使作者重新审视并更新b→sτ+τ-过程的预言。

Method: 在先前数据驱动方法基础上纳入主导的ψ(2S)共振，给出B→K(*)τ+τ-与Bs→φτ+τ-在全运动学范围的预言；在弱有效理论中提供包含新物理贡献的分支比半解析表达式；并进行维度6 SMEFT的全面分析，新物理实现于TeV能标。

Result: 识别出当前数据偏好的参数空间区域，其中b→sτ+τ-过程的分支比可增强数个量级；当前实验灵敏度仍远高于SM速率，但现有味物理数据暗示的增强意味着LHCb、CMS和Belle II的将来限制将探测相关SMEFT参数空间的重要区域；不同情景可给出相同的B→K(*)νν̄分支比预言，但可通过测量B→K(*)τ+τ-、Bs→τ+τ-和Bs→φτ+τ-加以区分。

Conclusion: b→sτ+τ-过程是探测新物理的有效探针，当前味物理数据暗示的分支比增强可由未来LHCb、CMS和Belle II实验检验，且结合多个末态测量可区分不同SMEFT情景。

Abstract: Tauonic flavour-changing neutral-current transitions ($b\to sτ^+τ^-$) are sensitive probes of New Physics connected to third-generation quarks and leptons. Motivated by the persistent anomalies in $R(D^{(*)})$ and the indications of enhanced $B^+\to K^{+}ν\barν$ rates, we revisit and update the predictions for $b\to sτ^+τ^-$ processes. Building upon a previous work on a data-driven approach to include the dominant $ψ(2S)$ resonances, we provide predictions for $B\to K^{(*)}τ^+τ^-$ ($B_s\toφτ^+τ^-$) over the full kinematic range. To include possible New Physics contributions within the Weak Effective Theory, we provide semi-analytic expressions for the corresponding branching ratios. We then perform a comprehensive dimension-6 SMEFT analysis of New Physics realised at the TeV scale. We identify regions of parameter space preferred by current data where the branching ratios of $b\to sτ^+τ^-$ processes can be enhanced by several orders of magnitude. While current experimental sensitivities remain far above the SM rates, the enhancements suggested by present flavour data imply that forthcoming limits from LHCb, CMS, and Belle~II will probe significant regions of the relevant SMEFT parameter space. Furthermore, we highlight that while different scenarios can lead to the same predictions for $B\to K^{(*)}ν\barν$ branching ratios, they can be disentangled by measuring $B\to K^{(*)}τ^+τ^-$, $B_s\toτ^+τ^-$ and $B_s\toφτ^+τ^-$.

</details>


### [323] [Estimating High-Energy Neutrino Effective Areas from Muon Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40188)
*Stephan A. Meighen-Berger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解析计算方法，用于描述中微子望远镜中μ子在探测阈值以上的传播距离及其能量损失的统计特性，精度与蒙特卡洛模拟相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子望远镜通过带电电流相互作用产生的μ子来观测中微子，多数μ子产生于探测器外数公里处，其能量损失具有随机性，传统上依赖蒙特卡洛模拟，计算成本高且不便于跨探测器快速比较。

Method: 解析计算μ子落到探测阈值以下之前所传播的距离，同时刻画其能量损失（随机性）的统计性质；模型对每个探测器仅用两个参数来表征响应。

Result: 该解析结果与蒙特卡洛传播结果的偏差在3%以内；每个探测器用两个参数即可与IceCube、KM3NeT/ARCA和P-ONE公布的有效面积匹配到约1%的精度。

Conclusion: 该模型可用于点源灵敏度、能量重建以及探测器间的一致性检验；由于计算将μ子输运物理与探测器响应解耦，因此可快速对新探测器或新损失模型进行基准评估。

Abstract: Neutrino telescopes observe neutrinos through the muons produced by charged-current interactions. Most of those muons are born kilometers outside the detector, and how much energy they lose on the way is stochastic. We show that we can compute analytically the distance a muon travels before it falls below the detection threshold, while describing the statistical properties of its losses. Our result reproduces Monte Carlo propagation to within $3\%$, and with two parameters per detector it matches the effective areas published by IceCube, KM3NeT/ARCA, and P-ONE to about $1\%$. We then show how this model can be used in point-source sensitivities, energy reconstruction, and consistency checks between detectors. The calculation disentangles muon-transport physics from a detector's response, so a new detector or loss model can be quickly benchmarked.

</details>


### [324] [In medium evolution of the energy-energy correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40224)
*Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将两点能量-能量关联函数（EEC）的标度演化拓展到QCD介质中传播的夸克与胶子喷注，基于含Glauber胶子的软共线有效理论建立介质内EEC的因子化描述，并在不透明度展开下计算单圈介质修正的喷注函数、完成大对数领头对数重求和，给出介质效应直接体现于反常维度的可测量运动学区间，并与p-Pb碰撞的EEC测量进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEC是检验基本碰撞中QCD的高精度喷注子结构观测量，但此前主要应用于基本碰撞，其在核反应中的介质效应尚缺乏系统的第一性原理理论描述；作者希望把EEC框架拓展到介质环境，以获得对介质内动力学的清晰、可检验探针。

Method: 采用第一性原理分析；使用含Glauber胶子相互作用的软共线有效理论（SCET_G）推导介质内EEC的因子化公式；在不透明度展开下计算单圈介质修正的夸克与胶子喷注函数；并对大标度对数进行领头对数重求和；最后将解析预言与p-Pb碰撞中的EEC测量进行对比。

Result: 得到了介质修正的夸克与胶子喷注函数的单圈结果及领头对数重求和；识别出一个实验上可及的运动学区间，在该区间内介质效应直接表现为对反常维度的介质诱导修正；并将理论预言与p-Pb碰撞的EEC测量进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了介质内EEC的因子化与重求和组织框架，表明EEC可作为透明探测介质内动力学的观测量；文章还讨论了其对小碰撞系统（O-O）及EIC的相关意义。

Abstract: In this work, we present a first-principles analysis of the scale evolution of the two-point energy-energy correlator (EEC) for quark and gluon jets propagating through QCD matter. The EEC is a jet substructure observable that encodes the angular distribution of energy flow within jets and has proven valuable for precision tests of QCD in elementary collisions. Extending this framework to reactions with nuclei, we derive a factorized description of the in-medium EEC using Soft Collinear Effective Theory with Glauber gluon interactions, allowing systematic inclusion of medium-induced interactions. Working in the opacity expansion, we compute the medium-modified quark and gluon jet functions at one loop and perform leading-logarithmic resummation of large-scale logarithms. We find an experimentally accessible kinematic regime where medium effects manifest directly through medium-induced corrections to the anomalous dimensions, providing a transparent probe of in-medium dynamics. We test the theoretical framework by comparing our analytic predictions with EEC measurements in p-Pb collisions and discuss its implications for small collision systems (O-O) and the EIC.

</details>


### [325] [$\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ corrections to the Top Quark mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40229)
*Daniele Gaggero,Ciaran Williams*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了顶夸克质量的三圈 \({\cal O}(\alpha\alpha_s^2)\) 混合 QCD-EW 修正，给出极点质量 \(M_t\) 与 \(\overline{\rm MS}\) 质量 \(m_t(\mu)\) 在该阶的关系，并讨论了含椭圆曲线周期的微分形式对物理结果的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克质量的精确确定对电弱精密测量和理论预言至关重要；极点质量与 \(\overline{\rm MS}\) 质量之间的转换关系需要在更高阶微扰精度下完善，而三圈混合 QCD-EW 修正是当前精度提升的重要目标。

Method: 将三圈自能表示为三圈 master integrals（MIs）的函数；选取适当的 MIs 使其满足 \(\varepsilon\)-factorized 形式的微分方程，从而将解表达为 iterated integrals；分析所遇到的微分形式类型，包括椭圆曲线的 periods。

Result: 得到了 \(\mathcal{O}(\alpha\alpha_s^2)\) 阶极点质量与 \(\overline{\rm MS}\) 质量的关系，并计算了该阶引起的 \(m_t(M_t)\) 移动。对于 \(M_t = 172.3\) GeV，移动量为 \(-216\) MeV，与当前引用的实验不确定度相当。

Conclusion: 三圈混合 QCD-EW 修正对顶夸克质量转换关系有不可忽略的贡献，其大小可与当前实验精度相比，因此在未来高精度顶夸克质量分析中应予以纳入。

Abstract: We calculate the three-loop $\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ mixed QCD-EW corrections to the top quark mass and obtain the relationships between the pole ($M_t$) and $\overline{\rm{MS}}$ ($m_t(μ)$) masses at this order. In performing our calculation, we express the three-loop self-energies as a function of three-loop master integrals (MIs). These MIs are defined so as to solve a differential equation in $ε$-factorized form, allowing for their solution to be expressed as iterated integrals. We discuss the types of differential forms that we encounter, which include periods of elliptic curves, and their role in the final physical result. Using our results, we calculate the shift in $m_t(M_t)$ arising at this order. For $M_t = 172.3$ GeV, the shift is -216 MeV, which is comparable to the currently quoted experimental uncertainty.

</details>


### [326] [Interpreting the High-Recoil LUX-ZEPLIN Event with Bino-/Singlino-like and Higgsino Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40234)
*Subhojit Roy,Pedro Schwaller,Carlos E. M. Wagner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LZ实验报告的约248 keV核反冲候选事件，研究了基于中性ino与Z玻色子不同耦合的两种超对称解释：弹性散射情形（bino/单态ino通过依赖于Higgsino不对称度X的对角轴矢Z耦合散射）和非弹性散射情形（赝狄拉克Higgsino通过几百keV能隙进行吸热散射）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验最近报告了一个能量约248 keV的核反冲候选事件，需要从理论模型角度对其进行解释。作者希望探讨超对称框架下中性ino暗物质能否通过不同的Z玻色子耦合机制来解释这一候选信号，并考察其与现有实验约束（如LZ上限、太阳中微子搜寻、希格斯质量）的相容性。

Method: 作者构建了两种超对称解释。弹性情形下，bino或单态ino型中性ino通过与Higgsino不对称度X=|N13|²−|N14|²成正比的对角轴矢Z耦合进行散射，并利用自旋相关盲点与自旋无关盲点不重合的特性，在保持X∼10⁻²的同时抑制希格斯介导的散射；在MSSM中这倾向于低tanβ，与观测到的希格斯质量存在张力，而NMSSM的树级四重耦合可缓解该张力。作者给出一个具体的NMSSM基准点（m_χ≈500 GeV，X≈0.007），其丰度来自略高于阈值的单态型赝标量共振，同时抑制现今湮灭。非弹性情形下，赝狄拉克Higgsino通过几百keV的能隙进行吸热散射，该能隙由PeV能标的gaugino产生，可通过反号bino-wino抵消或微弱单态ino混合降低。

Result: 弹性情形下，NMSSM基准点m_χ≈500 GeV、X≈0.007，低于当前LZ限制，其遗迹丰度由略高于阈值的单态型赝标量共振获得，并抑制现今湮灭。MSSM低tanβ解释与几TeV标量夸克谱下的希格斯质量存在张力，NMSSM可缓解。非弹性情形下，最小的热学实现受到太阳中微子搜寻的强约束。两种机制在候选事件能量之外给出不同的反冲谱。

Conclusion: 两种机制——弹性散射（bino/单态ino通过轴矢Z耦合）与非弹性散射（赝狄拉克Higgsino吸热散射）——都能为LZ候选事件提供超对称解释，且在远离候选能量处预测不同的反冲谱，可作为区分两种解释的实验判据。弹性NMSSM方案可通过单态型赝标量共振满足丰度并规避LZ限制，而非弹性方案的最小热学实现受太阳中微子约束较强。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R\simeq248$ keV. We study two supersymmetric interpretations based on distinct neutralino couplings to the $Z$ boson. In the elastic case, a bino- or singlino-like neutralino scatters through a diagonal axial $Z$ coupling set by the Higgsino asymmetry $X=|N_{13}|^2-|N_{14}|^2$ rather than by the total Higgsino fraction. Since the spin-dependent blind spot does not coincide with the spin-independent one, Higgs-mediated scattering can be suppressed while retaining $X\sim10^{-2}$. In the MSSM this favors low $\tanβ$, in tension with the observed Higgs mass for a few-TeV squark spectrum, a tension relieved by the tree-level quartic of the NMSSM. An explicit NMSSM benchmark with $m_χ\simeq500$ GeV and $X\simeq0.007$, below current LZ limits, obtains its relic abundance from a singlet-like pseudoscalar resonance just above threshold, which also suppresses present-day annihilation. In the inelastic case, a pseudo-Dirac Higgsino scatters endothermically, with a splitting of a few hundred keV generated by gauginos at the PeV scale which can be lowered by opposite-sign bino-wino cancellation, or by weak singlino mixing; the minimal thermal realization is strongly constrained by solar-neutrino searches. The two mechanisms predict distinct recoil spectra away from the candidate.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Dear Quantizers, Stochastic $\approx$ Brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38209)
*Justin Kulp*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将随机量子化表述为$(d+1)$维Langevin系统的平衡极限，借助随机系统的超对称表述引入Morse理论研究Langevin动力学，把随机量子化解释为通过体域上同调拓扑平衡TFT（EqmTFT）对相对QFT的绝对化，并将其丰富的Neumann边界数据与$d$维BV/BRST复形相联系；解析延拓揭示了与A模型及非相对论、Carrollian极限的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子化与拓扑场论、边界数据之间缺乏明确的统一结构描述，作者希望借助超对称与Morse理论为Langevin动力学提供拓扑/上同调层面的组织框架，并将其边界条件与BV/BRST形式化对接。

Method: 采用随机系统的超对称表述以应用Morse理论研究Langevin动力学；将随机量子化解释为体域共上同调拓扑平衡TFT（EqmTFT）对相对QFT的绝对化；分析其Neumann边界数据与$d$维BV/BRST复形的关系，并进行解析延拓。

Result: 建立了随机量子化与EqmTFT、BV/BRST复形以及Neumann边界数据之间的对应关系；通过解析延拓发现与A模型以及非相对论极限、Carrollian极限的联系。

Conclusion: 随机量子化可由体域拓扑平衡场论统一刻画，其边界自由度自然编码$d$维BV/BRST结构，并通向A模型及非相对论/Carrollian极限，为相关QFT结构提供了新的上同调视角。

Abstract: Stochastic quantization formulates $d$-dimensional Euclidean QFT as the equilibrium limit of a $(d+1)$-dimensional Langevin system. Using the supersymmetric formulation of stochastic systems, we apply techniques from Morse theory to study Langevin dynamics. We interpret stochastic quantization as the absolutization of a relative QFT by a bulk cohomological topological Equilibrium TFT ("EqmTFT"), and relate its enriched Neumann boundary data to the $d$-dimensional BV/BRST complex. Analytic continuation reveals connections to the A-model and non-relativistic and Carrollian limits.

</details>


### [328] [Construction of mirror pairs of Berglund-Hubsch Calabi-Yau orbifolds of loop-type polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38273)
*Maxim Malyutin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了构建BHK型Calabi-Yau流形及其镜像对的orbifold构造算法，针对loop型多项式进行了完整枚举，获得216个满足Calabi-Yau条件的唯一多项式，并从中发现两个具有三代费米子（N_gen=3）的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦论紧致化中，构建具有现实粒子物理现象学意义的模型需要Calabi-Yau流形具有恰好三代的夸克和轻子。BHK型orbifold构造提供了一类可系统枚举的候选流形，因此需要发展相应算法并系统筛选满足三代条件的构型。

Method: 提出用于loop型多项式的BHK型Calabi-Yau流形orbifold及其镜像对的构造算法；完整枚举满足Calabi-Yau条件的loop多项式；对每个多项式计算Euler示性数、代数（generations）数和单态（singlets）数；利用Roan的广义组合构造分析twisted sector态。

Result: 共枚举出216个满足Calabi-Yau条件的唯一loop多项式；对所有构型计算了Euler示性数、代数与单态数；确认所有研究的构型中均不存在Roan对，表明在解orbifold奇点时twisted sector对Hodge数差值的贡献为零；发现两个对角指数为{74, 3, 3, 5, 3}和{74, 3, 5, 3, 3}的构型，其夸克与轻子代数恰好为三（N_gen=3）。

Conclusion: 该工作为BHK型loop多项式的orbifold构造提供了系统方法，并识别出两个可实现三代费米子的构型，这对构建现象学上现实的粒子物理模型具有关键意义；同时Roan对的缺失简化了奇点解消时Hodge数的计算。

Abstract: In this paper, an algorithm for constructing orbifolds of Calabi-Yau manifolds of the Berglund-Hubsch-Krawitz (BHK) type and their mirror pairs for loop-type polynomials is proposed and applied. A complete enumeration of loop polynomials satisfying the Calabi-Yau condition was carried out, yielding 216 unique polynomials. For each polynomial, the Euler characteristics, the numbers of generations and singlets were computed, and the twisted sector states were analyzed using Roan's generalized combinatorial construction. It was established that Roan pairs are absent for all studied configurations, indicating a zero contribution of the twisted sectors to the difference in Hodge numbers upon resolving the orbifold singularities. The primary physical result of this work is the discovery of two configurations with diagonal exponents $\{74, 3, 3, 5, 3\}$ and $\{74, 3, 5, 3, 3\}$, for which the number of quark and lepton generations is precisely three ($N_{\text{gen}} = 3$), which is of key interest for constructing phenomenologically realistic models of particle physics.

</details>


### [329] [Detectors in Nonrelativistic Conformal Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38301)
*Cyuan-Han Chang,Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Ian Moult,Dam Thanh Son*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将探测器算符框架拓展至非相对论共形场论，定义速度微分能量通量算符，计算多类态的单点函数，并导出非相对论共形对撞机束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论QFT中探测器算符形式化已取得成功；作者希望将这一思路推广到非相对论共形场论（NRCFT），以研究其渐近通量关联。

Method: 在NRCFT中定义速度微分能量通量探测器算符E_v(n̂)，给出非微扰定义并研究其对称性与代数；计算自由费米子、幺正性极限费米子等少体与多体态的单点函数；通过论证E_v(n̂)正定性并在自旋1态上施加该条件，推导Hofman-Maldacena共形对撞机束缚的非相对论推广。

Result: 在相互作用三体态中，单点函数呈现反映Efimov三体波函数的丰富行为，并因共振相互作用形成增强的“喷注”结构；得到非相对论版共形对撞机束缚。

Conclusion: 探测器算符可用于研究NRCFT的渐近通量结构，并可望在冷原子到低能中子等实验平台上测量。

Abstract: Correlations in asymptotic fluxes provide one of the primary experimental means of studying physical systems. In the context of relativistic QFT, the formalization of such experiments in terms of detector operators has led to significant progress. Motivated by these successes, in this paper we initiate a study of detector operators in nonrelativistic conformal field theories (NRCFTs). In NRCFTs, fluxes are distributed in both angle and velocity, making the natural detector operator the energy flux differential in velocity, $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$. We provide non-perturbative definitions of these operators in NRCFTs, and study their symmetries and algebra. We calculate one-point functions of these detector observables in a variety of few and many body states of representative NRCFTs, including free fermions, as well as fermions at unitarity. In interacting three-body states, they exhibit a remarkably rich behavior reflective of the Efimov three-body wave-function, including the formation of an enhanced "jet" structure due to the presence of resonant interactions. We use these detectors to derive a nonrelativistic generalization of the Hofman-Maldacena conformal collider bounds by arguing for positivity of $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$, and imposing this in spin 1 states. We outline a number of future directions for the study of detectors in NRCFTs, and discuss how they can be measured in a variety of experimental platforms from cold atoms to low energy neutron experiments.

</details>


### [330] [Energy Transmission Across Holographic Conformal Interfaces in General Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38318)
*Igal Arav,Theodore Bertrand,Shira Chapman,Giuseppe Policastro,Sebastian Waeber*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用引力全息(holography)研究高维共形界面(conformal interface)的能量输运，对一大类AdS$_d$-切片domain-wall几何推导出以bulk warp factor表示的透射系数闭式公式，并证明透射与入射角及扰动剖面无关，同时给出由类中心荷量控制的透射界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维情形下共形界面能量输运具有普适性，但高维对应问题基本未被探索。在高维中散射可依赖入射角等额外运动学数据，且共形对称性的限制大大减弱；同时目前尚无基于CFT的高维共形界面结果，因此需要新方法给出首批明确结论。

Method: 采用引力全息(holographic)方法。针对一大类AdS$_d$-切片的domain-wall几何，通过bulk warp factor得到能量透射系数的闭式表达式；该方法可退化复现已知二维全息结果并推广至任意维度。

Result: 得到任意维度下能量透射系数的闭式公式，复现并推广二维全息结果；证明透射与入射角无关、与入射扰动剖面无关；并推导出由刻画界面两侧理论自由度数的类中心荷量所控制的透射界。这是高维共形界面反射与透射的首个明确结果。

Conclusion: 高维共形界面能量输运在一类domain-wall几何中呈现普适性，透射系数由bulk warp factor完全决定，为尚无CFT结果的高维共形界面问题提供了首个全息基准。

Abstract: Energy transport across conformal interfaces is universal in two spacetime dimensions, but its higher-dimensional counterpart has remained largely unexplored. In higher dimensions, scattering can depend on additional kinematic data, such as the angle of incidence, and conformal symmetry is far less restrictive. We use gravitational holography to study this problem. For a broad class of AdS$_d$-sliced domain-wall geometries, we derive a closed-form expression for the energy transmission coefficient in terms of the bulk warp factor. Our formula reproduces known two-dimensional holographic results and extends them to arbitrary dimension. Within this class, we establish different aspects of higher-dimensional universality. In particular, we show that the transmission is independent of the incidence angle and of the profile of the incident perturbation. We also derive bounds on transmission controlled by central-charge-like quantities characterizing the number of degrees of freedom of the theories on the two sides of the interface. This provides the first explicit result for reflection and transmission across conformal interfaces in higher dimensions, where no CFT-based results are currently available.

</details>


### [331] [Scale-Covariant Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38302)
*Nikolay Bobev,Pieter Bomans,Fridrik Freyr Gautason*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了源于D$p$-膜反作用的一类具有标度协变性的弦背景，并对其进行全息分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由D$p$-膜反作用产生的大范围标度协变弦背景，以借助全息对偶研究强耦合非共形规范理论的动力学。

Method: 在合适的Weyl标架下，度规含AdS因子且伸缩子具有非平凡剖面；利用四维规范超引力、D$p$-膜缠绕紧致流形以及直接在十维构造的方式，获得多类超引力解，并构建全息RG流、计算低激发局域算符谱。

Result: 给出了多类标度协变超引力解，包括由D2-膜产生的新超对称背景，以及十维中构造的无穷族背景；计算了低激发局域算符谱，并确定热自由能、标量两点函数等观测量。

Conclusion: 标度协变结构为全息分析提供了框架，可刻画强耦合非共形规范理论，其预言可通过格点计算检验。

Abstract: We explore a vast landscape of scale-covariant string backgrounds that arise from the backreaction of D$p$-branes. In a convenient Weyl frame the metric has an AdS factor and the dilaton has a non-trivial profile. This structure provides a notion of scale covariance that facilitates a holographic analysis and provides a window into the dynamics of the dual strongly coupled non-conformal gauge theory. We present different classes of supergravity solutions of this kind and discuss their dual field theory interpretation. We find several new supersymmetric backgrounds arising from D2-branes by utilizing four-dimensional gauged supergravity, D$p$-branes wrapped on compact manifolds, and infinite families constructed directly in ten dimensions. We construct holographic RG flows between some of the new supersymmetric backgrounds and compute the spectrum of low-lying local operators. Holography determines the form of observables such as the thermal free energy and scalar two-point functions, predictions which could be tested on the lattice.

</details>


### [332] [Quantum Gravity Principles for 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ Supergravity Theories and Asymptotic Hodge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38307)
*Lukas Kaufmann,Timo Weigand*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维N=2超引力矢量多重态模空间无穷远处的退化行为，借助极化Hodge结构的实变分结构，给出与量子引力预期一致的分类，并提出两个与量子引力相容的必要约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分哪些性质仅源于超引力本身、哪些需要量子引力中的一致UV完备化，从而为超引力与量子引力的边界提供判据。此前相关结论多在Calabi-Yau紧化的弦论情境中得到，本文希望推广到一般四维N=2超引力理论。

Method: 以支配矢量多重态模空间的极化Hodge结构实变分为基础，结合数学文献的最新进展，对无穷远距离退化进行分类；在规范耦合层面与去紧化极限到五维或六维、或弱耦合弦极限的可能解释进行比对；并构造Kähler基与讨论SNC紧化。

Result: 无穷远距离退化的分类在规范耦合层面与量子引力预期的去紧化极限（五维或六维）或弱耦合弦极限解释完全一致，且此结论对一般四维N=2超引力成立，不依赖于UV完备化。同时证明存在使预势三次耦合非负的Kähler基，与几何起源无关。

Conclusion: 提出四维N=2超引力与量子引力相容的两个非平凡约束：Hodge结构的整性作为完备性假设的必要条件，以及模空间存在简单正规交叉紧化，以保证弱耦合渐近引力对偶框架的相容性。

Abstract: We study the behaviour of general four-dimensional N=2 supergravity theories near boundaries at infinity of the vector multiplet moduli space, with a focus on a clear distinction between properties that follow from supergravity alone rather than from a consistent UV completion in quantum gravity. The basis for our analysis is the appearance of a real variation of polarised Hodge structure governing the vector multiplet moduli space of N=2 supergravity, combined with very recent advances in the mathematics literature. The resulting classification of infinite distance degenerations exactly agrees, at the level of gauge couplings, with a possible interpretation of the limits as decompactification limits to five or six dimensions or as weakly coupled string limits, as expected from a quantum gravity perspective. This observation extends previous results found in the context of string compactifications on Calabi--Yau varieties and, remarkably, holds for general four-dimensional N=2 supergravity theories regardless of a UV completion. We furthermore show the existence of a Kähler basis in which the triple couplings of the prepotential are non-negative, irrespective of a geometric origin of the supergravity theory. Finally we propose two non-trivial constraints for a four-dimensional N=2 supergravity to be compatible with quantum gravity: Integrality of the Hodge structure as a necessary condition for the completeness hypothesis and the existence of a simple normal crossing compactification of the moduli space to ensure compatibility of the weakly coupled asymptotic gravitational duality frames.

</details>


### [333] [Bootstrapping Weakly Broken Gauge Theories in (A)dS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38308)
*Daniel Baumann,Kurt Hinterbichler,Callum R. T. Jones,Nathan Meurrens*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于(反)德西特空间中弱破缺规范理论的bootstrap方法，针对对偶边界流守恒被双迹算符破坏的情形，通过积分Ward恒等式导出包含三点函数非局域贡献的赝荷守恒恒等式，并将其应用于Yang-Mills理论、双AdS引力希格斯机制及de Sitter共形引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论中对称性破缺（尤其由双迹算符引起的弱破缺）在(反)德西特空间的全息对偶中缺乏系统的bootstrap处理框架，且现有守恒恒等式在破缺情形下不再适用，需要新的约束条件来限制允许的理论空间。

Method: 发展弱破缺规范理论的bootstrap方法，聚焦对偶边界流守恒被双迹算符破坏的情形；积分相应的Ward恒等式，导出赝荷守恒恒等式，其中包含作为三点函数积分的固定非局域贡献，从而对理论空间施加一致性条件。

Result: 导出赝荷守恒恒等式并给出其非局域结构；在AdS中Yang-Mills理论里约束了两个量子化扇区之间的混合；在双AdS引力希格斯机制中，普通荷守恒恒等式恢复两个独立CFT的因子化，而赝荷守恒恒等式对任意对称性破缺参数成立；在de Sitter共形引力中约束了引力子与部分无质量自旋-2场的相互作用，涵盖最小理论及含额外标量或矢量物质的情形，结果与相应体理论预言一致。

Conclusion: 赝荷守恒恒等式为弱破缺规范理论提供了有效的自举约束工具，其非局域结构能够刻画对称性破缺引起的混合与相互作用，并在多个全息场景中与体理论预言吻合，验证了该框架的适用性。

Abstract: We develop a bootstrap approach for weakly broken gauge theories in (anti-)de Sitter space, focusing on cases in which the conservation of the dual boundary current is broken by a double-trace operator. Integrating the corresponding Ward identity, we derive pseudo-charge conservation identities that contain fixed nonlocal contributions as integrals of three-point functions. These identities impose consistency conditions on the space of allowed theories. As an illustrative example, we study Yang-Mills theory in AdS with symmetry-breaking boundary conditions for charged matter and show that the pseudo-charge conservation identities constrain the mixing between the two quantization sectors. We then apply the framework to the Higgs mechanism for gravity in two AdS spaces with a common boundary, coupled so that the corresponding stress tensors are not separately conserved. In this setting, the ordinary charge conservation identities recover the factorization into two independent CFTs, whereas the pseudo-charge conservation identities hold for arbitrary values of the symmetry-breaking parameter. Finally, for conformal gravity in de Sitter space, we constrain the interactions between the graviton and the partially massless spin-2 field, both in the minimal theory and in the presence of additional scalar or vector matter, finding agreement with the predictions of the corresponding bulk theories.

</details>


### [334] [A New Approach to Black Hole Thermodynamics in General EMD-like Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38311)
*Peng-Yu Wu,H. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了含任意数量Maxwell场与dilaton标量的广义EMD理论中的带电黑洞，导出了热力学量、标量荷与非极端参数之间的代数与微分关系，并给出了两套主方程。求解任一主方程即可在无需构造黑洞解的情况下得到全部热力学量。作者以电荷幂级数微扰展开获得所有热力学量，并利用微扰黑洞解验证至电荷四阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义EMD类理论中，直接构造带电黑洞解并计算其热力学量通常较为困难。作者希望寻找一种不依赖显式黑洞解的方法，通过热力学量与标量荷、非极端参数之间的关系统一确定系统的全部热力学性质。

Method: 考虑包含任意数量Maxwell场和dilaton标量的广义EMD理论；研究带电黑洞，推导热力学量、标量荷与非极端参数之间的若干代数关系和微分关系；根据基本变量选取的不同，得到两套热力学量的主方程；对主方程进行电荷幂级数微扰求解。

Result: 导出的两套主方程中任一组的解都能给出全部热力学量，而无需构造黑洞解。通过电荷幂级数展开获得所有热力学量的微扰表达式，并用微扰黑洞解验证至并包含电荷四阶的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种不依赖显式黑洞解、系统获取广义EMD理论带电黑洞热力学量的框架，并通过四阶微扰黑洞解验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We consider general EMD-like theories involving arbitrary numbers of both Maxwell fields and dilatonic scalars. We study charged black holes and derive several algebraic and differential relations among the thermodynamic quantities, scalar charges and the non-extremal parameter. These allow us to obtain two sets of master equations of thermodynamic quantities, depending on the choice of basic variables parameterizing the system. Solutions to either set of the master equations give rise to all the thermodynamic quantities, without constructing black hole solutions. We obtain all the thermodynamic quantities perturbatively in the power series expansions of charges, which we then verify using perturbative black hole solutions up to and including the quartic order in charges.

</details>


### [335] [Holographic Krylov spread complexity for a confining multi-charge AdS soliton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38313)
*Dimitrios Chatzis,Madison Hammond,Ricardo T. Santamaria,Jonathan Whittle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究一类ⅡB型超引力解（对偶于N=4 SYM形变、IR流向(2+1)维共形禁闭超对称理论）的全息展开复杂度，通过入射探针粒子的固有动量刻画局部算子展开复杂度的变化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将"展开复杂度"这一量子信息概念推广到非共形、禁闭的红外理论中，检验其在全息对偶框架下的定义与行为，尤其是存在守恒荷（角动量）时的推广。

Method: 采用"局部算子展开复杂度变化率 ∝ 体内部入射探针粒子的固有动量"的提议；在存在守恒Noether荷时，用Routhian定义固有坐标；对径向自由下落探针与带守恒角动量的转动下落探针两种情形进行数值轨迹求解。

Result: 给出了径向下落与带角动量转动下落两类探针的轨迹；数值结果显示探针轨迹呈振荡行为，对应复杂度的振荡。

Conclusion: 全息展开复杂度可在禁闭红外理论中被定义并计算；引入守恒荷时需借助Routhian构造固有坐标；探针的振荡轨迹表明复杂度随边界时间呈现振荡特征。

Abstract: We study holographic spread complexity for a Type IIB solution dual to a deformation of $\mathcal{N} = 4$ SYM that flows to a confining (2+1)-dimensional supersymmetric theory in the IR. We use the proposal that relates the rate of change of spread complexity of local operators with the proper momentum of an infalling probe particle in the bulk. We analyse two cases: a probe particle falling radially and a probe falling radially while rotating with conserved angular momentum. We elaborate on how to define the proper coordinate in the presence of a conserved Noether charge using the Routhian. The trajectories for the probes were studied numerically, revealing an oscillatory behaviour of the complexity.

</details>


### [336] [Scaling similarity and generalised conformal symmetry in D2-brane holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38336)
*Andrea Conti,Adolfo Guarino,Ricardo Stuardo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维最大ISO(7)规范超引力的纯膨胀子扇区中研究D2-膜全息，揭示质量less IIA理论中约化出的四维拉格朗日量具有标度相似性，对应三维最大超对称Yang-Mills理论的广义共形对称性，并构造了具有广义共形对称性的QFT3及其广义RG流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在D2-膜全息框架下，理解质量less IIA理论与三维最大超对称Yang-Mills理论之间的广义共形对称性，并将massive IIA理论中已知的全息CFT3不动点和RG流推广到质量less情形。

Method: 作者采用四维最大ISO(7)规范超引力的纯膨胀子扇区（作为IIA超引力在S^6上的一致截断），利用辅助时空构造，对质量less IIA理论中约化得到的四维拉格朗日量的标度相似性进行分析，并数值构造了广义RG流，同时重新审视Coulomb分支解并提出Gubser奇异性判据的推广。

Result: 研究表明，massive IIA理论中已知的全息CFT3不动点和RG流在质量less IIA理论中有自然对应物：由广义RG流连接的具有广义共形对称性的QFT3，并给出了数值构造；同时重新审视了Coulomb分支解，并提出了适用于具有标度相似性的超引力理论的Gubser奇异性判据推广。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了质量less IIA理论与具有广义共形对称性的三维量子场论之间的全息对应，推广了Gubser奇异性判据，为研究标度相似超引力中的全息RG流提供了新框架。

Abstract: We study D2-brane holography within the purely dilatonic sector of four-dimensional maximal ISO(7)-gauged supergravity, obtained as a consistent truncation of type IIA supergravity on S$^6$. In the dual frame, the four-dimensional Lagrangian resulting from the reduction of the massless IIA theory exhibits scaling similarity, thus reflecting the generalised conformal symmetry of the dual three-dimensional maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory. Using an auxiliary spacetime construction, we show that the holographic CFT$_3$ fixed points and RG-flows known in the massive IIA theory have natural counterparts in the massless IIA theory: QFT$_3$s with generalised conformal symmetry connected by generalised RG-flows, which we construct numerically. We also revisit the Coulomb branch solutions and propose an extension of Gubser's singularity criterion to supergravity theories exhibiting scaling similarity.

</details>


### [337] [Leading Logarithms in the Effective Theory of Gravity: corrections to the scalar mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38496)
*Diana V. Mysliaeva,Kirill M. Semenov-Tian-Shansky,Dmitrii V. Vasilev,Alexey Vladimirov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借鉴手征微扰理论技术，分析广义相对论有效场论中的领头对数级数，并递归计算标量极质量的三圈领头对数修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低能有效场论中，领头对数由领头阶拉格朗日量完全生成，是普适的低能预言。作者希望将手征微扰理论中处理领头对数的方法推广到广义相对论有效场论，以系统研究其对数修正。

Method: 作者采用手征微扰理论中发展出的技术，分析广义相对论有效场论中的领头对数序列；作为具体应用，计算标量极质量的三圈领头对数修正，并证明可递归推广至任意圈阶。计算采用一般规范固定参数，以检验物理可观测量中规范参数的完全抵消。

Result: 作者给出了标量极质量的三圈领头对数修正，并证明这些修正可以递归地确定到任意圈阶；同时显式验证了规范固定参数在物理可观测量中完全抵消。

Conclusion: 广义相对论有效场论中的领头对数可由领头阶拉格朗日量递归生成，属于普适的低能预言，且最终物理结果与规范选择无关。

Abstract: In a low-energy effective field theory (EFT), scaling logarithmic terms arise from the renormalization of higher-dimensional couplings that absorb divergences generated by loop integrals with lower-order vertices. Consequently, leading logarithms (LLs) are generated entirely by the leading-order Lagrangian, rendering them universal low-energy predictions. Adapting techniques developed for chiral perturbation theory, we analyze the series of LLs in the EFT of general relativity. As a concrete application, we compute LL corrections to the scalar pole mass up to three-loop order and demonstrate that they can be determined recursively to arbitrary loop order. To confirm the physical validity of our results, we carry out the calculation using a general gauge-fixing parameter and explicitly verify its complete cancellation in the physical observable.

</details>


### [338] [Symmetry-Restoring Counter-terms in Massive Gauge Theories with Non-Anticommuting $γ_5$ via Gauge-Invariant Sector Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38557)
*Andrea Quadri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在BMHV正则化方案下，计算了带自发对称破缺的有质量Abelian手征规范理论中Slavnov-Taylor恒等式的一圈破缺，并给出恢复该恒等式所需的一圈有限抵消项；借助FMS规范不变传播场形式与上同调技术，将ST破缺按独立扇区分解，并约束其对希格斯真空期望值的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有质量手征规范理论中，正则化（如BMHV）会破坏Slavnov-Taylor恒等式，需要系统理解其破缺结构及恢复所需抵消项；同时，基于FMS的规范不变且动力学传播场形式为在破缺相中一致处理该问题提供了新框架，并有望推广至非Abelian情形。

Method: 采用BMHV方案对有质量Abelian手征规范理论（含自发对称破缺）进行一圈计算；使用基于FMS的规范不变且动力学（可在圈内传播）场形式；提出可适用于任意圈阶的代数方法，将ST破缺分解为独立扇区；并利用上同调技术约束破缺项对希格斯真空期望值的依赖，导出一组微分方程作为检验。

Result: 得到了一圈ST恒等式破缺的具体结果，并给出恢复一圈ST恒等式所需的一圈有限抵消项；通过上同调约束得到破缺项对希格斯真空期望值依赖的微分方程组，可作为对破缺相中质量相关项的检验；在无质量极限下与文献已有计算一致。

Conclusion: 所提代数方法在圈展开各阶均成立，可实现ST破缺的独立扇区分解，并能约束其对希格斯真空期望值的依赖；该方法可推广至非Abelian情形，既适用于规范不变形式也适用于传统形式。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop breaking of the Slavnov-Taylor (ST) identities in a massive Abelian chiral gauge theory with spontaneous symmetry breaking, regularized in the Breitenlohner-Maison-`t Hooft-Veltman (BMHV) scheme. We also provide the one-loop finite counter-terms required to restore the one-loop ST identities. The recent formulation of spontaneously broken models based on the Fröhlich-Morchio-Strocchi (FMS) gauge-invariant and dynamical (i.e. propagating inside loops) fields is used. The proposed algebraic approach holds true to all orders in the loop expansion and allows for a consistent decomposition of the ST breaking into independent sectors. By using cohomological techniques we eventually constrain the dependence of the ST breaking on the vacuum expectation value of the Higgs field. This yields a set of differential equations that provide a useful check of the mass-dependent terms in the ST breaking terms in the broken phase. They can be generalized to the non-Abelian case, both in the gauge-invariant formalism and in the conventional one. In the massless limit we find agreement with previous calculations present in the literature.

</details>


### [339] [Non-Semisimple Integrability and Dynamical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38700)
*Anton Pribytok*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究由boost自同构方法得到的六族可积自旋-1/2链的Jordan结构与动力学极限，建立局域算子代数、谱可加性中心缺陷、多项式unipotent R-矩阵与常数braid极限之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究六族可积自旋-1/2链的Jordan结构与动力学极限，刻画有限周期链上幂零演化与谱结构、R-矩阵及braid极限之间的对应关系。

Method: 利用局域算子代数，结合特征多项式与最小多项式分析保守端点侵蚀；通过级数匹配与碎裂递推计算精确振幅与存活概率；给出保守Markov实现的排除判据，并判定指定反应密度的保守完备化是否容许差分形式正则R-矩阵。

Result: 确定保守端点侵蚀的特征与最小多项式，发现Jordan分划可改变而两多项式不变；区分了Class 5中通过对角相似移除符号的宇称障碍与Class 6中的宇称幂零依赖；得到带杀伤过程的精确振幅与存活概率；给出排除保守Markov实现的判据及容许正则R-矩阵的保守完备化条件。

Conclusion: 局域算子代数将有限周期链上的幂零演化与谱可加性中心缺陷、多项式unipotent R-矩阵和常数braid极限相联，揭示了Jordan结构与动力学极限的深层对应。

Abstract: We investigate Jordan structure and dynamical limits in six families of integrable spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ chains obtained from the boost automorphism method. Local operator algebras connect nilpotent evolution on finite periodic chains to central defects in spectral additivity, polynomial unipotent $R$-matrices and constant braid limits. For conservative endpoint erosion, we determine the characteristic and minimal polynomials and show that the Jordan partition can change while both polynomials remain unchanged. We also identify the deletion limits that distinguish the parity obstruction to removing signs by diagonal similarity in Class 5 from the parity nilpotent dependence in Class 6. Matching expansions and fragmentation recursions yield exact amplitudes and survival probabilities for processes with killing. In addition, we establish criteria excluding conservative Markov realizations and determine which conservative completions of specified reaction densities admit regular $R$-matrices of difference form.

</details>


### [340] [Relative entropy of entanglement and tripartite minimal surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38815)
*Takato Mori,Beni Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出边界三分划A:B:C的纠缠相对熵的全息对偶，给出领头阶的面积差公式，并在随机张量网络中证明上界与（一类网络）下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为纠缠相对熵这一重要的纠缠度量建立全息对应，并给出可在张量网络模型中严格验证的表述，从而加深对体-边对应下多分划纠缠结构的理解。

Method: 提出体最小三分划曲面Γmin与同调于AB的最小曲面γAB的面积差公式；通过构造与Γmin关联的可分态并计算其相对熵证明随机张量网络中的上界；对一、二、三个Haar随机张量网络，通过约束最大三分划乘积态交叠得到匹配的下界；证明过程采用候选乘积态的序贯优化，并赋予其最小三分划曲面局部搜索的几何解释。

Result: 在领头阶得到E_R(ρ_AB)=1/(4G_N)[Area(Γmin)−Area(γAB)]；随机张量网络中上界被证明，且对一、二、三个Haar随机张量网络严格建立匹配下界；在适当几何下，最小三分划曲面可形成类似Mercedes标志的非平凡三叉结点。

Conclusion: 该工作为纠缠相对熵提供了全息对偶与张量网络中的严格证据，表明其可由体几何的最小三分划曲面刻画，并揭示了三分划几何与乘积态优化之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We propose a holographic dual of the relative entropy of entanglement for a boundary tripartition $A:B:C$. Our proposal is that, at the leading order, \begin{align} E_R(ρ_{AB}) = \frac{1}{4G_N}\Big[\mathrm{Area}(Γ_{\min}) - \mathrm{Area}(γ_{AB})\Big] \notag \end{align} where $Γ_{\min}$ is the minimal bulk tripartition surface and $γ_{AB}$ is the minimal surface homologous to $AB$. For a general random tensor network, we prove the corresponding upper bound by constructing a separable state associated with $Γ_{\min}$ and evaluating its relative entropy. We also establish the matching lower bound rigorously for networks of one, two, and three Haar random tensors by bounding the maximal tripartite product-state overlap. In appropriate geometries, the minimal tripartition surface can form a nontrivial tri-junction resembling the Mercedes logo. The proof proceeds by sequential optimization over candidate product states, which has a natural geometric interpretation as a local search for the minimal tripartition surface.

</details>


### [341] [Complex Phase Structure of Kerr-AdS$_5$ Black Holes: Critical Saddles and Fisher Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39085)
*Bum-Hoon Lee,Hocheol Lee,Somyadip Thakur*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用二元约化模型研究Kerr–AdS₅黑洞复鞍点结构，区分局部鞍点简并与全局相共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清复黑洞鞍点结构中鞍点简并、相变与配分函数零点（Fisher零点）的不同角色。

Method: 在固定角速度Ω的巨正则系综下构建二元约化积分模型，分析复Hessian的Takagi奇异值，并检验KSW相位界；在固定角动量扩展系综中分析Pearcey近似。

Result: 小/大黑洞鞍点在临界点合并，与Hawking–Page转变在|Ω|<1范围内始终分离；标准测度下热AdS在各实合并点复邻域内一致占优（G足够小），故该邻域无零点；Fisher零点聚集于Hawking–Page共存线附近，间距量级为G；准欧氏Kerr–AdS₅族满足严格KSW相位界；固定角动量系综中两合并点合成尖点，呈依赖围道的Pearcey近似。

Conclusion: 研究表明局部鞍点简并与全局相共存本质不同，澄清了Takagi模、相变与配分函数零点在复黑洞鞍点结构中的各自作用。

Abstract: We study the complex saddle structure of singly rotating Kerr--AdS$_5$ black holes using a two-variable reduced model in the grand canonical ensemble at fixed angular velocity, $Ω$. The small and large black hole saddles merge at a critical point where one Takagi singular value of the complex Hessian vanishes, identifying the soft mode associated with the merger. This merger remains distinct from the Hawking--Page transition throughout the physical domain $|Ω| < 1$. For the reduced integral with the standard measure, we show that thermal AdS dominates uniformly in a complex neighborhood of each real merger point when Newton's constant $G$ is sufficiently small. Consequently, this neighborhood contains no zeros of the partition function, even though the local saddle merger is described by Airy-type behavior. The Fisher zeros instead accumulate near the Hawking--Page coexistence line, with spacing of order $G$, through interference between thermal AdS and the large black hole saddle. We also show that the real quasi-Euclidean Kerr--AdS$_5$ family satisfies a strict asymptotic Kontsevich--Segal--Witten (KSW) phase bound for $|Ω| < 1$, with the merger point lying inside the allowed domain. In the fixed angular momentum extended ensemble, two such merger points combine into a cusp with a contour-dependent Pearcey approximation. These results distinguish local saddle degeneracies from global phase coexistence and clarify the different roles of Takagi modes, phase transitions, and zeros of the partition function in the complex black hole saddle structure.

</details>


### [342] [Gravitational multipoles, antipodal matching relations, and the logarithmic soft graviton theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39253)
*Geoffrey Compère,Dima Fontaine,Wen-Bin Liu,Kevin Nguyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用Weyl张量的位置空间分析，在四维渐近平直时空中推导大质量粒子散射的经典对数软引力子定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立验证Laddha和Sen关于对数软引力子定理的频率空间推导，并为经典软定理提供位置空间证明。

Method: 从谐和规范下的Iyer-Damour多极解出发，获得跨空间无穷远的五个Newman-Penrose Weyl标量的对跖匹配关系（G线性阶及G²阶领头物质诱导对数），将相关关系联系到Boschetti-Campiglia与Compère-Robert的辐射规范表述；随后从散射数据直接计算所需渐近场，包括物质贡献和导致引力子拖曳的非线性效应，并结合Newman-Penrose演化方程。

Result: 得到所有五个Newman-Penrose Weyl标量的无穷对跖匹配关系；成功从散射数据计算渐近场并纳入引力子拖曳非线性效应；给出经典领头与对数软引力子定理的位置空间证明。

Conclusion: 位置空间分析独立确认了Laddha和Sen的对数软引力子定理频率空间推导，为经典软引力子定理提供了新证明途径。

Abstract: We derive the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem for the scattering of massive particles in four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetime using a position-space analysis of the Weyl tensor. Starting from the Iyer-Damour multipole solution in harmonic gauge, we obtain an infinite tower of antipodal matching relations across spatial infinity for all five Newman-Penrose Weyl scalars, at linear order in $G$ and for the leading matter-induced logarithms at order $G^2$. We connect the relation relevant to the logarithmic soft theorem to the radiative-gauge formulation of Boschetti-Campiglia and Compère-Robert. We then compute the required asymptotic fields directly from scattering data, including matter contributions and nonlinear effects responsible for graviton drag. Combining these results with the Newman-Penrose evolution equations yields position-space proofs of the classical leading and logarithmic soft graviton theorems, independently confirming the frequency-space derivation of the latter originally performed by Laddha and Sen.

</details>


### [343] [Near-horizon soft theorems from local Ward identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39288)
*Jin-Peng Zhuge,Peng Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为非退化静态球对称视界建立了一个统一的领头软光子定理表述，通过视界辛形式固定光子模式归一化，得到符号由边界定向决定的共同角向软因子，并给出局部角动量表象中的几何系数，进而推广至Yang-Mills场得到树级软胶子定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞视界与宇宙学视界此前缺乏统一的软定理描述。本文旨在揭示不同视界几何背后共同的软结构，将黑洞与宇宙学视界纳入统一框架。

Method: 通过视界辛形式固定光子模式归一化，推导领头软光子定理的统一形式；确定局部角向与动量表象中选定径向归一化下的几何系数；电荷守恒考虑视界端点数据及其他边界贡献；并将同一构造应用于Yang-Mills场。

Result: 得到符号由边界定向决定的共同角向软因子；给出几何系数；电荷平衡包含视界端点数据与其他边界贡献；对Yang-Mills场成功导出对应的树级软胶子定理。

Conclusion: 黑洞视界与宇宙学视界可用统一的软定理框架描述，不同视界几何共享共同的软结构，该构造可推广至非阿贝尔规范场。

Abstract: We develop a unified formulation of leading soft photon theorems for nondegenerate static spherical horizons. By fixing the photon mode normalization from the horizon symplectic form, we obtain a common angular soft factor whose sign follows from the boundary orientation. We determine the geometric coefficients in the local angular and momentum representations for a chosen radial normalization. The charge balance includes horizon endpoint data and contributions from other boundaries. This formulation brings black hole and cosmological horizons into a common description. Applying the same construction to Yang-Mills fields yields the corresponding tree-level soft gluon theorem. These results identify the common soft structure underlying different horizon geometries.

</details>


### [344] [Soliton like solutions of $λφ^4$ theory in de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39459)
*D. V. Diakonov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了de Sitter时空中带四次势标量场的经典解，通过嵌入坐标构造解析解族，按常矢量ξ的因果性质分类，计算经典作用量并分析D=4全局真空跃迁解的状态方程与推迟格林函数行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究de Sitter时空中四次势标量场经典解的解析结构及其物理效应，特别是解的因果分类、经典作用量的有限性以及真空跃迁对晚期信号传播的影响。

Method: 采用嵌入坐标X_α，假设解形式为φ=F(X·ξ)，按常矢量ξ的类时或类空性质分类求解，得到多族解析解；计算各解的经典作用量；分析D=4全局真空跃迁解的辐射状态方程及推迟格林函数。

Result: 对类时ξ，解全局正则，描述围绕极小值的全局振荡或全局真空跃迁；对类空ξ，解在全局patch奇异但在open slicing正则，在Poincaré patch对应收缩气泡。经典作用量仅当D≤5时有限。D=4全局真空跃迁解具有辐射状态方程w=1/3，其推迟格林函数相比真空背景出现增长的尾巴。

Conclusion: de Sitter时空四次势标量场存在按ξ因果性质分类的解析解族；D=4全局真空跃迁解对应辐射流体且其推迟格林函数出现增长尾巴，表明该跃迁在晚期放大了信号。

Abstract: We study classical solutions of a scalar field with quartic potential in de Sitter space-time. Using the ansatz $φ=F(X\cdotξ)$, via embeding coordinate $X_α$ of the ambiend space we find several families of analytic solutions classified by the causal character of the constant vector $ξ$. For time-like $ξ$, the solutions are globally regular and describe either global oscillations around a minimum or global vacuum transition; for space-like $ξ$, they are singular in the global patch but regular in the open slicing. In the Poincaré patch they correspond to contracting bubbles. We compute the classical actions of these solition which are finite only in spacetime dimensions $D \le 5$. We show that the $D=4$ global vacuum transition solution has the radiation equation of state $w=1/3$. In $D=4$ global vacuum transition solution, the retarded Green's function acquires a growing tail as compared to the Green's function on top of vacuum, implying that the transition amplifies the signal at late times.

</details>


### [345] [Dispersion relation, propagation features and canonical gauge structure of GNLED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39470)
*Abedennour Dib,José A. Helayël-Neto,Tomislav Prokopec,Alessandro D. A. M. Spallicci,Youssef Temmam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究非动力学背景下广义非线性电动力学的传播性质与正则规范结构，发现类空背景诱导有效质量，理论仍保持两个物理自由度与变形U(1)规范对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非动力学背景场对广义非线性电动力学（GNLED）光子传播与规范结构的影响，特别是背景诱导的导数耦合是否产生真实质量及额外极化。

Method: 从理论的光子二次扇区出发，通过场重定义将背景诱导的导数耦合转化为由 \(V_\mu=\partial_\mu\alpha/\alpha\)（\(\alpha=\sqrt{\partial\mathcal{L}/\partial\mathcal{F}|_B}\)）描述的质量样贡献；推导色散关系、传播子，并进行完整哈密顿分析以确定约束与自由度。

Result: 常值背景下，对纯类空 \(V_\mu\)，传播模式满足 \(\omega^2=\|\vec k\|^2+\|\vec V\|^2\)，定义有效质量 \(m_{\rm eff}=|\vec V|\) 与有限静止频率；相速超光速、群速亚光速，呈能隙模式色散。传播子中质量极点位于横场扇区，纵向奇异性属规范固定。哈密顿分析给出两个第一类约束、仅两个物理自由度，无额外极化；规范变换为变形阿贝尔 \(U(1)\) 对称性；纯类空背景下哈密顿量正定。

Conclusion: 背景诱导的有效质量不引入新极化，理论保持两个物理自由度与变形 Abelian \(U(1)\) 规范对称性，且纯类空背景下哈密顿量正定，理论自洽稳定。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation properties and canonical gauge structure of Generalised Non-Linear Electrodynamics (GNLED) in the presence of a non-dynamical background field. Starting from the quadratic photon sector of the theory, we perform a field redefinition that converts the background-induced derivative coupling into a mass-like contribution characterized by the vector \(V_μ=\partial_μα/α\) where $α= \sqrt{\left.\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}}{\partial \mathcal{F}}\right|_B}$. For a constant background, we derive the corresponding dispersion relation and show that, for a purely space-like \(V_μ\), the propagating mode obeys \(ω^2=\|\vec k\|^2+\|\vec V\|^2\), defining an effective mass scale \(m_{\rm eff}=|\vec{V}|\) and a finite rest frequency. The resulting phase and group velocities exhibit the characteristic dispersive behaviour of a gapped mode, with subluminal group velocity and superluminal phase velocity. We then construct the propagator and show that the massive pole resides in the transverse sector, while the longitudinal singularity is associated with gauge fixing. A complete Hamiltonian analysis yields two first-class constraints and only two physical degrees of freedom, demonstrating that the effective mass does not introduce an additional polarisation. The associated gauge transformation is a deformed Abelian \(U(1)\) symmetry. Finally, we show that the Hamiltonian is positive definite for purely space-like backgrounds.

</details>


### [346] [Nonperturbative Dyson--Schwinger equations in QCD: a first approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39506)
*Vladimir Dzhunushaliev,Vladimir Folomeev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出基于无穷非微扰Dyson-Schwinger方程组的量化方案，并给出静态情形下的首个截断近似，涉及规范场Green函数因子化、自由度划分及非线性Dirac方程，讨论了质量间隙与量纲嬗变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非微扰量化需处理无穷Dyson-Schwinger方程组，直接求解困难，因此需要合理的截断近似来获得可操作的理论框架，并理解质量间隙与量纲嬗变等非微扰现象。

Method: 提出静态情形下截断无穷方程组的近似：(a)通过引入标量场将真空规范场两点Green函数因子化；(b)非真空情形下将SU(3)规范场自由度分为"准经典"与"准量子"两类；(c)四点Green函数表示为两点Green函数的双线性组合；(d)给出夸克与规范场相互作用三点Green函数的近似，使夸克场算符的Dirac方程分裂为两方程，其一描述费米场期望值，其二含非线性项描述由真空规范场束缚的海夸克凝聚。同时讨论合适规范的选取。

Result: 该近似可能导致非线性Dirac方程出现，从而在相应方程解的能量谱中产生质量间隙；讨论了"量纲嬗变"的出现，并论证有限截断中出现的"闭合常数"在向无穷Dyson-Schwinger方程组过渡时应保留；还与非微扰量化与湍流随机理论之间的类比进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 所提截断近似为非微扰量化提供了可操作框架，非线性Dirac方程的出现在原则上可导致质量间隙，闭合常数支撑量纲嬗变并应延续至无穷方程组，且非微扰量化与湍流随机理论存在类比意义。

Abstract: We consider a procedure for nonperturbative quantization based on an infinite system of nonperturbative Dyson--Schwinger equations. We propose a first approximation for truncating this infinite system of equations in the static case. The main ideas underlying this approximation are as follows: (a) the two-point Green's functions of vacuum gauge fields are factorized by introducing scalar fields; (b) in the non-vacuum case, the degrees of freedom of the $SU(3)$ gauge field can be divided into ``almost classical'' and ``almost quantum'' degrees of freedom; (c) the four-point Green's functions of the gauge fields are represented as bilinear combinations of two-point Green's functions; (d) an approximation for the three-point Green's function describing the interaction between quarks and gauge fields is proposed, which leads to the splitting of the original Dirac equation for the quark-field operators into two equations, one describing the expectation value of the fermion field and the other acquiring a nonlinear term and serving to describe a condensate composed of sea quarks bound by the vacuum gauge field. We consider the choice of gauge appropriate for this approximation to nonperturbative quantization. We point out that the emergence of a nonlinear Dirac equation within this approximation may lead to a mass gap in the energy spectrum of solutions of the corresponding systems of equations. The issue of the emergence of ``dimensional transmutation'' within this approximation is also discussed. We provide arguments in favor of the statement that the ``closure constants'' appearing in the finite truncation and giving rise to ``dimensional transmutation'' should survive in the transition to the infinite system of Dyson--Schwinger equations. An analogy between nonperturbative quantization and the stochastic theory of turbulence is also discussed.

</details>


### [347] [Light-Ray Supersymmetry, BMS Algebras and the Averaged Null Energy Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39691)
*Dhruva K. S*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明酉超对称洛伦兹共形场论的光线代数包含普适的超对称广义 bms 代数，并在 d=3,4 维构造平行光线算符、计算其分次对易子，给出 ANEC 的超对称推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索共形场论中光线算符代数的代数结构，并寻求对平均零能量条件（ANEC）的、适用于强耦合理论的非微扰理解与证明。

Method: 基于全局超对称及微观因果性、酉性、洛伦兹不变性等基本原理，利用超对称流、R 对称流与能动张量构造支撑在余维一零超曲面上的平行光线算符，并在 d=3,4 维计算其分次对易子。

Result: 光线代数确实包含普适的超对称广义 bms 代数；由零面局部超对称代数可推出平均零能量算符为正算符，从而给出 ANEC 的超对称推导；结果在自由无质量超对称理论中得到验证。

Conclusion: 超对称光线代数是共形场论的普适结构，其构造不依赖微扰，适用于强耦合理论；并给出论证：局部超对称在一般超对称量子场论中仍保证 ANEC 成立。

Abstract: We show that the light-ray algebra of unitary supersymmetric Lorentzian conformal field theories contains a universal supersymmetric generalized $\mathfrak{bms}$ algebra. We construct parallel light-ray operators using the supersymmetry current, $R-$symmetry current and the stress tensor supported on a co-dimension one null hypersurface and determine their graded commutators in $d=3,4$ to verify this claim. Our construction only relies on global supersymmetry and fundamental physical principles such as microcausality, unitarity and Lorentz invariance and thus applies to strongly coupled theories. As a corollary, we show that the averaged null energy operator is a positive operator using the local supersymmetry algebra on the null sheet, providing a supersymmetric derivation of the averaged null energy condition (ANEC). Finally, we verify our results in free massless supersymmetric theories and present arguments to support that the local supersymmetry continues to ensure the ANEC is satisfied even in general supersymmetric quantum field theories.

</details>


### [348] [Fractional Superfluid Vortices and Worldsheet Wess--Zumino--Witten Models in Two-Color QCD and $Sp(2N)$ Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39760)
*Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究两色QCD及更一般赝实规范理论中有限重子密度下的超流涡旋。反对称双夸克凝聚允许分数最小涡旋，重子相位绕数ν=1/m，对应常规环流量子的1/m，每个涡旋局域一个Sp(2)≃SU(2)核心扇区。正交味平面内涡旋的领头阶手征作用严格因子化；离对角味零模在有限间距下不可归一化，但在重合时局域化，使可归一化扇区从[Sp(2)]^m增强到Sp(2m)。对Sp(2N)规范理论的基本费米子，体WZW项相容下降：n个重合最小涡旋簇携带Sp(2n)_N WZW理论，而重子相位绕数ν为正整数的味对称涡旋携带Sp(2m)_{Nν}。单位绕数下给出到Sp(2m)_N的涡旋映射，WZW不动点中心荷c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)，并暗示更广泛的非超对称四维/二维对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限重子密度下，超流涡旋的拓扑与低能有效理论结构（尤其是分数环流量子和核心扇区的对称性增强）尚缺乏对赝实规范理论（如两色QCD）的系统刻画，需要建立涡旋与二维WZW共形场论之间的对应关系。

Method: 采用双夸克凝聚的有效场论分析：考察重子相位绕数为ν=1/m的分数最小涡旋及其Sp(2)核心扇区；分析正交味平面内涡旋手征作用的因子化与味零模归一化行为；对Sp(2N)规范理论基本费米子推导体WZW项的相容下降规则，并计算涡旋簇与味对称涡旋所对应的WZW理论及其中心荷。

Result: 最小涡旋具重子相位绕数ν=1/m，环流量子为常规值的1/m，各局域Sp(2)核心；正交味平面涡旋领头阶手征作用严格因子化，味零模在重合时局域化使可归一化扇区由[Sp(2)]^m增强至Sp(2m)。体WZW项下降为：n个重合最小涡旋→Sp(2n)_N，味对称涡旋（ν为正整数）→Sp(2m)_{Nν}；单位绕数对应Sp(2m)_N，中心荷c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)。

Conclusion: 分数超流涡旋及其重合时的对称性增强与WZW项下降规则，为赝实规范理论中涡旋提供统一的二维共形场论描述，并提示存在更广泛的非超对称四维/二维对应。

Abstract: We study superfluid vortices in two-color QCD and more general pseudoreal gauge theories with even flavor number $N_f=2m$ at finite baryon density. The antisymmetric diquark condensate admits fractional minimal vortices with baryon-phase winding $ν=1/m$, corresponding to $1/m$ of the conventional circulation quantum, each localizing an $Sp(2)\simeq SU(2)$ core sector. For vortices embedded in mutually orthogonal flavor planes, the leading-order chiral action factorizes exactly. Off-diagonal flavor zero modes are non-normalizable at finite separation but become localized at coincidence, enhancing the normalizable sector from $[Sp(2)]^m$ to $Sp(2m)$. For fundamental fermions of an $Sp(2N)$ gauge theory, the bulk Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) term descends compatibly: a cluster of $n$ coincident minimal vortices carries an $Sp(2n)_N$ WZW theory, while a flavor-symmetric vortex with positive integer baryon-phase winding $ν$ carries $Sp(2m)_{Nν}$. At unit winding this yields the vortex map to $Sp(2m)_N$, with central charge $c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)$ at the WZW fixed point, and suggests a broader nonsupersymmetric four-dimensional/two-dimensional correspondence.

</details>


### [349] [AI for BMN Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39769)
*Min-xin Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 借助AI工具重新审视BMN弦在IIB pp波背景下的若干数学问题，在部分问题上取得进展，另一些问题仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些数学问题与高亏格临界超弦振幅的计算以及宇宙学常数的可能估算等重要物理问题相关，因此值得重新研究。

Method: 在AI（人工智能）工具辅助下，对作者此前BMN弦研究中若干数学表述明确的问题进行再探讨。

Result: 在若干问题上取得了显著进展，但仍有部分问题未能解决。

Conclusion: AI辅助方法在推进相关数学-物理问题上具有一定价值，但问题的完全解决仍需进一步工作。

Abstract: With the assistance of AI (Artificial Intelligence) tools, we revisit some mathematically well-formulated problems from our previous studies of BMN (Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase) strings on type IIB string theory pp-wave background. These mathematical problems have important physical implications, relating to the computations of higher-genus critical superstring amplitudes, possible estimate of the cosmological constant. We make significant progress on some problems, while others remain unresolved.

</details>


### [350] [Light non-vacuum BMS$_3$ blocks at order $1/c_{M}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39893)
*Boyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了四轻外部原初场、非真空交换的 BMS$_3$ 最高权块在 $1/c_M$ 阶修正的闭式表达式，证明到该阶只需全局部分与含单个非全局生成元的后代项，并给出重求和方法及独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 BMS$_3$ 共形块的大中心荷展开修正，以理解超相对论/平坦空间全息中的共形块结构，并补充非真空交换情形下 $1/c_M$ 修正的解析结果。

Method: 对四轻外部原初场、非真空交换的 BMS$_3$ 最高权块，保持 $c_L$ 与算子权固定，证明到 $1/c_M$ 阶只需全局扇区及含恰好一个 $L_{-n}$ 或 $M_{-n}$（$n\geq2$）的后代项；将计算截断为全局块及其导数并重求和；对成对相同外部算子，用阴影表示下归一化三点函数中引入边界引力子独立复现修正；对成对相同构型，用翻转向手征表示的 Virasoro 块作形式超相对论收缩进行交叉检验，并证明收缩与逐后代层提取 $1/c_M$ 系数对易；退化的 $\xi_p=0$ 交换另用商构造处理。

Result: 得到非零交换 boost 权 $\xi_p$ 下 $1/c_M$ 修正的闭式表达式（由全局块及其导数重求和给出）；成对相同外部算子时，边界引力子方法独立复现该修正，同一方法亦复现已知 $1/c$ 阶非真空 Virasoro 修正；超相对论收缩给出相同修正，且证明其与逐层提取 $1/c_M$ 系数对易；$\xi_p=0$ 退化交换不能由一般块的光滑极限得到，需单独商构造。

Conclusion: BMS$_3$ 块的 $1/c_M$ 修正可由全局块的截断与重求和给出，并通过边界引力子与超相对论收缩两条独立途径验证；退化 $\xi_p=0$ 情形属于非光滑的特殊极限，须单独处理。

Abstract: We derive a closed expression for the $1/c_{M}$ correction to highest-weight BMS$_3$ blocks with four light external primaries of generic weights and non-vacuum exchange. With $c_L$ and all operator weights held fixed, we prove that the global sector and descendants containing exactly one non-global generator $L_{-n}$ or $M_{-n}$ with $n\geq2$ suffice through this order. This truncation reduces the intrinsic calculation to global blocks and their derivatives, whose contributions we resum for nonzero exchanged boost weight $ξ_{p}$. For pairwise identical external operators, we independently reproduce the correction through the introduction of boundary gravitons in the normalized three-point functions in the shadow representation, while keeping the exchanged two-point function fixed. The analogous prescription also reproduces the known non-vacuum Virasoro correction at order $1/c$. For the same external configuration, a formal ultra-relativistic contraction of Virasoro blocks with one flipped chiral representation provides a further check: it yields the same correction, and we prove that contraction commutes with extraction of the $1/c_{M}$ coefficient at each fixed descendant level. The degenerate exchange with $ξ_{p}=0$ requires a separate quotient construction and is not obtained as a smooth limit of the generic block, which we discuss in the appendix.

</details>


### [351] [Quantum corrections to the Casimir effect in a scalar Hořava-Lifshitz theory with rough plates at low temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40081)
*Claudio Bórquez,Byron Droguett*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了(3+1)维Hořava-Lifshitz型理论中带四次耦合的自相互作用实标量场的Casimir效应，边界为服从Dirichlet条件的粗糙边界，得到重整化有效势、拓扑质量和Casimir能，并分析了红外发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨各向异性标度、边界粗糙度与温度对Casimir效应的联合影响，并检验非相对论性Hořava-Lifshitz理论中标量场量子效应与标准相对论结果的差异。

Method: 采用有效作用量与广义ζ函数方法，在低温区域推导包含各向异性标度、边界粗糙度和温度的重整化有效势；计算单圈拓扑质量与双圈Casimir能。

Result: 在无质量极限下，奇数z>1时拓扑质量和双圈Casimir贡献出现红外发散，与缺乏内禀质量标度相关；单圈Casimir能保持有限。z=1时，在零温和零粗糙度极限下恢复标准相对论Dirichlet结果。

Conclusion: Hořava-Lifshitz型理论中标量场的Casimir效应显著依赖各向异性标度指数z，奇数z>1导致红外发散，而z=1退化为标准相对论情形。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect of a massive, self-interacting real scalar field with quartic coupling in a $(3+1)$-dimensional Hořava-Lifshitz-type theory, subject to rough boundaries obeying Dirichlet boundary conditions. Using the effective action and generalized $ζ$-function approach, we derive the renormalized effective potential, incorporating anisotropic scaling, boundary roughness, and temperature in the low-temperature regime. We determine the topological mass at one-loop order and the Casimir energy up to two loops. In the massless limit, the topological mass and the two-loop Casimir contribution exhibit infrared divergences for odd values $z>1$, associated with the absence of an intrinsic mass scale, while the one-loop Casimir energy remains finite. For $z=1$, the standard relativistic Dirichlet result is recovered in the zero-temperature and vanishing-roughness limits.

</details>


### [352] [Large gaps and BTZ entropy in modular spectra with positive integer degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39930)
*Chi-Ming Chang,Reiko Liu,Wen-Jie Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过递归修复Virasoro真空的Maloney–Witten–Keller模补全，构造了具有唯一真空、离散能级和正整数简并度的模不变环面配分函数，并证明其能谱在足够大的中心荷下重现BTZ鞍点的微扰熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maloney–Witten–Keller的模补全给出了Virasoro真空的近似配分函数，但存在收敛性与非整数简并等问题。本文旨在构造严格模不变、能级离散且简并度为正整数的配分函数，并研究其是否能在足够大的中心荷下重现单一微扰BTZ鞍点的熵，尤其是在热AdS主导的能区。

Method: 采用递归修复Maloney–Witten–Keller模补全的方法，通过精确模修复与有限矩匹配逐步离散化谱带，同时保持已构造的低能级并控制收敛性。对c_L=c_R=c、a=(c-1)/12的情形，构造出原初维度间隙Δ_1=(1+κ)a（κ>0足够小）以及Δ_1=a+δ（任意固定δ≥0）的谱，并验证所有非真空原初场满足h,h̄≥(c-1)/24。

Result: 所构造的谱在固定δ族中，其态密度经固定非负归一化紧支撑光滑核平滑后，可在1/c的任意固定有限阶上与单一微扰BTZ鞍点的预测完全匹配；计数包含所有自旋与Virasoro后代。其对数在任意固定正E/c下重现Bekenstein–Hawking熵及其修正，其中E=Δ-c/12，包括0<E<c/12的热AdS主导区。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一类严格模不变的配分函数，其能谱在足够大的中心荷下与单一微扰BTZ鞍点的平滑态密度及Bekenstein–Hawking熵修正一致，表明在热AdS主导区仍可实现这种匹配，为二维共形场论中黑洞微态计数与模不变性之间的关系提供了新构造。

Abstract: We construct modular-invariant torus partition functions with a unique vacuum, discrete energy levels and positive integer degeneracies by recursively repairing the Maloney--Witten--Keller modular completion of the Virasoro vacuum. Exact modular repairs and finite moment matching discretize successive spectral bands while preserving earlier levels and controlling convergence. For $c_L=c_R=c$ and $a=(c-1)/12$, the construction realizes primary dimension gaps $Δ_1=(1+κ)a$ for sufficiently small fixed $κ>0$, and $Δ_1=a+δ$ for any fixed $δ\ge0$, at every sufficiently large real $c$. Every nonvacuum primary satisfies $h,\bar h\ge(c-1)/24$. In the fixed-$δ$ family, spectra can be chosen whose densities of states, smoothed with a fixed nonnegative normalized smooth kernel of compact support, match the correspondingly smoothed prediction of a single perturbative BTZ saddle through every fixed finite order in $1/c$. The count includes all spins and Virasoro descendants. Its logarithm reproduces the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy and its corrections at every fixed positive $E/c$, where $E=Δ-c/12$. This includes $0<E<c/12$, where thermal AdS dominates the canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [353] [Greybody factors of large Gauss-Bonnet d-dimensional black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39944)
*Filipe Moura,João Rodrigues*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在高频极限下解析计算了含Gauss-Bonnet修正的d维大球对称黑洞的灰体因子，涵盖eikonal极限与高度阻尼情形，给出完整透射与反射散射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解Gauss-Bonnet修正对高维黑洞辐射/散射性质的影响，需要在高频极限下获得灰体因子的解析结果，并覆盖不同频率区域及不同扰动类型。

Method: 解析计算d维大球对称Gauss-Bonnet黑洞的灰体因子；分别处理eikonal极限（频率实部远大于虚部）和高度阻尼情形（虚部远大于实部）；考虑引力子与试探标量场发射，并计算完整透射和反射散射系数；分析张量、矢量与标量引力扰动。

Result: 得到完整透射和反射散射系数；在高度阻尼极限下，张量、矢量、标量引力扰动给出相同结果；在eikonal极限下三者的结果不相同。

Conclusion: Gauss-Bonnet修正下高维大球对称黑洞的灰体因子在高频极限可解析求解；高度阻尼极限对三类引力扰动具有普适性，而eikonal极限则依赖扰动类型。

Abstract: We compute analytically greybody factors for d-dimensional large spherically symmetric black holes with Gauss-Bonnet corrections in the high frequency limit. Our calculations include both the eikonal limit, where the real part of the frequency of the scattered wave is much larger than the imaginary part, and the highly damped case,where the imaginary part of the frequency is much larger than the real part, addressing the emission of gravitons and test scalar fields, and yielding full transmission and reflection scattering coefficients. We consider tensorial, vectorial and scalar gravitational perturbations, obtaining the same result for the three cases in the highly damped limit, but not in the eikonal limit.

</details>


### [354] [Nilpotent Symmetries and Conserved Charges: BRST-Quantized Version of the 4D Abelian 2-Form Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40025)
*R. P. Malik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究4D自由阿贝尔2-形式规范理论的BRST量子化，推导(反-)BRST与(反-)余BRST守恒荷的修正版本，使其在幂零对称变换下不变，并证明两类荷彼此反对易（线性独立），同时从荷代数结构导出CF型限制条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是刻画该理论的(反-)BRST与(反-)余BRST对称性及其相应的Noether守恒荷，特别是解决原始Noether荷在幂零对称变换下不变性的问题。

Method: 方法上，本文讨论(3+1)维4D自由阿贝尔2-形式规范理论的BRST量子化，给出其无穷小、连续、离壳幂零的(反-)BRST与(反-)余BRST对称变换，并利用无穷小连续对称变换与其生成元（即Noether守恒荷）之间的深层数学关系，系统推导修正的守恒荷。

Result: 结果表明：原始Noether守恒荷在幂零对称变换下并不保持不变；修正后的荷具有不变性，且两类荷彼此反对易，从而证明其线性独立；并进一步导出(反-)BRST与(反-)余BRST不变的Curci-Ferrari (CF)型限制条件。

Conclusion: 结论是，该4D自由阿贝尔2-形式规范理论的(反-)BRST与(反-)余BRST荷需采用修正形式方能在相应幂零对称变换下不变，荷的反对易关系确立其线性独立性，并从荷代数导出CF型限制，揭示了对称变换与守恒荷之间的深刻数学联系。

Abstract: We focus on the Becchi-Rouet-Stora-Tyutin (BRST) quantized version of the four (3 + 1)-dimensional (4D) free Abelian 2-form gauge theory and (i) discuss its infinitesimal, continuous and off-shell nilpotent (anti-)BRST and (anti-)co-BRST symmetry transformations, and (ii) derive the corresponding Noether conserved (anti-)BRST and (anti-)co-BRST charges. We demonstrate that these charges are not invariant under the above nilpotent symmetry transformations. We systematically derive the modified versions of the above Noether charges which are found to be invariant under the above symmetry transformations. As a consequence, both types of the above charges anticommute with each-other which proves their linear independence. We also derive the (anti-)BRST and (anti-)co-BRST invariant Curci-Ferrari (CF) type restrictions from the algebraic structures of the Noether conserved charges. In all the above proofs and derivations, we take into account the deep mathematical relationship that exists between the infinitesimal and continuous symmetry transformations and their generators as the Noether conserved charges.

</details>


### [355] [Lie Algebra Saddles in the IKKT Matrix Model and Criteria for the Emergence of Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40183)
*Henry Liao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对IKKT矩阵模型中由有限维实李代数实现的经典鞍点进行分类，发现洛伦兹型鞍点的半单部分至多为纯空间性的so(1,3)，时间方向必属于可解根基，得出"空间可半单、时间必可解"的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKKT矩阵模型中时空的维数、几何与度规号差须由矩阵动力学自发涌现，因此需要刻画哪些经典解能产生具有正确号差的涌现时空，尤其是时间维度如何从代数结构中产生。

Method: 将十个Hermitian矩阵构成有限维实李代数的幺正表示作为一类鞍点，把运动方程约化为关于对称双线性型h的单一代数方程，并通过Casimir障碍、半单代数理想间耦合以及可解根基的作用来分析h的号差与可容许维度。

Result: 复化代数为单时Casimir障碍只允许h=0，解释了模糊球与膨胀宇宙解需质量项或红外正则化；半单代数仅在相异单理想耦合时才有鞍点，此类鞍点最早出现于六维，十二维起可达任意号差；至多十一维的所有非退化洛伦兹鞍点（含十维模型的所有洛伦兹背景）必有非零可解根基，半单部分唯一可能是纯空间性的so(1,3)，每个类时方向都含根基分量；十维时根基须交换且承载Weyl旋量表示，非交换根基最早出现于十一维。

Conclusion: 在IKKT矩阵模型中，洛伦兹型涌现时空的半单部分至多为纯空间性的so(1,3)，时间方向必须来自可解根基：空间可以半单，时间必须可解。这为时间维度的涌现提供了代数判据。

Abstract: In the IKKT matrix model, the dimension, geometry, and metric signature of spacetime must emerge from the matrix dynamics. We classify the Lie-algebraic saddles of the model: classical solutions in which the ten Hermitian matrices realize a finite-dimensional real Lie algebra in a unitary representation. For this class the equations of motion collapse to a single algebraic equation for a symmetric bilinear form $h$ on the algebra, and the signature of the emergent spacetime can be read off from $h$. Two facts control the outcome. First, when the complexified algebra is simple, a Casimir obstruction allows only $h=0$; this explains why fuzzy spheres and the expanding-universe solutions need mass terms or infrared regulators. Second, semisimple algebras admit saddles only through couplings between distinct simple ideals; such saddles first appear in six dimensions and reach every signature only from twelve. These facts yield criteria for the emergence of time. Every nondegenerate Lorentzian saddle in at most eleven dimensions---hence every Lorentzian background of the ten-dimensional model---has a nonzero solvable radical. The only nontrivial possibility of the semisimple part is the Lorentz algebra $\mathfrak{so}(1,3)$, which is purely spacelike, and every timelike direction has a component in the radical. When the semisimple part is nontrivial, it acts on the radical only from ten dimensions on; at ten dimensions, the radical must be abelian and must carry the Weyl-spinor representation, which solves the Lorentzian equations of motion; a nonabelian radical first appears in eleven dimensions. As a result, space can be semisimple; time must be solvable.

</details>


### [356] [The classical limit of the Magnus expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40291)
*Andreas Brandhuber,Graham R. Brown,Sebastien MacDonald,Paolo Pichini,Gabriele Travaglini,Pablo Vives Matasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将量子场论中 Magnus 展开的经典极限与置换的 Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf 代数联系起来，在一个三次标量理论中证明了超经典贡献在 Schwinger 固有时被积函数层面完全抵消，且经典 Magnus 振幅与世界线量子场论结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Magnus 展开的经典极限与经典散射（引力、标量 QED）的传播子结构密切相关，但其与组合 Hopf 代数结构（尤其是置换的 Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf 代数）之间的深层联系尚不明确。作者希望揭示这种代数结构如何支配经典极限中的抵消机制，并借此简化 Murua 系数的计算。

Method: 作者研究一个描述大质量粒子与无质量标量量子耦合的三次标量理论，该理论捕捉了引力与标量 QED 经典散射相关的传播子结构。利用大质量传播子的 Schwinger 参数化，在树图层面计算 Magnus 振幅的经典极限；并通过与 Hopf 代数（特别是伴随第一 Eulerian 投影子的作用，它湮灭与不连通贡献相关的 shuffle 积）建立联系来分析抵消。

Result: 1) 证明了所有超经典贡献在 Schwinger 固有时被积函数层面（积分之前）即完全抵消；2) 经典 Magnus 振幅与世界线量子场论（WQFT）的预期精确匹配；3) 两项结果均依赖于不连通图的抵消，该抵消与 Hopf 代数相关，具体对应于伴随第一 Eulerian 投影子的作用；4) 由于世界线图包含一般的有向树拓扑，而场论图仅包含有向链，这一对应给出了用更简单的有向链系数表示一般 Murua 系数的新公式；5) 观察到 Magnus 振幅与支配多条 Wilson 线指数化的 web-mixing 矩阵之间存在启发性联系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Magnus 展开经典极限与 Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf 代数之间的桥梁，揭示了 Eulerian 投影子在不连通图抵消中的核心作用，并由此导出 Murua 系数的新表达式，同时指出 Magnus 振幅与 Wilson 线 web-mixing 矩阵的潜在联系。

Abstract: We uncover a connection between the classical limit of the Magnus expansion in quantum field theory and the Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf algebra of permutations. We study a cubic scalar theory describing a massive particle coupled to massless scalar quanta, which captures the propagator structure relevant to classical scattering in gravity and scalar QED. Using a Schwinger parametrisation of the massive propagators, we compute the classical limit of Magnus amplitudes at tree level in this theory. First, we prove that all hyperclassical contributions cancel at the level of the Schwinger proper-time integrand, before integration. Then, we show that the classical Magnus amplitude precisely matches expectations from worldline quantum field theory. Both results rely on cancellations of disconnected diagrams which we relate to the Hopf algebra, specifically to the action of the adjoint first Eulerian projector, which annihilates the shuffle products associated with disconnected contributions. Moreover, the worldline diagrams include generic directed-tree topologies, whereas the field-theory diagrams only include directed chains. The correspondence we establish between the two therefore leads to a new formula for generic Murua coefficients in terms of simpler directed chain coefficients. Finally, we observe a suggestive connection between Magnus amplitudes and the web-mixing matrices governing the exponentiation of multiple Wilson lines.

</details>
