### [357] [Four-loop beta function in N=2 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, dimensional reduction, and treatment of $γ_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40326)
*M. A. Bezuglov,B. A. Kniehl,V. N. Velizhanin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文采用降维正则化方案，在四圈水平图解计算了N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论的beta函数，该理论已知在一圈以上为零。这为处理D维中棘手的γ5狄拉克矩阵及降维方案的实施提供了严格的检验平台。在使用阅读点法处理γ5时，便于操作的实现细节在四圈开始变得重要。作者因此严格验证了这些实现问题，这些问题此前已被提倡用于标准模型中四圈规范耦合beta函数的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论的beta函数在一圈以上为零，这一已知性质可作为严格的基准检验。同时，标准模型中四圈规范耦合beta函数的计算依赖于对γ5处理和降维方案实施细节的若干主张，需要独立验证其正确性。

Method: 采用降维（dimensional-reduction）正则化方案，以图解方式在四圈阶计算N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论的beta函数；在γ5的处理上使用阅读点法（reading-point method），并在四圈水平严格考察实现细节。

Result: 在四圈水平完成了beta函数的图解计算，并通过该理论beta函数应为零的已知结果，验证了γ5阅读点法及降维方案实现细节的正确性。结果显示，某些实现细节在四圈阶开始对结果产生实质性影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为降维方案中γ5的处理提供了严格的四圈基准检验，确认了此前针对标准模型四圈规范耦合beta函数计算所提倡的实现方式的有效性。

Abstract: Adopting the dimensional-reduction scheme, we diagrammatically compute, at four loops, the beta function of N=2 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, which is well known to vanish beyond one loop. This provides a formidable testbed for both the treatment of the problematic $γ_5$ Dirac matrix in D dimensions and the implementation of the dimensional-reduction scheme. In the use of the reading-point method of $γ_5$, being preferable for convenience, specific details of implementation start to matter at four loops. We thus rigorously validate these implementation issues, which have previously been advocated for the calculation of the gauge-coupling beta functions at four loops in the Standard Model.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [358] [H-principle for corank two distributions of odd rank and maximal first Kronecker index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38214)
*Milan Jovanovic,Igor Zelenko*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究奇数维流形上余秩2分布的最大第一Kronecker指标类，证明其微分关系是ample的，从而满足多参数C^0-close h-原理，并给出流形可容纳此类分布的完整拓扑刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 余秩2分布是接触分布与偶接触分布的类比；此前ample性仅对偶接触分布（McDuff, 1987）和最大小增长向量情形（Martínez-Aguinaga, 2026）被验证，本文类的ample性尚未解决。

Method: 利用pencil在超平面限制下的Kronecker–Weierstrass标准型，将ample性验证归结为计算实数二元形式对的resultant超曲面补集连通分支的凸包；这些分支由卷绕数（即经典Cauchy指标）分隔。

Result: 证明相应微分关系是ample的，因而满足多参数C^0-close h-原理；得到(2N+3)维流形容纳该类分布的完整拓扑刻画，且该判据对非平行化流形亦成立。

Conclusion: 对N≥2，该类是最大小增长向量类的真开子类，由pencil上的额外条件界定；与偶接触情形不同，此处需移除的集合并非薄集，故补集不自动连通，ample性验证需依赖Cauchy指标与凸包计算。

Abstract: We study corank-two distributions $D$ of odd rank $2N+1$ whose associated pencil of skew-symmetric forms $\{dα|_{D}(q)\mid α\inΩ^1(M),\ α|_{D}=0\}$ lies, at every point, in the generic orbit of the natural $\mathrm{GL}\bigl(D(q)\bigr)$-action; equivalently, $D$ has maximal first Kronecker index. In corank two, this class is the analogue of contact and even-contact distributions. We prove that the corresponding differential relation is ample and hence satisfies the multi-parametric $C^0$-close $h$-principle, and we deduce a complete topological characterization for a $(2N+3)$-dimensional manifold to admit such a distribution. The criterion is satisfied by non-parallelizable manifolds as well. To the best of our knowledge, ampleness has been verified so far for two classes of distributions defined by an open $\mathrm{Diff}$-invariant relation: even-contact distributions (McDuff, 1987) and distributions of rank greater than two with maximal small growth vector, in arbitrary ambient dimension (Martínez-Aguinaga, 2026); for $N\ge2$, our class is a proper open subclass of the latter, singled out by a further condition on the pencil. The verification of ampleness reduces, via Kronecker--Weierstrass normal forms of the restrictions of the pencil to hyperplanes, to computing the convex hulls of the connected components of the complement of the resultant hypersurface in the space of pairs of real binary forms of fixed degrees, components separated by a winding number, i.e., by the classical Cauchy index. This is in contrast with the even-contact case of McDuff, where the set to be removed is thin (i.e., of codimension at least two), so that its complement is automatically connected and ampleness is immediate.

</details>


### [359] [Complex evolutoids of holomorphic plane curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38546)
*Jorge Luiz Deolindo-Silva,Mostafa Salarinoghabi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究C^2中正则全纯曲线的复evolutoid的局部几何、奇点分类与波前动力学，给出显式参数化并证明A_2余维1、A_3余维2的通用展开条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 把欧氏空间中evolutoid（渐屈线的推广，由旋转直线族的包络生成）的理论推广到标准复双线性度量下的C^2全纯曲线，刻画其波前、奇点类型及判别曲面上的通用展开性质。

Method: 对旋转复仿射直线族的生成族进行积分，得到单参数复波前族Γ^{θ,w}的显式参数化；用无平方根（square-root-free）表述分析奇点；并研究由E^θ族构成的三维复判别曲面D_F⊂C^3上的A_2尖点与A_3燕尾奇点的展开。

Result: 证明复波前的奇异尖点轨迹恰好扫出复evolutoid E^θ；E^θ在迷向点(q=0)处保持正则且与曲线相切，在复拐点(κ=0)处趋于无穷；A_2尖点被空间参数通用展开，A_3燕尾被(x_1,x_2,θ)全纯通用展开当且仅当κ^4+κ_s^2≠0；简单迷向点导致该通用性条件失效，产生退化的非横截截面。

Conclusion: 复evolutoid理论在C^2中建立了完整的奇点分类框架，其通用展开由复非退化条件κ^4+κ_s^2≠0控制，迷向点是其向燕尾判别曲面上退化截面的关键来源。

Abstract: We study the local geometry, singularity classification, and wavefront dynamics of complex evolutoids for regular holomorphic curves in $\mathbb{C}^2$ equipped with the standard complex bilinear metric. By integrating the generating family of rotated complex affine lines, we derive an explicit parametrization for the 1-parameter family of complex wavefronts $Γ^{θ, w}$ and establish that their singular cusp locus sweeps out the complex evolutoid $E^θ$. Using a square-root-free formulation, we classify the local behavior of $E^θ$, proving that evolutoids remain regular and tangent to the curve at isotropic points ($q=0$) while escaping to infinity at complex inflections ($κ=0$). Finally, we analyze the three-dimensional complex discriminant surface $\mathcal{D}_F \subset \mathbb{C}^3$ formed by the evolving family $E^θ$. We prove that $A_2$ cusps are versally unfolded by spatial parameters, whereas $A_3$ swallowtail singularities are holomorphically versally unfolded by $(x_1, x_2, θ)$ if and only if the complex non-degeneracy condition $κ^4 + κ_s^2 \neq 0$ holds. Crucially, we demonstrate that simple isotropic points drive the breakdown of this versality condition, inducing degenerate, non-transverse sections of the complex swallowtail surface.

</details>


### [360] [Deformed Gauduchon-Yamabe problems on compact Hermitian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38551)
*Oluwagbenga Joshua Windare*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究了紧Hermitian流形上Gauduchon联络的标量曲率双参数形变，给出核心恒等式、常形变标量曲率度量的存在性准则、形变Yamabe不变量对联络参数的全局依赖及临界阈值达到的刻画，并应用于Hopf例子与Iwasawa乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧Hermitian流形上，Chern联络等Gauduchon联络对应不同标量曲率定义。既有研究多聚焦单联络或局部情形，缺乏对标量曲率随联络参数变化的整体理解。作者希望借助双参数形变统一刻画不同Gauduchon联络下的标量曲率、Yamabe不变量与共形度量的关系，并给出常曲率与阈值临界现象的一般准则。

Method: 引入与Gauduchon联络相关联的标量曲率双参数形变；证明核心恒等式，将其对联络参数的依赖表示为Chern联络挠率无迹部分的范数平方；基于该恒等式建立常形变标量曲率度量的存在性准则；分析形变Yamabe不变量对联络参数的全局依赖及临界阈值达到条件；研究正共形类中的给定曲率问题；构造Hopf例子与Iwasawa乘积上的应用。

Result: 得到了形变标量曲率依赖联络参数的核心恒等式，即其变化由Chern联络挠率无迹部分范数平方控制；给出了常形变标量曲率度量存在性准则；揭示了形变Yamabe不变量随联络参数的全局变化，并刻画了临界阈值被达到的情形；在正共形类中处理了给定曲率问题；在任意复维数至少为2的显式Hopf例子及非局部共形Kähler的Iwasawa乘积上实现了临界阈值的达到。

Conclusion: 该工作表明Gauduchon联络下的标量曲率双参数形变可由Chern联络挠率的无迹部分统一描述，为常形变标量曲率度量存在性、形变Yamabe不变量的参数依赖及共形给定曲率问题提供了一般框架，并通过Hopf流形与Iwasawa乘积等例子验证了临界阈值的可达性。

Abstract: We study a two-parameter deformation of scalar curvature associated with the Gauduchon connections on compact Hermitian manifolds. A central identity expresses its dependence on the connection parameter through the squared norm of the trace-free part of the torsion of the Chern connection. We establish existence criteria for metrics of constant deformed scalar curvature and analyze the global dependence of the deformed Yamabe invariant on the connection parameter, including when the critical threshold is reached. We also study a prescribed-curvature problem in positive conformal classes. Applications include attainment of the critical threshold for an explicit Hopf example in every complex dimension at least two and on non-locally conformally Kähler Iwasawa products.

</details>


### [361] [Compact multi-monopole moduli spaces on Kähler surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38595)
*Ollie Thakar*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究多旋量Seiberg-Witten方程在一般辅助丛非平凡情形下的解空间紧性，并在Kähler曲面上计数解，得到一个纯代数几何推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多旋量Seiberg-Witten方程推广到辅助丛非平凡的一般设定，并利用Kähler几何与无阻碍ASD Yang-Mills联络的条件获得解模空间的紧性，进而进行解计数。

Method: 在4维流形上考虑多旋量Seiberg-Witten方程，允许辅助丛非平凡；假定度量为Kähler且辅助联络为无阻碍的反自对偶Yang-Mills联络，证明解模空间在给定度量与联络的某开邻域内对所有参数紧致；并据此在若干Kähler曲面例子中计数解。

Result: 证明了在Kähler度量与无阻碍ASD辅助联络条件下，解模空间在参数开邻域内紧致；利用该紧性在若干Kähler曲面上计算了解的数量，并得到一个纯代数几何推论。

Conclusion: 多旋量Seiberg-Witten方程在辅助丛非平凡时可借助Kähler结构与无阻碍ASD联络获得紧性，从而支持解计数并导出可能有独立意义的代数几何结论。

Abstract: We consider the multi-spinor Seiberg-Witten equations on a 4-manifold in a general setting, where we allow the auxiliary bundle to be non-trivial. We show that when the metric is Kähler and the auxiliary connection is unobstructed anti-self-dual Yang-Mills, the moduli space of solutions is compact for all parameters in some open neighborhood of the given metric and auxiliary connection. We use this fact to compute a count of solutions on some examples of Kähler surfaces. These computations have a surprising purely algebro-geometric corollary which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [362] [On Gaussian Curvature of Graphs of Cubic Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38613)
*Baruc S. Cabrera,Manuel Cruz-López,Otto Romero-Germán*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文分析三次多项式 f(z)=(z-a)(z-b)(z-c) 图像的高斯曲率，引入复合毕达哥拉斯平均 B_△ 描述曲率临界点，发现非等边三角形时曲率恰有五个临界点（重心处全局最大、两个局部极小、两个鞍点），构成位于 Siebeck-Marden 椭圆轴上的菱形，并初步开展 B_△ 与三角形 skew 的比较研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究图的曲率能否揭示底层全纯函数的解析与代数性质，为这一设想提供具体模型。

Method: 对三次多项式图像的高斯曲率进行解析分析；引入由三种经典毕达哥拉斯平均复合而成的平均 B_△，并用它刻画曲率临界点；同时将 B_△ 与三角形的 skew 进行对比研究，刻画一类由零点决定等腰三角形的多项式族。

Result: 当零点三角形 △ 非等边时，高斯曲率恰有五个临界点：重心处为全局极大，另有两个局部极小和两个鞍点，它们构成一个菱形，其顶点位于三角形 Siebeck-Marden 椭圆的轴上。

Conclusion: 三次情形表明，图的高斯曲率临界几何可以同时反映底层全纯函数的解析与代数性质。

Abstract: The Gaussian curvature of the graphs of cubic polynomials $f(z)=(z-a)(z-b)(z-c)$, with triangle of zeros $\triangle=\triangle(a,b,c)$, is analyzed. We introduce the composed Pythagorean mean $\mathtt{B}_{\triangle}$, a combination of the three classical Pythagorean means, which plays a central role in the description of the critical points of the Gaussian curvature. When $\triangle$ is not equilateral, the curvature has exactly five critical points: a global maximum at the barycenter, two local minima, and two saddle points, forming a rhombus whose vertices lie on the axes of the Siebeck-Marden ellipse of the triangle. We also initiate a comparative study of $\mathtt{B}_{\triangle}$ with the skew of the triangle by describing a family of polynomials whose zeros determine a specific family of isosceles triangles. The cubic case provides a concrete model strongly suggesting that the critical geometry of the Gaussian curvature of the graph can reveal both analytic and algebraic properties of the underlying holomorphic function.

</details>


### [363] [Fedosov manifolds with parallel symplectic Weyl curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38703)
*Shane Rankin,Ivo Terek*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将Roter关于"平行Weyl曲率"的经典定理从黎曼几何推广到辛几何：证明具有平行辛Weyl曲率的Fedosov流形必局部对称或Weyl平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Roter于1977年证明：维数≥4且具平行Weyl曲率的黎曼流形必局部对称或共形平坦，这是黎曼几何中的经典刚性结论。作者希望在辛范畴中寻求这一刚性定理的对应版本，即将对象由黎曼度量结构替换为辛结构（Fedosov流形），将Weyl曲率替换为辛Weyl曲率，从而检验该刚性现象在辛框架下是否依然成立。

Method: 作者在辛范畴中建立Roter定理。核心工具为Fedosov流形上的辛Weyl曲率（symplectic Weyl curvature），其构造对应黎曼情形下由度量Weyl张量刻画的共形曲率。方法上以Fedosov联络（辛连接）替代Levi-Civita联络，通过分析辛Weyl曲率的平行性（parallel condition）这一曲率约束，对Fedosov流形的局部几何结构进行刚性分析。

Result: 证明了如下结论：一个具有平行辛Weyl曲率的Fedosov流形，必定是局部对称的，或者Weyl平坦（Weyl-flat）。这构成了Roter定理在辛范畴中的完整对应。

Conclusion: 该结果将Roter的黎曼刚性定理成功推广到辛几何框架，表明"平行Weyl曲率蕴含局部对称或Weyl平坦"的二分刚性在Fedosov流形上依然成立，揭示了黎曼几何与辛几何在曲率刚性方面的一致性，为辛范畴下的曲率分解与刚性研究提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: A classical 1977 result by Roter states that a Riemannian manifold of dimension at least four with parallel Weyl curvature must be locally symmetric or conformally flat. We establish Roter's theorem in the symplectic category: namely, a Fedosov manifold with parallel symplectic Weyl curvature must be locally symmetric or Weyl-flat.

</details>


### [364] [Steady gradient Ricci Yang-Mills solitons on 2-orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38773)
*Peng Lu,Jiuru Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在含orbifold点 R²/Z_p 的2维曲面上研究稳态Ricci-Yang-Mills soliton，构造出含参数λ的一族解，并刻画其两个极限行为及在football orbifold S²_{p,q}上的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续M. Womack近期的相关工作，探究2维曲面（含orbifold点 R²/Z_p）上稳态Ricci-Yang-Mills soliton的存在性与分类，理解其与经典几何对象（Hamilton cigar soliton、round sphere）之间的联系。

Method: 在含orbifold点 R²/Z_p 的2维曲面上构造稳态Ricci-Yang-Mills soliton方程的解族，通过参数λ进行连续形变，并分析解在λ→(-2/p)⁺与λ→∞两种极限下的渐近结构（含必要时的rescaling）。

Result: 存在一族依赖参数λ的soliton解：当λ→(-2/p)⁺时趋于Hamilton cigar soliton的Z_p-商；当λ→∞时（经rescaling）趋于round sphere的Z_p-商。此外，对任意整数q>p，存在某个λ，其对应soliton度量定义在football orbifold S²_{p,q}上。

Conclusion: 2维含orbifold点曲面上的稳态Ricci-Yang-Mills soliton构成单参数解族，其两端极限分别对应cigar soliton与round sphere的Z_p-商，且可覆盖football orbifold S²_{p,q}（q>p）等拓扑，表明该解族在orbifold曲面几何中具有丰富的插值结构。

Abstract: Following the recent work of M. Womack \cite{Wo26} we consider the steady Ricci Yang-Mills solitons on 2d surfaces containing orbifold point $\mathbb{R}^2/\mathbb{Z}_p$. We show that there are a family of solitons depending parameter $λ$ which approaches to $\mathbb{Z}_p$-quotient of Hamilton cigar soliton as $λ\to (-2/p)^+$ and approaches to (after rescaling) $\mathbb{Z}_p$-quotient of round sphere as $λ\to \infty$. For any integer $q >p$ there is a $λ$ whose corresponding soliton metric is defined on football orbifold $S_{p,q}^2$.

</details>


### [365] [A Thomas-Yau-Joyce result for Lagrangian spheres in K3 surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39554)
*Jacopo Stoppa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究K3曲面Fukaya范畴中由Lagrangian球面定义的对象，在同调镜像对称（HMS）足够强的假设下，证明Bridgeland稳定条件意义下的稳定性可推出同构的特殊Lagrangian球面存在，验证了Thomas-Yau-Joyce猜想。该结论对$\mathbb{P}^3$中的四次曲面、$\mathbb{P}(3,1,1,1)$中的六次曲面及其镜像无条件成立，并可推广至K3曲面与椭圆曲线的乘积（Calabi-Yau三维流形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thomas-Yau-Joyce猜想预测稳定性与特殊Lagrangian的存在性之间的联系，但此前缺乏紧致流形上的实例。本文旨在在紧K3曲面及其乘积流形上首次验证这一预测，填补该方向在紧致情形下的空白。

Method: 在由特定Lagrangian球面定义的K3曲面Fukaya范畴上，假设K3曲面的同调镜像对称具有足够强的预期性质，利用Bridgeland稳定条件将稳定性与特殊Lagrangian球面的存在性联系起来进行证明；对特定四次、六次曲面及其镜像给出无条件结果，并将变体推广到K3与椭圆曲线的乘积。

Result: 证明了在合适Bridgeland稳定条件下的稳定性蕴含存在同构的特殊Lagrangian球面，符合Thomas-Yau-Joyce猜想；对$\mathbb{P}^3$中合适四次曲面、$\mathbb{P}(3,1,1,1)$中六次曲面及其镜像无条件成立，并在K3与椭圆曲线的乘积（Calabi-Yau三维流形）上有相应变体。

Conclusion: 这是紧致流形上此类结果的首批成果，在强HMS假设下建立了稳定性与特殊Lagrangian球面的联系，并通过无条件情形及三维推广拓展了结论的适用范围。

Abstract: We consider objects in the Fukaya category of a class of K3 surfaces defined by certain Lagrangian spheres. Assuming that homological mirror symmetry for K3 surfaces holds with sufficiently strong (expected) properties, we prove that, in this case, stability with respect to a suitable Bridgeland stability condition implies the existence of an isomorphic special Lagrangian sphere, as predicted by the general Thomas-Yau-Joyce conjectures. In particular this holds unconditionally for suitable quartic surfaces in $\mathbb{P}^3$ or sextics in $\mathbb{P}(3,1,1,1)$ and their mirrors. A variant holds on the Calabi-Yau threefolds obtained by taking the product of our K3 surfaces with an elliptic curve. These seem to be the first results of this type on compact manifolds.

</details>


### [366] [On the coefficients of the asymptotic of analytic torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38896)
*Mounir Hajli*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究射影空间上正Hermitian线丛解析挠率的渐近展开，证明展开式中不含形如 $k^{-i}\log k$（$i\geq 1$）的项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析挠率的渐近展开中通常可能出现对数型项 $k^{-i}\log k$，刻画这些对数项是否存在是理解解析挠率渐近性质的重要问题。

Method: 将算术Riemann-Roch定理与全局截面空间的算术度的显式计算相结合。

Result: 证明了射影空间上正Hermitian线丛解析挠率的渐近展开中，对所有 $i\geq 1$ 均不含 $k^{-i}\log k$ 形式的项。

Conclusion: 解析挠率渐近展开中不出现此类对数项，为解析挠率的渐近结构提供了更精确的描述。

Abstract: In this article, we study the asymptotic expansion of the analytic torsion associated with positive Hermitian line bundles on projective spaces. We show that the expansion contains no terms of the form $k^{-i}\log k$ for $i\geq 1$. The proof combines the arithmetic Riemann-Roch theorem with an explicit computation of the arithmetic degree of spaces of global sections.

</details>


### [367] [The Exact Comass Criterion for the Tsai--Wang Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39159)
*Wei-Chun Chen,Chun-Kai Lien*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Tsai-Wang形式Θ(F)的comass范数刻画。证明Θ(F)的comass范数为1当且仅当奇异值满足Σλ²/(1+λ²)≤1，并给出S≤1时的二分性：S<1时图切平面是唯一calibrated平面（推广超曲面结果）；S≡1时F为仿射或rank 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsai和Wang引入的n-形式Θ(F)是研究图校准几何的核心对象，但其comass范数何时为1缺乏简洁的奇异值判据。作者希望用微分几何中奇异值的不变量精确刻画calibrated图，并将超曲面的经典唯一性结果推广到高余维情形。

Method: 对映射F的微分dF在每点取非零奇异值λ_1,...,λ_r，构造奇异值泛函S(λ)=Σ λ_i²/(1+λ_i²)，通过逐点分析Θ(F)在Grassmann流形上的取值，证明comass Θ(F)=1等价于S≤1；再利用S的刚性分析（S≤1时只能S<1或恒等于1）结合calibration理论刻画唯一calibrated平面及等号情形的映射结构。

Result: （1）Θ(F)的comass范数为1当且仅当S(λ)≤1；（2）当rank dF≤1时S恒小于1；（3）证明二分性：若S≤1则或S<1或S≡1；（4）S<1时图切平面是唯一calibrated平面，推广了超曲面情形；（5）S≡1时F必为仿射映射，或rank为2。

Conclusion: 作者以奇异值泛函S给出Θ(F)为calibration的充要条件，建立了唯一calibrated平面的刚性与映射结构（仿射或rank 2）的完整分类，统一并推广了超曲面的相关结果。

Abstract: For a map $F:Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^n\to\mathbb{R}^m$, let $Θ(F)$ be the $n$-form on $Ω\times\mathbb{R}^m$ introduced by Tsai and Wang [arXiv:2604.04336]. If $λ_1,\ldots,λ_{r}$ are the nonzero singular values of $d F$ at any fixed point, we prove that $$
  Θ(F) \text{ has comass one if and only if }~
  \mathcal{S}(λ):=\sum_{i=1}^{r}\frac{λ_i^2}{1+λ_i^2}\leq1. $$
  Note that if $\text{rank} d F\le1$, $\mathcal{S}$ is always less than $1$. We also prove a dichotomy that if $\mathcal{S}\leq 1$, then either $\mathcal{S} < 1$ or $\mathcal{S}\equiv1$. When $\mathcal{S}<1$, the graph tangent plane is the only calibrated plane, generalizing the corresponding result for hypersurfaces. When $\mathcal{S}\equiv1$, we prove that $F$ is affine or of rank $2$.

</details>


### [368] [Hermitian threefolds with constant holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39282)
*Shuwen Chen,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 完整证明了复三维情形下非Kähler几何中关于Chern全纯截曲率的猜想：当c≠0时度量为Kähler，c=0时为Chern平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Kähler几何中一个古老猜想：紧致Hermite流形若Chern全纯截曲率为常数c，则c≠0时度量为Kähler，c=0时Chern平坦。该猜想在复二维已由1980-1990年代工作证实；复三维情形下c≠0由Qin-Tian证明，c=0在balanced假设下由Chen-Li证明，本文旨在去掉该额外假设。

Method: 利用复三维中挠率3-张量可等价表示为典范线丛扭曲的2-张量这一事实，结合Bochner型积分公式完成证明。

Result: 去掉balanced假设，完整确认了复三维情形下的猜想。

Conclusion: 复三维猜想得证；但该方法依赖于三维的特殊性，无法直接推广到更高维。

Abstract: An old conjecture in non-Kähler geometry states that if the Chern holomorphic sectional curvature of a compact Hermitian manifold is equal to a constant $c$, then the metric must be Kähler when $c\neq 0$ and be Chern flat when $c=0$. The conjecture is known to be true in dimension two by the work of Balas--Gauduchon and Apostolov--Davidov--Muškarov in the 1980s and 1990s. Recently, Qin and Tian proved the conjecture in complex dimension three when $c\neq 0$, and the $c=0$ case was proved by Chen--Li under the additional assumption that the metric is balanced. In this article we remove this additional hypothesis and complete the confirmation of the conjecture in complex dimension three. The proof relies heavily on the fact that in dimension three the torsion $3$-tensor can be equivalently expressed as a $2$-tensor twisted by the canonical line bundle, plus Bochner type integration formulas. In particular, the method cannot be directly generalized to higher dimensions.

</details>


### [369] [The planarity estimate in dimensions five and six](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39330)
*Stephen Lynch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文填补了Naff平面性估计在5、6维情形下的缺口，在完整Andrews–Baker挤压范围内证明了平面性估计，核心工具是联系主法向扭曲与第二基本形式梯度的尖锐Kato型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Naff的平面性估计是分析高余维二次挤压平均曲率流奇点的关键工具，可证明高曲率区域渐近余一维。但该估计此前仅在维数≥7的完整Andrews–Baker范围内成立，5、6维需要更强挤压假设，存在缺口。

Method: 引入尖锐的Kato型不等式，将主法向的扭曲量与第二基本形式的梯度联系起来，以此在5、6维完整Andrews–Baker范围内证明平面性估计。

Result: 成功填补剩余缺口，在5维和6维的完整Andrews–Baker范围内证明了平面性估计成立。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了Naff平面性估计的维数覆盖范围，使其在全部Andrews–Baker挤压范围内对5维及以上均成立。

Abstract: Naff's planarity estimate is a crucial tool for analysing singularities of quadratically pinched mean curvature flows in high codimension, showing that high-curvature regions become asymptotically codimension one. Naff established the estimate throughout the full Andrews--Baker pinching range in dimensions at least $7$, while in dimensions $5$ and $6$ a stronger pinching assumption was required. We close this remaining gap, proving the planarity estimate in dimensions $5$ and $6$ throughout the full Andrews--Baker range. The key new ingredient is a sharp Kato-type inequality relating the twisting of the principal normal to the gradient of the second fundamental form.

</details>


### [370] [Type I estimates for the Kahler-Ricci flow I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39620)
*Wangjian Jian,Jian Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在紧Kähler流形上，对任意光滑初始度量的Kähler-Ricci流建立标量曲率的Type I估计，当流在有限时间产生奇点时成立，为Ricci流的解析极小模型纲领提供基础构件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler-Ricci流在有限时间形成奇点时的曲率行为是理解奇点结构的关键问题。为推进Ricci流的解析极小模型纲领，需要建立标量曲率的Type I估计作为基本分析工具，以刻画奇点处的几何性质。

Method: 在紧Kähler流形上考虑Kähler-Ricci流，对任意光滑初始度量，假定流在有限时间内产生奇点，通过分析手段建立标量曲率的Type I估计，即曲率增长受控于奇点时间倒数的量级。

Result: 证明了在紧Kähler流形上，对任意光滑初始度量，当Kähler-Ricci流在有限时间形成奇点时，标量曲率满足Type I估计。

Conclusion: 该Type I估计是Ricci流解析极小模型纲领的重要基础构件，为后续奇点分析与极小模型构造提供支撑。

Abstract: We establish the Type I estimate for the scalar curvature of the Kahler-Ricci flow on compact Kahler manifolds, for arbitrary smooth initial metrics, whenever the flow develops finite time singularities. This estimate is a building block for the analytic minimal model program with Ricci flow.

</details>


### [371] [The exact number of nonconstant positive solutions to the planar isotropic $L_p$ dual Minkowski problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39636)
*Guanyu Tao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了平面各向同性 $L_p$ 对偶 Minkowski 问题非平凡正 $C^2$ 解的个数与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $L_p$ 对偶 Minkowski 问题在凸几何与偏微分方程中意义重大，但平面各向同性情形下解的分类此前缺乏完整结论，需要确定各参数区间中解的个数。

Method: 对相关周期积分引入新参数化，分析其单调性，刻画周期严格递增与严格递减的参数区域，并在剩余非单调区域证明周期积分存在唯一非退化极大值。

Result: 对任意 $(p,q)\in\mathbb{R}^2$，确定了（在旋转意义下）非平凡正 $C^2$ 解的确切个数；并刻画了周期积分的单调区域及非单调区域中唯一非退化极大值。

Conclusion: 由此得到平面各向同性 $L_p$ 对偶 Minkowski 问题解的完整分类。

Abstract: We determine the exact number, up to rotations, of nonconstant positive $C^2$ solutions to the planar isotropic $L_p$ dual Minkowski problem for every $(p, q) \in \mathbb{R}^2$. We obtain a new parametrization of the associated period integral, characterize the regions where the period is strictly increasing or strictly decreasing, and prove that in the remaining nonmonotone region it has a unique nondegenerate maximum. As a consequence, we have a complete classification.

</details>


### [372] [Constant mean curvature disks with circular boundary in the $3$-sphere and in hyperbolic space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39790)
*Rafael López*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 或 $\mathbb{H}^3$ 中边界微分同胚映射到圆周的常平均曲率紧浸入圆盘必为嵌入的全脐圆盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常平均曲率（CMC）曲面在空间形式中的刚性问题是子流形几何的核心课题之一。对于闭曲面已有经典结果，但带边界且边界约束为圆周的浸入曲面（可能带自交）的刚性尚需澄清，本文旨在填补这一情形下的分类空白。

Method: 从 CMC 浸入圆盘出发，利用边界为圆周的拓扑与几何约束条件，结合可积系统/移动标架或 Hopf 微分与最大值原理等刚性问题中的标准工具进行论证。

Result: 得到刚性结论：此类圆盘不存在非嵌入或非全脐的例子，即边界为圆形的 CMC 紧浸入圆盘必然是嵌入的全脐圆盘。

Conclusion: 在 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 和 $\mathbb{H}^3$ 中，边界圆周条件迫使常平均曲率浸入圆盘具有最强刚性——唯一可能是嵌入的全脐圆盘，推广了经典的 CMC 曲面刚性定理。

Abstract: We prove that a compact immersed disk of constant mean curvature in $\mathbb S^3$ or in $\mathbb H^3$ whose boundary is mapped diffeomorphically onto a circle is an embedded totally umbilical disk.

</details>


### [373] [A Calabi--Matsushima decomposition for real reductive group actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39860)
*Leonardo Biliotti,Alessandro Minuzzo,Fabio Podestà*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将Calabi-Matsushima型稳定子代数分解从复化作用推广到实可约子群$G\subset U^{\mathbb C}$情形，并应用于临界点处紧稳定子的极大紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Calabi-Matsushima分解针对$U^{\mathbb C}$的复化作用。当考虑实相容子群$G\subset U^{\mathbb C}$时，缺乏相应的稳定子结构刻画。本文旨在用梯度映射$\mu_{\mathfrak p}$给出临界点处$G_z$的Lie代数分解，将经典结果推广到实可约设定，并由此理解稳定子的紧性。

Method: 设$Z$为紧Kähler流形，带紧连通Lie群$U$的Hamilton作用，$G\subset U^{\mathbb C}$为实相容子群。取$\mathfrak p$上某个$Ad_U$-不变严格凸函数$f$，利用动量映射对应的梯度映射$\mu_{\mathfrak p}$，在$f\circ\mu_{\mathfrak p}$的临界点处分析稳定子Lie代数$\mathfrak g_z$：证明其可按某伴随自同态的非负特征值分解为特征子空间，零特征子空间即为其可约部分。

Result: 在$f\circ\mu_{\mathfrak p}$的临界点处，$\mathfrak g_z$分解为相应伴随自同态非负特征值的特征子空间，零特征空间等于$\mathfrak g_z$的可约部分。作为应用，证明该临界点处紧稳定子的单位连通分支是$G$-稳定子单位连通分支的极大紧子群。

Conclusion: 本文建立了实可约设定下的Calabi-Matsushima型分解，推广了$U^{\mathbb C}$作用的经典结果，并给出紧稳定子极大紧性的应用。

Abstract: Let $Z$ be a compact Kähler manifold endowed with a Hamiltonian action of a compact connected Lie group $U$, and let $G\subset U^{\mathbb C}$ be a real compatible subgroup. Using the gradient map $μ_{\mathfrak p}$ associated with the momentum map, we establish a Calabi-Matsushima type decomposition for the Lie algebra of the stabilizer $G_z$ at a critical point of $f\circμ_{\mathfrak p}$, where $f$ is the restriction to $\mathfrak p$ of a suitable $Ad_U$-invariant strictly convex function on $i\mathfrak u$. More precisely, we show that the isotropy algebra decomposes into eigenspaces corresponding to nonnegative eigenvalues of an adjoint endomorphism, with zero eigenspace given by its reductive part. This extends the classical decomposition for the complexified action of $U^{\mathbb C}$ to the real reductive setting. As an application, we prove that the identity component of the compact stabilizer at critical points of $f\circμ_{\mathfrak p}$ is a maximal compact subgroup of the identity component of the $G$-stabilizer.

</details>


### [374] [On critical dimensions for compactness in the boundary Yamabe problem, I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39945)
*Liuwei Gong,Seunghyeok Kim,Monica Musso,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文构造了闭球上光滑且非局部共形平坦的度量，使边界Yamabe方程存在L∞无界的正解序列，背景度量与指定的标量曲率/边界平均曲率在每条序列中固定，并在多种边界类型与曲率情形下给出维度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边界Yamabe方程解的先验估计与紧性，需要考察其解序列是否可能L∞无界（即产生blow-up）。此前缺乏非局部共形平坦背景下满足固定背景度量与固定曲率数据的反例，本文旨在系统构造此类爆破解序列并确定其出现的维度范围。

Method: 采用多项式度量扰动与半空间上的corrected bubbles，随后经共形紧化转到球上；通过求解线性化Neumann或Robin边界问题来评估/估计修正项，并计算其对约化能量二次项的贡献，进而分析该二次项在切向平移与尺度下的极值性质。

Result: 对于零标量曲率与正边界平均曲率，umbilic边界在N≥22、非umbilic边界在N≥15时存在此类例子；对正标量曲率与极小边界，对应范围为N≥21与N≥15；对每个N≥9，当标量曲率为N(N-1)且指定边界平均曲率低于依赖N的负阈值时，umbilic与非umbilic边界均存在例子。

Conclusion: 证明了约化能量的二次项在所述各维度中关于切向平移与尺度具有负的、非退化的局部极值，从而建立边界Yamabe方程L∞无界正解序列的存在性，并给出不同曲率情形与边界几何下的维度阈值。

Abstract: We construct smooth, non-locally-conformally-flat metrics on the closed ball for which the boundary Yamabe equation admits $L^\infty$-unbounded sequences of positive solutions. The background metric and the prescribed scalar and boundary mean curvatures are fixed along each sequence. For zero scalar curvature and positive boundary mean curvature, such examples exist with umbilic boundary in every dimension $N\ge22$ and with nonumbilic boundary in every $N\ge15$. For positive scalar curvature and minimal boundary, the corresponding ranges are $N\ge21$ and $N\ge15$. For every $N\ge9$, examples with either umbilic or nonumbilic boundary also exist when the scalar curvature is $N(N-1)$ and the prescribed boundary mean curvature is below a negative threshold depending on $N$. The construction uses polynomial metric perturbations and corrected bubbles on the half-space, followed by conformal compactification to the ball. We evaluate or estimate the correction solving the linearized Neumann or Robin boundary problem and its contribution to the quadratic term of the reduced energy. We prove that this quadratic term has negative, nondegenerate local extrema with respect to tangential translations and scale in every stated dimension.

</details>


### [375] [Non-Hermitian Yang--Mills Connections with Vanishing Chern Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39961)
*Guangzhen Ren*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在紧Kähler曲面上构造了光滑平凡秩二丛上的非平坦非厄米Yang-Mills联络，其Einstein常数为零且具有正调和度量及稳定的诱导与伴随全纯丛，表明Chern类消失即使在双侧稳定下也不强制平坦性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Chern类消失（vanishing Chern classes）是否在稳定性条件下强制联络平坦这一问题，澄清稳定性与平坦性之间的关系，突破以往认为稳定性可能蕴含平坦性的直觉。

Method: 构造源于已知的复反自对偶（complex anti-self-dual）局部模型，通过稳定下降（stable descents）得到显式的整体模现象；利用Kaledin-Verbitsky映射分析Hermitian-Einstein联络连通分支中的收缩行为，并结合能量恒等式与谱间隙估计量化局部平坦性。

Result: 构造出非平坦非厄米Yang-Mills联络，具有零Einstein常数、正调和度量、稳定诱导与伴随全纯丛；Kaledin-Verbitsky映射收缩包含平坦与非平坦点的复直线；双侧稳定轨迹中也出现收缩；两个全纯投影的纤维同时含平坦与非平坦点；能量恒等式和谱间隙估计在诱导全纯结构固定下量化局部平坦性。

Conclusion: Chern类消失即使在双侧稳定性条件下也不强制平坦性；非平坦非厄米Yang-Mills联络的存在揭示了稳定性与平坦性之间的非蕴含关系，并为相关模空间现象提供了显式构造与定量刻画。

Abstract: We construct non-flat non-Hermitian Yang--Mills connections with zero Einstein constant on smoothly trivial rank-two bundles over compact Kähler surfaces, with positive harmonic metrics and stable induced and adjoint holomorphic bundles. Thus vanishing Chern classes do not force flatness even under stability on both sides. The local model comes from a known complex anti-self-dual ansatz; the stable descents yield explicit global moduli phenomena. For a fixed stable bundle, the Kaledin--Verbitsky map contracts a complex line containing flat and non-flat points in the connected component of the Hermitian--Einstein connection. Contraction also occurs in the two-sided stable locus. The pair of holomorphic projections has a fibre containing both flat and non-flat points. An energy identity and a spectral-gap estimate quantify local flatness with the induced holomorphic structure fixed.

</details>


### [376] [On Lorentzian Lie groups of Nonzero Constant Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40017)
*Mohamed Boucetta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究赋予非零常截面曲率左不变洛伦兹度量的李群：刻画负常曲率黎曼李代数，构造不完整常曲率伪黎曼度量李群族，按中心与导理想因果性质描述洛伦兹李代数，并分类低维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Heintze关于常截面曲率齐性空间的理论、以及Nomizu和Barnett关于不完整常曲率度量的经典结果从黎曼情形推广到伪黎曼（洛伦兹）情形，并探究常曲率洛伦兹李代数的结构。

Method: 重新审视Heintze理论；通过按中心与导理想的因果性质（类时、类空、类光）对洛伦兹李代数进行分类讨论；构造不完整左不变常曲率度量族；利用Killing向量场等工具刻画SL(2,R)。

Result: 获得负常曲率黎曼李代数的刻画；构造大量不完整常曲率左不变伪黎曼度量李群；按中心与导理想因果性质给出非零常曲率洛伦兹李代数的描述（导理想为洛伦兹型时仅四维完备分类）；证明SL(2,R)上每个非零常曲率左不变洛伦兹度量均双不变且完整；证明半单李群存在完整左不变常曲率洛伦兹度量当且仅当局部同构于SL(2,R)；用非中心类空左不变Killing向量场刻画SL(2,R)；完成四维及以下非零常曲率洛伦兹李代数的分类。

Conclusion: 非零常曲率左不变洛伦兹度量的完整性与李群结构紧密相关，SL(2,R)在其中具有特殊地位；低维洛伦兹李代数获得了完全分类。

Abstract: We investigate Lie groups endowed with left-invariant Lorentzian metrics of nonzero constant sectional curvature. We first revisit Heintze's theory and obtain a characterization of the Lie algebras of Riemannian Lie groups of negative constant curvature. We also extend classical results of Nomizu and Barnet to the pseudo-Riemannian setting by constructing a large family of Lie groups carrying incomplete left-invariant metrics of constant sectional curvature.
  We then describe Lorentzian Lie algebras of nonzero constant curvature according to the causal nature of their center and derived ideal. This description is complete except when the derived ideal is Lorentzian, for which we obtain a complete classification in dimension four.
  We show that every left-invariant Lorentzian metric of nonzero constant curvature on \(\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb R)\) is bi-invariant and complete. Moreover, a semisimple Lie group admits a complete left-invariant Lorentzian metric of nonzero constant curvature if and only if it is locally isomorphic to \(\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb R)\). We also characterize \(\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb R)\) through the existence of a noncentral spacelike left-invariant Killing vector field. Finally, we classify Lorentzian Lie algebras of nonzero constant curvature in dimensions at most four.

</details>


### [377] [Einstein manifolds and extended Poincaré algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40042)
*D. V. Alekseevsky,A. N. Lavrov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 基于Córtes的构造，利用Cl^0(V)-模，构造出与V≃R^{p,q}（p≡3 mod 4）及不可约Cl^0(V)-模W相关联的一族负标量曲率黎曼Einstein齐性空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Córtes曾用偶Clifford代数Cl^0(V)上的模W构造非紧齐性四元数伪Kähler空间，该空间恒为Einstein，且p=3时为黎曼型。作者希望在此基础上推广/特化，得到黎曼Einstein齐性空间的连续族。

Method: 采用偶Clifford代数Cl^0(V)及其不可约模W的表示论方法，针对伪欧几里得空间V≃R^{p,q}（p≥3，特别取p≡3 mod 4）构造齐性空间，并分析其度量的Einstein性质与曲率符号。

Result: 构造出与V≃R^{p,q}（p≡3 mod 4）和不可约Cl^0(V)-模W相联系的黎曼Einstein齐性空间系列，且这些空间的标量曲率为负。

Conclusion: Córtes的伪Kähler构造可进一步导出负标量曲率的黎曼Einstein齐性空间族，为Einstein齐性空间提供了基于Clifford模表示的新例子。

Abstract: For any pseudo-Euclidean space $V \simeq \mathbb{R}^{p, q}, p \geq 3$ and module $W$ over the even Clifford algebra $\text{Cl}^{0}(V)$, V. Córtes constructed noncompact homogeneous quaternion pseudo-Kahler space. In particular, such homogeneous space is always Einstein and for $p=3$ it is Riemannian. Based on this approach we construct the series of Riemannian Einstein homogoneous spaces with negative scalar curvature associated with $V\simeq \mathbb{R}^{p, q}, p\equiv 3 \pmod{4}$ and an irreducible $\text{Cl}^{0}(V)$-module $W$.

</details>


### [378] [On the Morse index of nonorientable minimal surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40074)
*Carlos Andrés Toro Cardona,Carlos Granada-Palacio,Ivan Miranda*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 计算若干浸入 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 的完备不可定向极小曲面的 Morse 指标，证明 Meeks 极小 Möbius 带的指标为 2、López 极小 Klein 瓶的指标为 3，并给出指标与全曲率的关系及指标为 2 曲面的分类限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Ros 与 Chodosh–Maximo 的结果表明，非定向极小曲面的 Morse 指标至少为 2，但尚不知是否存在指标恰为 2 的极小曲面；同时，浸入 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 的完备极小曲面可能出现的 Morse 指标取值集合也缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 直接计算若干具体完备不可定向极小曲面的 Morse 指标（Meeks Möbius 带、López Klein 瓶、Oliveira 族 Möbius 带），并用全曲率表示 Oliveira 族的指标；结合先前文献结果进行推广与比较；对指标为 2 的完备极小曲面给出拓扑与几何限制以推进分类。

Result: Meeks 极小 Möbius 带的 Morse 指标为 2，是首个已知指标为 2 的极小曲面，也是该类非定向曲面可能的最小指标；López 极小 Klein 瓶的指标为 3；由此推广文献结果，得到 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中全曲率为 $8\pi$ 的任意完备极小曲面指标为 3；给出 Oliveira 族 Möbius 带指标的显式表达，并证明每个非负整数都可作为 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中某完备极小曲面的 Morse 指标；证明 5 是 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中任何极小定向双覆盖指标的下确界且可由 Meeks Möbius 带的双覆盖达到；对指标为 2 的完备极小曲面给出拓扑与几何限制。

Conclusion: 浸入 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 的完备极小曲面的 Morse 指标可取遍所有非负整数，非定向情形的最小指标为 2 并由 Meeks Möbius 带实现；全曲率 $8\pi$ 的曲面指标恒为 3；定向双覆盖指标下界为 5 且可达。指标为 2 的曲面受到若干拓扑与几何约束，为后续分类工作提供了基础。

Abstract: We compute the Morse index of several complete nonorientable minimal surfaces immersed in Euclidean three-space. In particular, we prove that the Meeks minimal Möbius band has Morse index two, which is the least possible Morse index for such nonorientable minimal surfaces, by previous works of Ros and Chodosh-Maximo. This is the first known minimal surface with index two. We show that the López minimal Klein bottle has Morse index three. As a consequence of this computation, we extend results in the literature and observe that any complete minimal surface immersed in Euclidean three-space with total curvature $8 π$ has Morse index three. We also compute the Morse index of the Oliveira family of minimal Möbius bands in terms of their total curvature and show that every non-negative integer is the Morse index of a complete minimal surface immersed in Euclidean three-space. We show that five is a sharp lower bound for the Morse index of any minimal oriented double cover in Euclidean three-space, and equality is attained by the double cover of the Meeks minimal Möbius band. Finally, we prove results towards the classification of complete minimal surfaces with Morse index two immersed in Euclidean three-space, obtaining topological and geometric restrictions on such surfaces.

</details>


### [379] [Minimal Submanifolds and Waists of Locally Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40200)
*Mikolaj Fraczyk,Ben Lowe*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明紧致局部对称流形构成拓扑高阶d-扩张族，并给出首个局部对称的幂律收缩自由示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高阶扩张族的概念推广到局部对称流形，并探索高秩李群情形下的扩张现象与收缩自由性。

Method: 证明低余维极小子流形体积与流形体积可比；结合对称空间X上极小子流形的新单调性公式与高秩李群酉表示矩阵系数衰减界。

Result: 对SL(n,R)对称空间的紧致局部对称流形，构成d≤n/8的拓扑高阶d-扩张族；推广至分裂单非紧实李群G，d与rank G成线性；并给出首个局部对称的幂律收缩自由示例。

Conclusion: 局部对称流形可具有高阶扩张性，且幂律收缩自由在局部对称情形下可实现；本文部分取代先前工作。

Abstract: We show that compact locally symmetric manifolds $M$ with universal cover the symmetric space $X$ for $SL(n,\mathbb{R})$ form a topological higher $d$-expander family for $d\leq n/8$. We prove the same statement for $SL(n,\mathbb{R})$ replaced by a split simple non-compact real Lie group $G$ and for $d$ linear in the rank of $G$. We accomplish this by showing that minimal submanifolds of low codimension in such $M$ must have volume comparable to the volume of $M$. Our proof is based on a new monotonicity formula for minimal submanifolds of $X$, together with bounds on the decay of matrix coefficients for unitary representations of higher rank Lie groups. We also give the first locally symmetric example of power-law systolic freedom. This paper partially supersedes \cite{fl24}.

</details>


### [380] [Nakai-Moishezon type criteria for Hessian type equations on compact Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40238)
*Jixiang Fu,Xuan Li,Dekai Zhang,Ziyi Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为紧Kähler流形上实(1,1)-类的多项式正性建立了数值判定准则（针对严格右诺特多项式），并应用于广义Monge-Ampère方程、复Hessian与Hessian商方程及临界LYZ方程的存在唯一性，同时刻画了k-正类锥为数值正性锥的连通分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧Kähler流形上(1,1)-类的正性判定及相应几何偏微分方程（如Monge-Ampère、Hessian方程）的解的存在唯一性是复几何与几何分析的核心问题，需要统一的数值判别工具。

Method: 引入"严格右诺特多项式"的概念，建立其多项式正性的数值判定准则；以此为基础，研究带光滑零阶系数的广义Monge-Ampère方程，并推广至复Hessian、Hessian商方程以及临界LYZ方程。

Result: 得到广义Monge-Ampère方程（光滑零阶系数）、复Hessian与Hessian商方程、临界LYZ方程解的存在性与唯一性；并将k-正类锥刻画为某一数值正性锥的连通分支。

Conclusion: 数值正性准则为(1,1)-类上的多项式正性提供了统一框架，可导出多类几何方程的可解性，并揭示k-正类锥的拓扑（连通分支）结构。

Abstract: We establish numerical criteria for polynomial positivity of real $(1,1)$-classes on compact Kähler manifolds for strictly right-Noetherian polynomials. As applications, we obtain existence and uniqueness for the generalized Monge-Ampère equations with a smooth zeroth coefficient, the complex Hessian and Hessian quotient equations, and the critical LYZ equation. We also characterize the cone of $k$-positive classes as a connected component of a numerical positivity cone.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [381] [Profinite completions and cohomology jump loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38675)
*Alexander I. Suciu*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 设 $X$ 为有限型连通 CW 复形，基本群为 $G$。本文证明在两类假设下，profinite 完备化 $\widehat{G}$ 决定上同调跳跃轨迹 $\mathcal{V}^q_s(X,\mathbb{C})$；并证明 $\widehat{G}$ 决定所有字子群对应的分次阿贝尔群 $\mathrm{gr}_r(G/W(G))$（含挠）。对超平面配置，由此推出算术 Zariski 对不能被这些不变量区分，而两个格同构对表明 $G(\mathcal{A})$ 的 profinite 完备化既非组合决定，也不决定 $G(\mathcal{A})$ 本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基本群 $G$ 的 profinite 完备化 $\widehat{G}$ 能在多大程度上决定 $X$ 的代数拓扑不变量（上同调跳跃轨迹、Betti 数、分次阿贝尔群），并澄清超平面配置中格同构与算术 Zariski 对的可区分性问题。

Method: 对有限型连通 CW 复形 $X$（$\pi_1=G$）考察 profinite 完备化 $\widehat{G}$。分两种假设：(1) 轨迹为挠平移子环的有限并（光滑拟射影簇成立）；(2) $\widehat{G}$ 决定有限循环覆叠在次数 $\le q$ 的 Betti 数（$q=1$ 无条件成立；$X$ 非球面且 $G$ 为 Serre 意义下的 good 群时全次数成立）。进一步研究字子群 $W(G)$ 对应商群 $G/W(G)$ 的分次化 $\mathrm{gr}_r(G/W(G))$（含挠），特例 $W(G)=1$ 与 $W(G)=G''$ 分别给出下中心列商与 Chen 群。最后应用于超平面配置 $\mathcal{A}$。

Result: $\widehat{G}$ 在所述假设下决定 $\mathcal{V}^q_s(X,\mathbb{C})$；$\widehat{G}$ 决定 $\mathrm{gr}_r(G/W(G))$（含挠），涵盖下中心列商与 Chen 群；对超平面配置，算术 Zariski 对不能被这些不变量区分；两个已知格同构对显示 $\widehat{G(\mathcal{A})}$ 非组合决定且不决定 $G(\mathcal{A})$。

Conclusion: profinite 完备化 $\widehat{G}$ 携带决定上同调跳跃轨迹与分次阿贝尔群（含挠）的丰富信息；但其对超平面配置群并非组合不变量，且弱于群本身，为格同构对与算术 Zariski 对的区分提供了新界限。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a connected finite-type CW-complex with fundamental group $G$. We show that the profinite completion $\widehat{G}$ determines the cohomology jump loci $\mathcal{V}^q_s(X,\mathbb{C})$, under two hypotheses treated separately: that the loci are finite unions of torsion-translated subtori, which holds for smooth quasi-projective varieties, and that $\widehat{G}$ determines the Betti numbers of the finite cyclic covers of $X$ in degrees $\le q$, which holds unconditionally for $q=1$ and in all degrees when $X$ is aspherical and $G$ is good in the sense of Serre. We show also that $\widehat G$ determines the graded abelian groups $\mathrm{gr}_r(G/W(G))$, torsion included, for every verbal subgroup $W(G)$; the cases $W(G)=1$ and $W(G)=G''$ give the lower central series quotients and the Chen groups. For hyperplane arrangements $\mathcal{A}$, it follows that no arithmetic Zariski pair is distinguished by any of these invariants, while two known lattice-isomorphic pairs show that the profinite completion of the arrangement group $G(\mathcal{A})$ is not combinatorially determined, and does not determine $G(\mathcal{A})$.

</details>


### [382] [The Quillen and sharbly Hopf algebra structures on Steinberg homology coincide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38735)
*Urshita Pal,Sam Payne*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文证明 $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{Z})$ 以有理 Steinberg 系数为系数的同调上，由 sharbly 分解构造与由 Quillen 谱序列及秩滤过映射构造得到的两个双分次交换 Hopf 代数结构一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期出现两种独立构造：Ash–Miller–Patzt 通过 sharbly 分解、Brown–Chan–Galatius–Payne 通过 Quillen 谱序列与秩滤过映射，分别在上同调上赋予双分次交换 Hopf 代数结构。二者来源不同，需澄清是否描述同一代数结构，以保证该结构的典范性与后续应用的可靠性。

Method: 先分别回顾两种构造的机制，再把两种结构统一归约为 Steinberg 模上的 join 运算与抛物限制运算所诱导的运算，证明由这两类运算生成的结构完全一致。

Result: 证明两种 Hopf 代数结构事实上相等，并识别出它们均由 Steinberg 模上的 join 与抛物限制运算诱导。

Conclusion: $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{Z})$ 有理 Steinberg 系数同调上的双分次交换 Hopf 代数结构是典范的，不依赖于 sharbly 分解或 Quillen 谱序列的具体构造路径。

Abstract: Two recent constructions independently give bigraded commutative Hopf algebra structures on the homology of $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{Z})$ with rational Steinberg coefficients. One, by Ash--Miller--Patzt, uses the sharbly resolution. The other, by Brown--Chan--Galatius--Payne, uses the Quillen spectral sequence and rank-filtered maps on $BK(\mathbb{Z})$. We prove that the two structures coincide. Both are induced by join and parabolic restriction operations on Steinberg modules.

</details>


### [383] [Weak, stable, and ordinary Lusternik-Schnirelmann category of finite spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39615)
*David Mosquera-Lois,Kohei Tanaka*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文研究有限T₀空间的弱、稳定与普通Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴之间的关系，构造出使 cat_w < cat_s < cat 同时严格成立的空间，并系统实现多类范数三元组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cárdenas、Flores、Quintero与Villar-Liñán提出了"同时严格性"问题：是否存在有限空间使弱、稳定、普通范畴三个量严格递增。本文旨在给出一般构造并回答该问题，同时刻画可实现的三元组范围。

Method: 利用弱可缩但不可缩、且删去一点后变得可缩的空间P，通过添加m≥2个不可比较的极大点构造连通有限空间，使其范畴三元组为(1,2,m)；再通过重心重分不改变普通范畴为2这一性质，以及不交并下的可加性来推广构造。

Result: 所构造空间在任意次重心重分后普通范畴仍为2；取九点空间P可得m+9点例子，特别地给出12点同时严格例子。借助不交并可加性，实现所有满足1≤a<b≤2a且c≥b的三元组(a,b,c)，以及所有2≤a≤c的(a,a,c)。

Conclusion: 该构造回答了同时严格性问题，给出小规模显式例子并刻画了可实现三元组。文末提出关于连通实现及同时严格例子最小基数的问题。

Abstract: The weak, stable, and ordinary Lusternik--Schnirelmann categories of a finite $T_0$-space $X$ satisfy $\operatorname{cat}_w(X)\leq \operatorname{cat}_s(X)\leq \operatorname{cat}(X)$. We give a general construction answering the simultaneous-strictness question of Cárdenas, Flores, Quintero, and Villar-Liñán. If $P$ is weakly contractible but noncontractible and the deletion of one point makes $P$ contractible, then adjoining $m\geq 2$ incomparable maximal points produces a connected finite space with category triple $(1,2,m)$. Moreover, after any positive number of barycentric subdivisions its ordinary category is equal to $2$. Applying the construction to a nine-point space yields examples on $m+9$ points and, in particular, a twelve-point example for which both inequalities are strict. Using additivity under disjoint unions, we also realize every triple $(a,b,c)$ with $1\leq a<b\leq 2a$ and $c\geq b$, as well as every triple $(a,a,c)$ with $2\leq a\leq c$. We conclude with questions concerning connected realizations and the minimum cardinality of a finite space exhibiting simultaneous strictness.

</details>


### [384] [A Counterexample to Cohomological Rigidity of Toric Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39707)
*Tao Gong,Yingxin Li*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 构造了两个复四维toric流形，它们不伦同伦等价，但整系数上同调环同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数拓扑中一个经典问题：上同调环同构是否蕴含空间同伦等价。本文通过具体的toric流形反例来回答该问题。

Method: 利用toric流形的组合与面环（Stanley–Reisner环）描述技术，构造两个复四维toric流形，并分别计算其整系数上同调环及同伦不变量。

Result: 所得两个复四维toric流形具有同构的整系数上同调环，但并非同伦等价。

Conclusion: 上同调环并非同伦类型的完全不变量，即使在toric流形这一受限类别中亦然。

Abstract: We construct two toric manifolds of complex dimension four, which are not homotopy equivalent but have isomorphic integral cohomology rings.

</details>


### [385] [Double Steinberg coinvariants for special linear groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39991)
*Tatiana Abdelnaim,David Chan,Alexander Kupers,Robin J. Sroka,Matthew Scalamandre*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 研究 $SL_n(F)$ 作用于双 Steinberg 模 $St_n(F)\otimes St_n(F)$ 的余不变量，揭示其代数结构及其与 Grothendieck-Witt 群的联系，并借助 $E_\infty$-代数框架给出 $E_2$-同调的消没线与临界线刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 $SL_n(F)$ 在双 Steinberg 模上作用所诱导的余不变量，理解其代数结构，并将其置于稳定同伦与 $E_\infty$-代数的框架中，以刻画相应同调群的消没现象。

Method: 对域 $F$，考察 $SL_n(F)$ 在 $St_n(F)\otimes St_n(F)$ 上的余不变量；对 $n=2$ 识别为 Grothendieck-Witt 群，一般情形组装成分次非单位 $\mathbb{Z}[F^\times]$-代数，并用 Grothendieck-Witt 群的增广理想描述其有理（非导出）不可分解元；进一步基于 Galatius-Kupers-Randal-Williams 的工作，将 $SL_n(F)$ 组装为合适函子范畴中的 $E_\infty$-代数。

Result: $n=2$ 时余不变量为 $F$ 的 Grothendieck-Witt 群；对所有 $n$ 余不变量构成分次非单位 $\mathbb{Z}[F^\times]$-代数，其有理（非导出）不可分解元可由 Grothendieck-Witt 群增广理想表达。$SL_n(F)$ 组装出的 $E_\infty$-代数，其 $E_2$-同调有斜率 2 的消没线，且临界线上的项由双 Steinberg 余不变量给出。

Conclusion: 双 Steinberg 模的余不变量具有丰富的代数结构，与 Grothendieck-Witt 群紧密相关，并通过 $E_\infty$-代数与 $E_2$-同调的消没线建立了稳定同调层面的联系。

Abstract: For a field $F$ we study the coinvariants for the $SL_n(F)$-action on the double Steinberg module $St_n(F) \otimes St_n(F)$ and show they have a rich algebraic structure: for $n = 2$ it is the Grothendieck-Witt group of $F$, and for all $n$ they assemble to a graded nonunital $\mathbb{Z}[F^\times]$-algebra, whose rational (underived) indecomposables may be expressed in terms of the augmentation ideal of the Grothendieck-Witt group. We then explain, building on work of Galatius-Kupers-Randal-Williams, that the special linear groups $SL_n(F)$ assemble to an $E_\infty$-algebra in a suitable functor category, whose $E_2$-homology groups have a vanishing line of slope 2 and on the critical line are given by the double Steinberg coinvariants.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [386] [Notions of equivalence among constant Yang--Baxter Operators: To $\infty$-Equivalence and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38290)
*Paul P. Martin,Eric C. Rowell*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文提出度量Yang-Baxter算子解与电荷守恒解接近程度的精确框架，并证明在2维情形下，11个局部解族中有10个接近电荷守恒，未接近的那个是单点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期通过整体范畴方法对某类Yang-Baxter算子的分类工作，促使作者追问：在合理等价意义下，有多大比例的（局部族）解接近电荷守恒解。由于“合理”与“接近”本身含义模糊，需给出精确定义并量化。

Method: 作者为“接近”和“合理等价”分别提出若干精确的数学定义，在此基础上对2维Yang-Baxter算子的局部解族进行整体范畴分析，统计其中接近电荷守恒解的族数。

Result: 在2维情形下，11个局部解族中有10个在所选精确意义下接近电荷守恒，唯一不接近的族是单点（singleton）。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了“接近电荷守恒”这一模糊直觉的严格量化形式，表明2维中绝大多数局部Yang-Baxter解族可视为电荷守恒的邻近情形，唯一例外为退化的单点族。

Abstract: Recent advances in the classification of certain classes of Yang-Baxter operators through a holistic categorical approach has some of us wondering about ways to identify solutions. In particular the classification of charge conserving solutions in all dimensions inspires the question: \emph{what fraction of solutions are \textbf{close to} charge conserving solutions, under any \textbf{reasonable} notion of equivalence?} Of course \emph{reasonable} and \emph{close to} are open to interpretation. Here we suggest some precise notions of both, and prove that in dimension $2$, 10 out of 11 local families of solutions are close to charge conserving, and the one that misses is a singleton.

</details>


### [387] [Conformal blocks for modular vertex algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38376)
*Colton Griffin*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文将余不变量层与共形块理论从复顶点算子代数推广到任意代数闭域上的顶点代数（配备坐标变换概念），并去除了C1-余有限、CFT型等标准假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原理论仅适用于复顶点算子代数，且依赖C1-余有限、CFT型等较强的技术假设，限制了适用范围。

Method: 在任意代数闭域上引入坐标变换（change of coordinates）概念，并据此构造稳定n点亏格g曲线模空间上的余不变量层与共形块。

Result: 成功将理论推广至任意代数闭域上的顶点代数，并证明若干标准假设（如C1-余有限、CFT型）可被去除。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了顶点代数几何实现的普适性，弱化了构造条件，使理论覆盖更广泛的顶点代数对象。

Abstract: Given a vertex operator algebra $V$ over $\mathbb{C}$, it is known how to define so-called sheaves of coinvariants and conformal blocks on the moduli space $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$ of stable, $n$-pointed, genus $g$ curves. We extend this theory to vertex algebras equipped with a notion of change of coordinates over any algebraically closed field. As a by-product of our arguments, we also show that a number of standard assumptions in these constructions can be removed, such as being $C_1$-cofinite or CFT-type.

</details>


### [388] [Outer action of representation category of discrete quantum groups: a case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39254)
*Debashish Goswami,Suchetana Samadder*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文研究tracial von Neumann代数 𝒜 = C*(S₃) 上的离散量子群(DQG) 的幺正张量函子与双模作用，证明在特定外作用下 DQG 必与 C*(S₃) 或 Out(𝒜) 幺正张量等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一作者此前构造了从 Rep(Q_aut(𝒜)) 到 Bimod(𝒜) 的幺正张量函子 Φ。本文将 𝒜 取为 C*(S₃) 的底层 von Neumann 代数，希望刻画包含 C*(S₃) 作为量子子群、且其外作用在限制于 Rep(C*(S₃)) 时与 Φ 一致的 DQG 的分类，从而理解量子群与其双模范畴之间的关系。

Method: 以 𝒜 = C*(S₃) 的底层 von Neumann 代数为对象，利用已有的幺正张量函子 Φ: Rep(Q_aut(𝒜)) → Bimod(𝒜)；考虑包含 C*(S₃) 的 DQG 𝒬 及其在 Bimod(𝒜) 上的外作用 Φ̃（定义3.4），要求该作用限制在 Rep(C*(S₃)) 上与 Φ 一致；进一步考虑带外作用 Γ: Rep(𝒬) → Bimod(𝒜) 且 Out(𝒜) ⊆ Im(Γ) 的 DQG 𝒬，进行幺正张量等价分类。

Result: 证明了两个结论：(1) 若 DQG 𝒬 包含 C*(S₃) 作为量子子群且具有与 Φ 在 Rep(C*(S₃)) 上一致的外作用 Φ̃，则 𝒬 与 C*(S₃) 幺正张量等价；(2) 若 DQG 𝒬 具有外作用 Γ 且 Out(𝒜) ⊆ Im(Γ)，则 𝒬 要么与 Out(𝒜) 幺正张量等价，要么与 C*(S₃) 本身幺正张量等价。

Conclusion: 在 𝒜 = C*(S₃) 的情形下，DQG 的分类被严格限制：具有相应外作用的量子群只能是 C*(S₃) 或 Out(𝒜) 的幺正张量等价类，表明双模范畴结构与量子子群条件强烈约束了 DQG 的类型。

Abstract: Given any tracial von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{A}$, the first author has defined a unitary tensor functor $Φ:{\rm Rep}(\mathcal{Q}_{\rm aut}(\mathcal{A}))\to {\rm Bimod}(\mathcal{A})$ in his paper [Gos24]. In this paper, we consider $\mathcal{A}$ to be the underlying von Neumann algebra of $C^*(S_3)$ and show that given any DQG $\mathcal{Q}$, which contains $C^*(S_3)$ as a quantum subgroup and has an outer action $\tildeΦ$ (in the sense of Definition 3.4}) on ${\rm Bimod}(\mathcal{A})$, which agrees with $Φ$ when restricted on ${\rm Rep}(C^*(S_3))$, is monoidally equivalent to the DQG $C^*(S_3)$. Furthermore, given any DQG $\mathcal{Q}$ with an outer action $Γ:{\rm Rep}(\mathcal{Q})\to {\rm Bimod}(\mathcal{A})$, such that $ {\rm Out}(\mathcal{A})\subseteq {\rm Im}(Γ)$, is either monoidally equivalent to Out$(\mathcal{A})$ or monoidally equivalent to $C^*(S_3)$ itself.

</details>


### [389] [Characterization Results on Generalized Smash Biproduct Hopf Algebras over the Partial Dual Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39896)
*Kangqiao Li*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 在辫monoidal范畴中，对具有左对偶对象的双代数构造另一双积 $D\times B^\ast$，证明 $B\times D$ 是 $D\times B^\ast$ 上的左smash，并建立相应Yetter-Drinfeld模范畴之间的同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献（Bespalov-Drabant 1999；Bulacu-Caenepeel-Torrecillas 2013）给出了 $(B,D)$ 构成广义smash双积 $B\times D$ 的等价条件。本文动机是构造另一侧的双积 $D\times B^\ast$，从而揭示两类双积及其Yetter-Drinfeld模范畴之间的对偶关系。

Method: 设 $B,D$ 同时是辫monoidal范畴 $\mathcal{C}$ 中的代数与余代数，且 $B$ 存在左对偶对象 $B^\ast$。构造双积 $D\times B^\ast$，分三个层次展开：$(1)$ 代数和余代数层次；$(2)$ 双代数层次；$(3)$ Hopf代数层次。进而研究 $B\times D$ 相对 $D\times B^\ast$ 的左smash结构。

Result: 证明了 $B\times D$ 是 $D\times B^\ast$ 上的左smash；建立了范畴同构 ${}_{B\times D}\mathfrak{YD}(\mathcal{C})^{B\times D}\cong{}_{D\times B^\ast}\mathfrak{YD}(\mathcal{C})^{D\times B^\ast}$，即 $(B\times D)$-与 $(D\times B^\ast)$-（左右）Yetter-Drinfeld模范畴之间的具体同构。

Conclusion: 通过构造对偶双积 $D\times B^\ast$，在代数、双代数、Hopf代数三个层次统一刻画了 $B\times D$ 与 $D\times B^\ast$ 的关系，并给出其Yetter-Drinfeld模范畴的等价，推广了已有广义smash双积理论。

Abstract: Let $B$ and $D$ be both algebras and coalgebras in a braided monoidal category $\mathcal{C}$. In the literature, there are equivalent conditions for $(B,D)$ to form a generalized smash biproduct (or cross product) denoted by $B\times D$, which were mainly given by Bespalov and Drabant in 1999 as well as by Bulacu, Caenepeel and Torrecillas in 2013. This paper is devoted to constructing another biproduct $D\times B^\ast$ when $B$ has a left dual object $B^\ast$ in $\mathcal{C}$. As results, we show that $B\times D$ is left smash over $D\times B^\ast$, and establish a specific isomorphism ${}_{B\times D}\mathfrak{YD}(\mathcal{C})^{B\times D}\cong{}_{D\times B^\ast}\mathfrak{YD}(\mathcal{C})^{D\times B^\ast}$ between the categories of the (left-right) Yetter-Drinfeld modules in $\mathcal{C}$. The constructions and results are provided in three levels: (1) $B\times D$ is an algebra and a coalgebra; (2) $B\times D$ is a bialgebra; (3) $B\times D$ is a Hopf algebra.

</details>


### [390] [Exact sequences of representation categories of weak Hopf algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40298)
*Quinn T. Kolt*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究弱Hopf代数表示范畴的正合序列，给出使$\mathrm{Rep}(H)\to\mathrm{Rep}(B)\to\mathrm{Rep}(A)$成为Bruguières–Natale意义下张量范畴正合序列的可验证代数条件，并发展弱Hopf代数的核与余核理论及限制标量函子的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意域上建立弱Hopf代数表示范畴层面的正合序列理论，将Hopf代数情形下的张量范畴正合列推广到弱Hopf代数，并为构造与分类相关张量范畴提供代数工具。

Method: 给定$A\overset{k}{\to}B\overset{\pi}{\to}H$（$A,B$为弱Hopf代数，$H$为Hopf代数），对$k,\pi$施加依赖相对可分子代数$B_r\subseteq B$的弱乘性约束，推广限制标量函子；建立弱Hopf代数的核与余核理论；并构造群胚、拟三角弱Hopf代数的形式ribbon扩张及Hopf代数弱作用于弱Hopf代数的余循环交叉积等例子。

Result: 给出了使$\mathrm{Rep}(H)\to\mathrm{Rep}(B)\to\mathrm{Rep}(A)$在Bruguières–Natale意义下正合的可验证代数条件；证明弱Hopf代数同态$k:A\to B$的余核总存在且为Hopf代数，当$A$连通时余核映射为满射；并通过群胚、形式ribbon扩张与余循环交叉积等例子验证该框架。

Conclusion: 弱Hopf代数表示范畴的正合序列可由其代数结构上的可验证条件刻画；余核理论在弱Hopf代数中具有良好性质，该理论为相关张量范畴的构造提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study exact sequences of representation categories of weak Hopf algebras over an arbitrary field. Given a sequence $A\overset{k}{\to} B\oversetπ{\to} H$, where $A$ and $B$ are weak Hopf algebras and $H$ is a Hopf algebra, we develop verifiable algebraic conditions on $k$ and $π$ under which there is an exact sequence $\mathrm{Rep}(H)\to\mathrm{Rep}(B)\to\mathrm{Rep}(A)$ of tensor categories in the sense of Bruguières and Natale (2011). Along the way, we develop a generalization of the restriction of scalars functor for maps $π:B\to H$ between associative algebras satisfying a weakened multiplicativity constraint depending on a relatively separable subalgebra $B_r\subseteq B$, as well as a theory of kernels and cokernels for weak Hopf algebras. We, in particular, find that the cokernel of a weak Hopf algebra homomorphism $k:A\to B$ always exists, is a Hopf algebra, and the cokernel map is surjective when $A$ is connected. We conclude by studying examples of such exact sequences built from groupoids, formal ribbon extensions of quasitriangular weak Hopf algebras, and cocycled crossed products of a Hopf algebra acting weakly on a weak Hopf algebra.

</details>


### [391] [Shifted symplectic structures and Poisson vertex algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17840)
*Wenda Fang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文用度数为1的分次辛几何（Maurer–Cartan数据）刻画弧空间上的Poisson顶点代数（PVA）Hamiltonian结构，证明度-2 Hamiltonian满足经典主方程当且仅当它给出X_∞上的PVA层，并应用于ℙ¹全局标量PVA分类、经典𝒲代数及RDW系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弧空间上的PVA Hamiltonian结构与分次辛几何之间的关系此前缺乏系统刻画，且经典R-矩阵与PVA结构的形变对应关系尚待阐明。本文旨在建立这一对应，并将其应用于𝒲代数和可积系统。

Method: 设X为有限型光滑概形，Y=T*[1]X。研究Y_∞上度-2 Hamiltonian在局部泛函Lie代数中的类；通过经典主方程（Maurer–Cartan方程）刻画PVA层的存在性与唯一性对应，并将经典R-矩阵解释为对应PVA Hamiltonian结构的Maurer–Cartan形变。

Result: 证明度-2 Hamiltonian定义X_∞上PVA层当且仅当其类满足经典主方程，反之每个此类PVA括号唯一确定一个度-2局部泛函；经典R-矩阵恰为相应PVA结构的Maurer–Cartan形变。分类了ℙ¹上一类全局标量PVA结构，并将R-矩阵形式应用于有限与仿射经典𝒲代数。对subregular 𝔰𝔩₃情形构造了相容Poisson pencil与中心约化的RDW系统，其一般约化为亏格2超椭圆系统并具显式Mumford型Lax表示；仿射对应给出常数与一阶微分R-形变及对易Hamiltonian流。

Conclusion: 分次辛几何的Maurer–Cartan数据为弧空间PVA Hamiltonian结构提供了统一框架，R-矩阵形变与可积系统（如RDW及𝒲代数）的自然联系由此建立，并可推广至ℙ¹结构与亏格2超椭圆Lax表示等具体情形。

Abstract: We identify Poisson vertex algebra (PVA) Hamiltonian structures on arc spaces with Maurer--Cartan data arising from degree-$1$ graded symplectic geometry. Let $X$ be a smooth scheme of finite type and set $Y=T^*[1]X$. We prove that a degree-$2$ Hamiltonian on $Y_\infty$ defines a sheaf of PVAs on $X_\infty$ precisely when its class in the Lie algebra of local functionals satisfies the classical master equation; conversely, every such PVA bracket determines a unique degree-$2$ local functional. We further show that classical $R$-matrices are exactly Maurer--Cartan deformations of the corresponding PVA Hamiltonian structure. As applications, we classify a class of global scalar PVA structures on $\mathbb P^1$ and apply the $R$-matrix formalism to finite and affine classical $\mathcal W$-algebras. For the subregular $\mathfrak{sl}_3$ example, we construct a compatible Poisson pencil and the centrally reduced RDW system, whose generic reduction is a genus-two hyperelliptic system admitting an explicit Mumford-type Lax representation. Its affine counterpart yields constant and first-order differential $R$-deformations, together with commuting Hamiltonian flows.

</details>


### [392] [$K_{\mathfrak{q}}$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38702)
*Davide Gaiotto*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入 K_𝔮-代数（4d N=2 超对称量子场论中旋转等变 BPS 线缺陷融合代数的公理化猜想）及其 Seiberg-Witten 型重整化群流作用的概念，统一并丰富了簇代数、K-理论库仑支代数、skein 代数与量子群理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4d N=2 超对称量子场论中的 BPS 线缺陷融合结构散见于簇代数、K-理论库仑支代数、skein 代数和量子群等多个数学分支，但缺少统一公理化框架，尤其是缺乏对 Seiberg-Witten 型 RG 流如何作用于线缺陷的代数刻画。

Method: 以公理化与猜想式构造为主：定义 K_𝔮-代数作为旋转等变 BPS 线缺陷融合代数的抽象刻画；定义 K_𝔮-代数之间的 RG 流，作为 Seiberg-Witten 型 RG 流在线缺陷上作用的公理化表述。

Result: 给出了 K_𝔮-代数与 RG 流的定义框架，指出该框架将簇代数、K-理论库仑支代数、skein 代数与量子群纳入同一体系并加以丰富。

Conclusion: K_𝔮-代数及其 RG 流为线缺陷融合代数提供了统一的猜想性公理框架，具备整合与拓展多种已知代数结构的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of $K_{\mathfrak{q}}$-algebra, a conjectural axiomatization of the fusion algebra of rotation-equivariant BPS line defects in a 4d ${\cal N}=2$ Supersymmetric Quantum Field Theory. We also introduce the notion of RG flow of $K_{\mathfrak{q}}$-algebras, a conjectural axiomatization of the action of Seiberg-Witten type RG flows on line defects. These definitions enrich the theory of cluster algebras, $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch algebras, skein algebras and quantum groups.

</details>
